# 人文/经济地理学期刊最新论文速递（2026-11-20）

> 数据来源：Crossref / OpenAlex 元数据（检索日期 2026-11-20）。收录范围：各刊近 30 天在线发表的研究论文（每刊最多 15 篇，按发表日期倒序）；近 30 天不足 5 篇的期刊回溯至近 4 个月或最新一期。共 **163** 篇。中文译文由 AI 翻译，引用请以原文为准。部分 Elsevier 期刊（Applied Geography、Geoforum）的最新在线论文尚未在开放元数据中提供摘要，仅列出题目。

# 一、按期刊分类

## Regional Studies

*本期收录：5 篇｜卷期：Vol. 60(1)*

### [1] Emergence of relatedness: offshore oil and gas and offshore wind energy in Esbjerg

**关联性的涌现：埃斯比约的海上油气产业与海上风电产业**

- 作者：Mads Bruun Ingstrup, Max-Peter Menzel
- 发表：2026-11-16｜Vol. 60(1)｜DOI: [10.1080/00343404.2026.2750385](https://doi.org/10.1080/00343404.2026.2750385)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；能源转型与基础设施｜关键词：技术关联性、海上风电、演化经济地理、地理邻近

**Abstract:** This study examines how relatedness emerged between the offshore oil and gas and offshore wind industries in Esbjerg, Denmark. Drawing on a qualitative processual case study, we show that organisational search evolved from intra-industry search via exploratory cross-industry search to regular cross-industry search. These changes were accompanied by the evolution of interorganisational networks and the institutional environment. We further demonstrate that geographical proximity emerged as an outcome of this process rather than as its precondition. Our findings therefore reposition relatedness not only as a driver of regional development but also as an emergent outcome of organisational and institutional change.

**摘要：** 本研究考察了丹麦埃斯比约海上油气产业与海上风电产业之间关联性（relatedness）的涌现过程。基于质性的过程性案例研究，我们表明组织搜寻从产业内搜寻经由探索性跨产业搜寻演变为常规化跨产业搜寻。这些变化伴随着组织间网络和制度环境的演化。我们进一步证明，地理邻近是这一过程的结果，而非其前提条件。因此，我们的发现对关联性进行了重新定位：它不仅是区域发展的驱动力，也是组织与制度变迁的涌现结果。

### [2] Regional development trajectories and the political geography of the far-right

**区域发展轨迹与极右翼的政治地理**

- 作者：Alexander Schallmeiner
- 发表：2026-11-11｜Vol. 60(1)｜DOI: [10.1080/00343404.2026.2741179](https://doi.org/10.1080/00343404.2026.2741179)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；区域发展、创新与演化经济地理｜关键词：极右翼、区域发展轨迹、序列分析、被遗弃地区

**Abstract:** How are long-term regional development trajectories associated with far-right electoral growth in Europe? Applying sequence analysis to national-relative economic, demographic and structural change across EU15 NUTS-3 regions (1982–2017), this paper classifies regions into six development trajectories and links them to far-right vote shares in European Parliament elections (1999–2024). Far-right growth is weakest in the securely incorporated high-income core and stronger across several historically distinct non-core trajectories. Comparable increases emerge from different regional histories that differ in timing and electoral composition, and are not reducible to static measures of left-behindness.

**摘要：** 长期的区域发展轨迹与欧洲极右翼选举得票增长之间有何关联？本文对欧盟15国NUTS-3区域（1982–2017年）相对于本国的经济、人口和结构变化进行序列分析，将区域划分为六种发展轨迹，并将其与欧洲议会选举（1999–2024年）中的极右翼得票率相关联。极右翼增长在稳定融入的高收入核心区最弱，而在若干历史上各不相同的非核心轨迹中较强。相似的增长幅度源自不同的区域历史，这些历史在时间节点和选举构成上存在差异，且不能简化为“被遗弃程度”（left-behindness）的静态测度。

### [3] Soft spatial imaginaries as a regional dynamic capability

**作为区域动态能力的软性空间想象**

- 作者：Andrew G. McClelland, Duncan Shaw
- 发表：2026-11-11｜Vol. 60(1)｜DOI: [10.1080/00343404.2026.2741181](https://doi.org/10.1080/00343404.2026.2741181)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：软性空间想象、动态能力、地方领导力、区域未来塑造

**Abstract:** Soft spatial imaginaries offer a novel conceptualisation of a regional dynamic capability to adapt to and shape collective action on regional development challenges towards desirable futures. This capability encompasses six dimensions embedded within a time- and region-specific opportunity space: sensing change, seizing opportunities, transforming organisations, boundary organisations, place leadership and continuous activity. By combining these concepts for the first time, we advance the translation of dynamic capabilities into regional studies and progress the growing interest in regional future-making practices for turbulent times. Our conceptualisation provides a broadened scope to understand collective agency in converging multiple actors on an agreed spatial imaginary.

**摘要：** 软性空间想象（soft spatial imaginaries）为区域动态能力提供了一种新颖的概念化，即适应并塑造针对区域发展挑战的集体行动、使之迈向理想未来的能力。该能力涵盖嵌入于特定时间和区域机会空间中的六个维度：感知变化、把握机遇、组织转型、边界组织、地方领导力和持续行动。通过首次将这些概念结合起来，我们推进了动态能力向区域研究的转译，并推动了动荡时期日益受到关注的区域未来塑造实践研究。我们的概念化为理解多元行动者围绕共识性空间想象汇聚的集体能动性提供了更宽广的视野。

### [4] Legal geographies and strategic coupling: an analysis of the medicinal cannabis industry in Colombia

**法律地理学与战略耦合：哥伦比亚医用大麻产业分析**

- 作者：Diana Morales, Tiago Teixeira
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Vol. 60(1)｜DOI: [10.1080/00343404.2026.2726962](https://doi.org/10.1080/00343404.2026.2726962)
- 主题：全球化、地缘经济与产业网络；区域发展、创新与演化经济地理｜关键词：全球生产网络、法律地理学、战略耦合、医用大麻

**Abstract:** Global production networks (GPNs) research often treats law as a static institutional backdrop, yet it plays a fundamental role in GPNs’ developmental outcomes. In this paper we bring GPN 1.0 into conversation with legal geography to study law, actors, firms, and regional assets as mutually constitutive; highlighting how their interactions co-produce the economic spaces where strategic coupling and regional development are forged. We thus analyse the emergence and development of Colombia’s medicinal cannabis, legalised in 2016. Our analysis shows how the dynamics between law, strategic coupling, power imbalance and the state produced a geographically uneven industrial development and produced social exclusion.

**摘要：** 全球生产网络（global production networks, GPNs）研究常将法律视为静态的制度背景，但法律在GPN的发展成效中发挥着根本作用。本文将GPN 1.0与法律地理学进行对话，将法律、行动者、企业和区域资产视为相互建构的要素，突出其互动如何共同生产出战略耦合（strategic coupling）与区域发展得以形成的经济空间。据此，我们分析了2016年合法化的哥伦比亚医用大麻产业的兴起与发展。分析表明，法律、战略耦合、权力失衡与国家之间的动态关系造成了地理上不均衡的产业发展，并导致了社会排斥。

### [5] Foundational economy, geography and ‘left behind places’

**基础经济、地理与“被遗弃地区”**

- 作者：Andy Pike, Maryame Amarouche, Vincent Béal et al.
- 发表：2026-11-03｜Vol. 60(1)｜DOI: [10.1080/00343404.2026.2739242](https://doi.org/10.1080/00343404.2026.2739242)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：基础经济、被遗弃地区、尺度、地方

**Abstract:** Foundational economy (FE) is part of the proliferation of alternative approaches following the Global Financial Crisis in 2008. FE is an influential agenda in urban and regional studies, but its geographical dimensions are under-developed. This paper explains and demonstrates the value and challenges of a more geographically grounded and sensitive approach to FE. It uses spatial ontology, scale and place to make FE more geographical by mapping, understanding, and explaining the differentiated and uneven geographies and interrelations of the FE zones and their governance and institutions. The potentials and pitfalls of this FE for ‘left behind places’ are then examined.

**摘要：** 基础经济（foundational economy, FE）是2008年全球金融危机后大量涌现的替代性路径之一。FE是城市与区域研究中一项有影响力的议程，但其地理维度尚欠发展。本文阐释并论证了一种更具地理根基和地理敏感性的FE路径的价值与挑战。文章运用空间本体论、尺度和地方，通过描绘、理解和解释FE各领域及其治理与制度的差异化、不均衡地理及其相互关系，使FE更具地理性。随后，文章考察了这种FE对“被遗弃地区”（left behind places）的潜力与陷阱。

## Economic Geography

*本期收录：4 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先；Vol. 102(4)*

### [6] Constructing Hybrid Futures: Fossil-Green Interpath Governance in a Global South Resource Periphery

**建构混合未来：全球南方资源边缘地区的化石—绿色跨路径治理**

- 作者：Linus Kalvelage
- 发表：2026-11-09｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/00130095.2026.2747931](https://doi.org/10.1080/00130095.2026.2747931)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；能源转型与基础设施｜关键词：跨路径治理、绿色路径发展、绿色氢能、纳米比亚

**Abstract:** Amid accelerating environmental crises, economic geography has increasingly turned to green path development. Yet existing research underplays how actors strategically shape the coevolution of fossil and green paths, particularly in Global South resource peripheries where industrial trajectories emerge simultaneously. This article advances the interpath linkages framework by examining how actors govern interpath relations during the critical phase of resource mobilization. It makes three distinct conceptual contributions. First, it embeds interpath dynamics within a Global South perspective, conceptualizing how triple structural pressures (foreign leverage, developmental stress, and efficiency imperatives) reshape resource formation beyond Western-centric transition models. Second, to understand how industrial futures are negotiated, it introduces the framework of interpath governance, capturing how path advocates, systemic actors, and hybrid firm actors deploy antagonistic and moderating modes of agency across spatial scales to assemble, neutralize, or contest interpath linkages. Third, it unveils concrete strategies through which interpath dynamics unfold in investment, market, knowledge, and legitimacy arenas. Developed through qualitative research in Namibia, where major offshore oil discoveries coincide with ambitious green hydrogen initiatives, the findings demonstrate how interpath governance operates under acute context pressures to harmonize seemingly opposing industrial futures. Overall, the article advances an agency-centered understanding of fossil-green co-evolution, highlights how interpath governance shapes hybrid energy trajectories, and thus contributes to debates on green path creation and the structural constraints on sustainability transitions in a Global South resource periphery.

**摘要：** 在环境危机加速的背景下，经济地理学日益转向绿色路径发展研究。然而，现有研究低估了行动者如何战略性地塑造化石路径与绿色路径的共同演化，尤其是在产业轨迹同时涌现的全球南方资源边缘地区。本文通过考察行动者在资源动员这一关键阶段如何治理路径间关系，推进了跨路径联系（interpath linkages）框架。本文作出三方面的概念贡献。第一，将跨路径动态嵌入全球南方视角，概念化三重结构性压力（外部杠杆、发展压力与效率要求）如何在西方中心的转型模式之外重塑资源形成。第二，为理解产业未来如何被协商，引入跨路径治理（interpath governance）框架，刻画路径倡导者、系统性行动者与混合型企业行动者如何跨空间尺度运用对抗性与调和性的能动模式，以组装、中和或争夺跨路径联系。第三，揭示跨路径动态在投资、市场、知识与合法性领域中展开的具体策略。研究基于在纳米比亚开展的定性研究——当地重大海上石油发现与雄心勃勃的绿色氢能倡议同时出现——结果表明跨路径治理如何在严峻的情境压力下协调看似对立的产业未来。总体而言，本文推进了以能动性为中心的化石—绿色共同演化理解，凸显跨路径治理如何塑造混合能源轨迹，从而为绿色路径创造以及全球南方资源边缘地区可持续转型的结构性约束等讨论作出贡献。

### [7] Flood Protection and Adaptation Labor in Jakarta, Indonesia

**印度尼西亚雅加达的防洪与适应劳动**

- 作者：Sophie Webber, Wahyu Kusuma Astuti, Rhino Ariefiansyah et al.
- 发表：2026-07-04｜Vol. 102(4)｜DOI: [10.1080/00130095.2026.2718196](https://doi.org/10.1080/00130095.2026.2718196)
- 主题：劳动、生计与日常生活；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：适应劳动、防洪基础设施、气候变化、雅加达

**Abstract:** Jakarta is undergoing an infrastructural transformation in response to increasing flood risks, constructing new dams, flood canals, reservoirs and retention ponds, and ongoing normalization of the city’s rivers. However, one crucial element of responding to severe flood risks that has so far been overlooked in Jakarta and other vulnerable cities is the work of building and maintaining this flood protection infrastructure. In this article, we respond to this research gap by analyzing the work of the Blue Troops, a new provincial workforce who construct and maintain Jakarta’s flood protection infrastructure. Drawing from extensive fieldwork interviewing and observing the Blue Troops in Jakarta, we catalog their adaptation labor practices, showing the accumulated health and safety risks of their work. At the same time, we demonstrate that the workers find this to be reliable and meaningful collective work, essential for reproducing cities in a time of climate catastrophe. In short, we show that workers trade precarious labor practices and bodily risks against secure public-sector employment and a sense of contributing to their city. This article, therefore, contributes to a growing scholarly concern with the labor implications of climate change, suggesting that the organization, conditions, and value of this work will be crucial political and analytical terrain.

**摘要：** 为应对日益增加的洪水风险，雅加达正在经历基础设施转型，修建新的水坝、泄洪渠、水库和滞洪池，并持续推进城市河流的“常态化”整治。然而，应对严重洪水风险的一个关键要素——建设和维护这些防洪基础设施的工作——迄今在雅加达及其他脆弱城市中一直被忽视。本文通过分析“蓝色部队”（Blue Troops）——一支负责建设和维护雅加达防洪基础设施的新的省级劳动力队伍——的工作来回应这一研究空白。基于对雅加达蓝色部队的大量田野访谈与观察，我们梳理了他们的适应劳动实践，揭示其工作所累积的健康与安全风险。同时，我们表明工人们认为这是可靠且有意义的集体工作，对于在气候灾难时代再生产城市至关重要。简言之，我们展示了工人如何以不稳定的劳动实践和身体风险换取稳定的公共部门就业以及为城市作贡献的意义感。因此，本文为学界日益关注的气候变化对劳动的影响作出贡献，表明这类工作的组织、条件与价值将成为至关重要的政治与分析领域。

### [8] The Role of Regional Recombination Capacity in Shaping the Technological Space

**区域重组能力在塑造技术空间中的作用**

- 作者：Diego Ocampo-Corrales, Rosina Moreno
- 发表：2026-07-04｜Vol. 102(4)｜DOI: [10.1080/00130095.2026.2701018](https://doi.org/10.1080/00130095.2026.2701018)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理｜关键词：重组创新、关联性、技术空间、专利数据

**Abstract:** This article investigates the role of regions’ recombinatorial technological capacity in shaping the technological space. To do so, we identify novel combinations of technologies and track their evolution by tracing all subsequent inventions that incorporate the same combination. Building on the concepts of relatedness and geographic proximity, we focus on the relevance of the technological antecedents of a pair of technologies combined for the first time in determining their success. This is due through the estimation of the likelihood of a new technological combination eventually becoming embedded within the broader knowledge space. Using patent data from 1976 to 2022 in the case of the European regions, we find strong evidence that a higher degree of relatedness between the technological antecedents of the two combined technologies significantly increases the likelihood that the combination will be reused in future inventions. Additionally, we find that the success of a new combination also benefits from the presence of dissimilar knowledge—not directly involved in the combination’s antecedents but accessible within the surrounding technological environment. In these cases, the greater the relatedness between the new invention’s antecedents and the broader regional knowledge base, the more likely it is to generate a high number of follow-on inventions and contribute meaningfully to the formation of the technological space.

**摘要：** 本文研究区域的技术重组能力在塑造技术空间中的作用。为此，我们识别技术的新颖组合，并通过追踪所有包含同一组合的后续发明来追踪其演化。基于关联性（relatedness）与地理邻近性概念，我们重点关注首次组合的一对技术的技术前因对于决定其成功的重要性。这是通过估计新技术组合最终嵌入更广泛知识空间的可能性来实现的。利用1976—2022年欧洲各区域的专利数据，我们发现强有力的证据表明，两项被组合技术的技术前因之间关联度越高，该组合在未来发明中被重复使用的可能性就显著越大。此外，我们发现新组合的成功还得益于不相似知识的存在——这些知识并不直接参与该组合的前因，但可在周围技术环境中获取。在这些情况下，新发明的前因与更广泛的区域知识基础之间的关联度越高，其产生大量后续发明并对技术空间的形成作出实质贡献的可能性就越大。

### [9] Geopoliticizing the Firm: Embraer, Corporate Nationality, and the Geopolitical Constitution of Contracts, Capabilities, and Markets

**将企业地缘政治化：巴西航空工业公司、企业国籍与合同、能力和市场的地缘政治构成**

- 作者：Kean Fan Lim, Tiago Teixeira
- 发表：2026-07-04｜Vol. 102(4)｜DOI: [10.1080/00130095.2026.2732942](https://doi.org/10.1080/00130095.2026.2732942)
- 主题：全球化、地缘经济与产业网络；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：跨国公司、企业国籍、地缘经济、巴西航空工业公司

**Abstract:** Transnational corporations (TNCs) drive fast-changing global economic geographies amid fraught geopolitical relations. However, three influential theoretical accounts of the firm, the nexus of contracts, the resource-based view, and Global Production Networks 2.0, still underspecify how corporate nationality and home–host state geopolitical agendas constitute TNCs’ formation, operations, and strategic management. This omission creates a gap in explaining the connections between geopolitical conditions and TNC operations. This article addresses this gap by geopoliticizing the firm. To this end, it integrates key aspects of the three theoretical accounts into a novel conceptual framework that comprises three analytical mechanisms: geopolitical agenda alignment, territorially defined capability formation, and territorially differentiated product development. Empirically, the article primarily draws on twenty-two interviews with elites across different firms and institutions to examine how Embraer, an aerospace producer, evolved following its establishment in 1969 as an integral part of Brazilian state developmentalism. After privatization in 1994, Embraer simultaneously (dis)engaged with multiple state geopolitical agendas to straddle US–China geopolitical tensions and compete internationally. Such proactive geopolitical (dis)engagement augmented Embraer’s ability to secure contracts, create capabilities, and capture markets across multiple countries. Through this case study, the article demonstrates the importance of conceptualizing the entwinement of TNC activities with both corporate nationality and variegated geopolitical contexts.

**摘要：** 在紧张的地缘政治关系中，跨国公司（TNCs）推动着快速变化的全球经济地理。然而，关于企业的三种有影响力的理论——契约关系论（nexus of contracts）、资源基础观（resource-based view）以及全球生产网络2.0（GPN 2.0）——仍未充分阐明企业国籍以及母国—东道国的地缘政治议程如何构成跨国公司的形成、运营与战略管理。这一疏漏导致在解释地缘政治条件与跨国公司运营之间的联系时存在空白。本文通过将企业地缘政治化来弥补这一空白。为此，本文整合上述三种理论的关键方面，构建一个新的概念框架，包含三种分析机制：地缘政治议程契合、地域界定的能力形成，以及地域差异化的产品开发。在经验层面，本文主要基于对不同企业和机构精英的22次访谈，考察航空航天制造商巴西航空工业公司（Embraer）自1969年作为巴西国家发展主义的有机组成部分成立以来的演变。1994年私有化后，巴西航空工业公司同时与多个国家的地缘政治议程进行（脱）接合，以在中美地缘政治紧张中左右逢源并参与国际竞争。这种主动的地缘政治（脱）接合增强了巴西航空工业公司在多个国家获取合同、创造能力和占领市场的能力。通过该案例研究，本文表明将跨国公司活动与企业国籍及多样化地缘政治情境的交织加以概念化的重要性。

## Journal of Economic Geography

*本期收录：10 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [10] Navigating industrial diversification in megacities: interconnections between industrial dynamics and changing commuting patterns in Shanghai

**超大城市产业多样化的路径：上海产业动态与通勤模式变化的相互关联**

- 作者：Zhan Cao, Shijia Lin, Ben Derudder et al.
- 发表：2026-11-18｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag070](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag070)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；健康、移动性与可达性｜关键词：产业多样化、通勤模式、演化经济地理、上海

**Abstract:** Industrial diversification plays an essential role in the longer-term growth of urban economies. However, its effects on daily life are less explored. Using microdata on firms and mobility across 164 subdistricts of Shanghai, this study applies an evolutionary economic geography framework to assess how intra-urban industrial diversification influences commuting patterns. We find that diversification into more unrelated and complex industries increases commuting duration and distance, suggesting that while industrial diversification fosters economic growth, it may raise concerns about quality of life and social inequities. Possible moderating effects like employment density, transit access and housing supply are discussed.

**摘要：** 产业多样化对城市经济的长期增长具有重要作用，但其对日常生活的影响较少被探讨。本研究利用上海164个街道的企业与移动微观数据，运用演化经济地理学框架，评估城市内部产业多样化如何影响通勤模式。研究发现，向更不相关、更复杂的产业多样化会增加通勤时长和距离，这表明产业多样化在促进经济增长的同时，可能引发对生活质量和社会公平的担忧。文章还讨论了就业密度、公共交通可达性和住房供给等可能的调节效应。

### [11] Indigenous communities and the shaping of a lithium labour regime in Jujuy’s salt-flat ecologies

**胡胡伊盐沼生态中的原住民社区与锂矿劳动体制的形塑**

- 作者：Nuria Giniger, Solana Renosto
- 发表：2026-11-16｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag086](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag086)
- 主题：劳动、生计与日常生活；能源转型与基础设施｜关键词：劳动体制、锂矿开采、原住民社区、阿根廷

**Abstract:** This article examines how Indigenous communities shape the lithium-mining labour regime in the Atacama Puna of Jujuy, Argentina. Drawing on ethnographic research, it shows how global mining arrangements are territorially reconfigured through communal landownership, community negotiation, subcontracting, salt-flat ecologies and historically constituted forms of social reproduction. These articulations produce distinctive recruitment practices, labour segmentation, uneven occupational risks and gendered reproductive burdens. The article advances labour-regime analysis by demonstrating that communities actively constitute the labour regimes of the energy transition through their political organisation, property relations and livelihood histories within the specific ecological conditions of the salt flats.

**摘要：** 本文考察原住民社区如何形塑阿根廷胡胡伊省阿塔卡马高原（Atacama Puna）的锂矿劳动体制。基于民族志研究，文章表明全球采矿安排如何通过社区土地所有制、社区谈判、分包、盐沼生态以及历史形成的社会再生产形式，在领土上被重新配置。这些衔接产生了独特的招聘实践、劳动力分割、不均衡的职业风险以及性别化的再生产负担。本文通过证明社区借助其政治组织、产权关系和生计历史，在盐沼特定生态条件下积极构建能源转型的劳动体制，推进了劳动体制分析。

### [12] Being(s) there(s)—geographies of tacit knowledge through avatars, telepresence robots, and holograms

**身在彼处——通过虚拟化身、远程呈现机器人和全息影像的默会知识地理**

- 作者：Johanna Hautala, Marianne Mäntyoja
- 发表：2026-11-14｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag083](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag083)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理｜关键词：默会知识、远程呈现、虚拟化身、知识地理

**Abstract:** We aim to understand emerging geographies of tacit knowledge through advanced remote-work technologies: telepresence robots, holograms and virtual avatars. These technologies enable ‘being there’ in more than one location (and space) at once and using communication that resembles a face-to-face experience. We examine, if tacit knowledge can be shared through these technologies. Based on interviews and autoethnography, we find that each technology confers the ability to exert specific spatial agency elsewhere. From the intellectualist perspective, all studied technologies allow the sharing of some tacit knowledge, whereas virtual avatars enable the sharing of some tacit knowledge also from the phenomenological perspective.

**摘要：** 我们旨在理解通过先进远程工作技术——远程呈现机器人、全息影像和虚拟化身——而出现的默会知识新地理。这些技术使人能够同时“身处”多个地点（和空间），并使用类似面对面体验的沟通方式。我们考察默会知识能否通过这些技术进行共享。基于访谈和自我民族志，我们发现每种技术都赋予了在他处施展特定空间能动性的能力。从理智主义视角看，所研究的所有技术都允许共享部分默会知识；而从现象学视角看，虚拟化身同样能够实现部分默会知识的共享。

### [13] Urban densification to support climate adaptation: balancing costs and agglomeration benefits in the Netherlands

**以城市加密支持气候适应：荷兰的成本与集聚收益权衡**

- 作者：Yashvant R Premchand, Peter Mulder
- 发表：2026-11-04｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag077](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag077)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：城市加密、气候适应、集聚经济、荷兰

**Abstract:** Where should we build new housing under growing climate hazards? This study develops a framework that balances the economic benefits of density against the geographically varying costs of climate adaptation. We apply it to the Netherlands, where demand for new housing is high, but much of the land lies in floodplains or subsidence-prone areas. Agglomeration benefits are proxied by land values, while adaptation costs are derived from engineering estimates of flood protection and soil subsidence. Combining these data allows us to map spatial trade-offs and identify where development remains welfare-enhancing. Our findings show that dense cities continue to generate strong net welfare gains, even in places with high costs, while low-density settlements generate a welfare loss for new housing. We identify density thresholds above which housing development becomes feasible. Many medium-sized Dutch cities already exceed these thresholds, making densification more beneficial than peripheral expansion. Climate adaptation thus strengthens—rather than weakens—the case for urban densification.

**摘要：** 在日益加剧的气候灾害下，新住房应建在何处？本研究构建了一个框架，在密度带来的经济收益与地理上存在差异的气候适应成本之间进行权衡。我们将其应用于荷兰，该国新住房需求旺盛，但大部分土地位于洪泛区或易沉降地区。集聚收益以土地价值作为代理变量，适应成本则来自防洪和地面沉降的工程估算。结合这些数据，我们得以绘制空间权衡图，并识别开发仍能提升福利的区域。研究结果表明，即使在成本较高的地方，高密度城市仍能产生显著的净福利收益，而低密度聚落的新建住房则造成福利损失。我们识别出住房开发变得可行的密度阈值。许多荷兰中等规模城市已超过这些阈值，使得城市加密比外围扩张更为有利。因此，气候适应强化而非削弱了城市加密的理由。

### [14] Towards an Ambivalence framework: decision-making on skills and training in global reproduction networks

**迈向矛盾性框架：全球再生产网络中的技能与培训决策**

- 作者：Erika Faigen, Tiago Teixeira, Roseline Wanjiru
- 发表：2026-11-04｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag074](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag074)
- 主题：全球化、地缘经济与产业网络；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：全球再生产网络、矛盾性框架、技能培训、战略耦合

**Abstract:** The global production networks (GPNs) literature, with the instructive concept of strategic coupling, has attended to commercial production, often bracketing out social reproduction. This leaves a conceptual and analytical gap around uneven outcomes emerging in strategic coupling processes. The paper therefore turns to global reproduction networks (GrPNs) and engages with the concepts of power and ambivalence, and with convention theory to develop the ambivalence framework. The framework builds on the co-constitution of production and social reproduction in which imperatives, such as price and efficiency, well-being, nurturing, and altruism, co-exist. It turns the conceptual-analytical focus to decision-making on training, where these imperatives are ranked and generate outcomes for training and beyond. The explanatory potential of the framework is sketched with a vignette of organic rice farming in Viamão, Brazil.

**摘要：** 全球生产网络（global production networks, GPNs）文献借助战略耦合这一富有启发性的概念，关注商业生产，却常常将社会再生产置于括号之外。这在战略耦合过程中出现的不均衡结果方面留下了概念与分析上的空白。因此，本文转向全球再生产网络（global reproduction networks, GrPNs），结合权力与矛盾性（ambivalence）概念以及惯例理论（convention theory），构建矛盾性框架。该框架建立在生产与社会再生产相互构成的基础上，其中价格与效率、福祉、养育和利他主义等要求并存。它将概念分析的焦点转向培训决策，在这一过程中上述要求被排序，并对培训及其他方面产生结果。文章以巴西维亚芒（Viamão）有机稻作为例，简述了该框架的解释潜力。

### [15] The impact of geographical and technological proximity on knowledge spillovers from formal standards

**地理邻近与技术邻近对正式标准知识溢出的影响**

- 作者：Peiyu Zhan, Silvia Massini, Mabel Sánchez-Barrioluengo
- 发表：2026-11-03｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag076](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag076)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理｜关键词：邻近性、知识溢出、正式标准、信息通信技术产业

**Abstract:** Proximity is traditionally viewed as crucial for overcoming the search and transfer costs associated with imperfectly codified knowledge. But does proximity still matter when formal standard publication jointly enhances the codification, certainty, and accessibility of technical knowledge? Evidence from the U.S. Information and Communication Technology industry shows that technological proximity drives substantial spillover gains, largely irrespective of location. Conversely, geographical proximity confers a compensating advantage when technological proximity is low. We reveal a nuanced interplay of proximity in knowledge spillovers: while formal standardization effectively flattens the world for the technologically proximate, co-location remains vital for their less proximate counterparts.

**摘要：** 传统上，邻近性被视为克服不完全编码知识相关搜寻与转移成本的关键。但当正式标准的发布同时增强了技术知识的编码化、确定性和可获取性时，邻近性是否仍然重要？来自美国信息通信技术产业的证据表明，技术邻近性带来了可观的溢出收益，且在很大程度上与区位无关。相反，当技术邻近性较低时，地理邻近性提供了补偿性优势。我们揭示了知识溢出中邻近性的微妙相互作用：正式标准化对技术邻近者而言有效地“抹平”了世界，而对邻近性较低者而言，同地布局仍然至关重要。

### [16] Polycrisis and labour agency in global production networks: evidence from Bangladesh’s apparel industry

**全球生产网络中的多重危机与劳工能动性：来自孟加拉国服装业的证据**

- 作者：Aysha Siddika
- 发表：2026-11-03｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag079](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag079)
- 主题：全球化、地缘经济与产业网络；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：多重危机、劳工能动性、全球生产网络、孟加拉国服装业

**Abstract:** This article explains how temporally uneven polycrisis reshapes labour agency within apparel production networks. Developing a spatial–temporal analytical framework, it shows how interactions, amplification, and acceleration across crises reconfigure workers’ opportunity structures through legitimacy, profitability, and social reproduction pressures. This qualitative case study of Bangladesh’s apparel industry examines how the Rana Plaza accident, COVID-19, and the climate crisis interacted to produce cumulative effects that enabled openings and constrained mobilization, shifting labour agency across associational, coalitional, and micro-practices over time. The study advances labour regime analysis by demonstrating the temporal contingency and spatial situatedness of labour agency during polycrisis.

**摘要：** 本文解释了时间上不均衡的多重危机（polycrisis）如何重塑服装生产网络中的劳工能动性。文章构建了一个时空分析框架，表明危机之间的相互作用、放大和加速如何通过合法性、盈利能力和社会再生产压力，重构工人的机会结构。本研究以孟加拉国服装业为定性案例，考察拉纳广场（Rana Plaza）事故、COVID-19疫情和气候危机如何相互作用并产生累积效应，既开启了机会又限制了动员，使劳工能动性随时间在结社、联盟和微观实践之间转移。本研究通过揭示多重危机期间劳工能动性的时间偶然性和空间情境性，推进了劳动体制分析。

### [17] Peripheral participation, metropolitan ownership: core and periphery configurations in Japan’s Green Transformation technological paths

**边缘参与，都市所有：日本绿色转型技术路径中的核心与边缘格局**

- 作者：Sebastián Baeza-González, Natsuki Kamakura
- 发表：2026-11-02｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag075](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag075)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；能源转型与基础设施｜关键词：核心—边缘、技术路径、专利、日本绿色转型

**Abstract:** Research on innovation beyond metropolitan cores has paid less attention to whether peripheral participation is matched by formal ownership of patents on key technological paths. We distinguish spatial and network participation from the headquarters geography of patent applicants, and apply kernel density, core–periphery network, and main-path analyses to sixty-two Japanese Green Transformation technologies. Four technology participation configurations emerge. Yet most main-path patent–firm links involve firms headquartered in Japan’s three major metropolitan areas. The study highlights that participation in main technological paths differs from ownership, which marks the tensions between core and peripheral configurations of inventive activity.

**摘要：** 关于大都市核心以外地区创新的研究，较少关注边缘地区的参与是否与关键技术路径上的专利正式所有权相匹配。我们将空间参与和网络参与同专利申请人总部的地理分布区分开来，并对62项日本绿色转型（Green Transformation）技术进行核密度分析、核心—边缘网络分析和主路径分析。研究识别出四种技术参与格局。然而，大多数主路径专利—企业关联涉及总部位于日本三大都市圈的企业。研究强调，参与主要技术路径不同于拥有所有权，这体现了发明活动的核心与边缘格局之间的张力。

### [18] People, places, and firms: improving our understanding of wage and productivity disparities

**人、地方与企业：增进对工资与生产率差异的理解**

- 作者：Yadira Gómez-Hernández, Henry G Overman
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag078](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag078)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理｜关键词：空间差异、工资差异、劳动者分选、集聚经济

**Abstract:** In many countries spatial disparities in wages and productivity are large and persistent. This raises questions about the causes and consequences of such disparities. Two inter-related issues—the sorting of high skilled workers and the productivity benefits of urban density—are central to the way in which urban economists have approached these questions. In this commentary, we argue that despite the emerging consensus that sorting matters for wage disparities, the literature has made less progress on other dimensions of these disparities and a number of methodological issues remain unresolved.

**摘要：** 在许多国家，工资和生产率的空间差异巨大且持续存在，这引发了关于此类差异成因与后果的问题。两个相互关联的问题——高技能劳动者的分选以及城市密度带来的生产率收益——是城市经济学家探讨这些问题的核心。在这篇评论中，我们认为，尽管已逐渐形成分选对工资差异具有重要作用的共识，但现有文献在这些差异的其他维度上进展较少，且若干方法论问题仍未解决。

### [19] Building without income mixing: public housing quotas in France

**有建设而无收入混合：法国的公共住房配额**

- 作者：Guillaume Chapelle, Laurent Gobillon, Benjamin Vignolles
- 发表：2026-10-21｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1093/jeg/lbag072](https://doi.org/10.1093/jeg/lbag072)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：公共住房、社会混合、居住隔离、SRU法

**Abstract:** We study the effects of the SRU law introduced in France in December 2000 to support scattered development of public housing in cities and favor social mixity. This law imposes 20 per cent of public dwellings to all medium and large municipalities of large-enough cities, with fees for those not abiding by the law. Using exhaustive fiscal data, we evaluate the effects of the law over the 1996–2008 period using a difference-in-differences approach at the municipality and neighborhood levels. We find that the law stimulated public housing construction in treated municipalities, but only slightly increased the presence of low-income households. Indeed, new public dwellings enter categories to which medium-income are eligible and most additional occupants are not poor. Within municipalities, the policy decreased public housing segregation but it barely decreased low-income segregation. This comes from local authorities increasing over time the presence of public dwellings in neighborhoods away from existing public housing but in places concentrating low-income households.

**摘要：** 我们研究法国于2000年12月出台的SRU法的效应，该法旨在支持公共住房在城市中的分散开发并促进社会混合。该法要求足够大城市中所有中等及以上规模的市镇达到20%的公共住房比例，未达标者需缴纳费用。利用详尽的税务数据，我们采用双重差分方法，在市镇和街区层面评估了该法在1996—2008年间的效应。研究发现，该法刺激了受影响市镇的公共住房建设，但仅略微增加了低收入家庭的比例。实际上，新建公共住房多属于中等收入家庭有资格申请的类别，且大多数新增住户并非贫困人口。在市镇内部，该政策降低了公共住房的隔离程度，但几乎没有降低低收入人群的隔离程度。这是因为地方政府随着时间推移，在远离既有公共住房、但低收入家庭集中的街区增加了公共住房。

## Urban Geography

*本期收录：8 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [20] Selling the city. Real estate narratives and the affective economies of luxury urbanism in Austin

**推销城市：奥斯汀的房地产叙事与豪华城市主义的情感经济**

- 作者：Sarah Klosterkamp
- 发表：2026-11-17｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/02723638.2026.2749467](https://doi.org/10.1080/02723638.2026.2749467)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：豪华房地产、情感经济、金融化、房地产营销

**Abstract:** Luxury real estate markets do not simply sell buildings; they sell imaginaries of urban life. This paper explores how cities are narrated, staged, and emotionally mobilized through real estate marketing practices. It takes readers on a VIP realtor bus tour through downtown Austin, where five high-end residential developments are presented by brokers to their peers. Drawing on ethnographic participation, the article examines how the city is actively staged as an object of desire and investment. Each stop foregrounds a different dimension of contemporary luxury housing – from speculative views and carefully staged interiors to amenities and the targeting of elite buyers. Framed as a note from the field, the paper shows how real estate professionals collectively perform and circulate narratives of urban value, translating processes of financialized urban growth into situated encounters. Engaging the idea of “selling the city,” popularized in media portrayals of luxury real estate, the article highlights how rarely urban research accesses this perspective from within. It argues that property tours offer a unique vantage point into how cities are perceived, evaluated, and segmented at the top end of the housing market.

**摘要：** 豪华房地产市场销售的不仅是建筑，更是关于城市生活的想象。本文探讨城市如何通过房地产营销实践被叙述、展演并在情感上被动员。文章带领读者参加一次穿越奥斯汀市中心的VIP房产经纪人巴士之旅，途中经纪人向同行展示五个高端住宅开发项目。借助民族志参与，本文考察城市如何被积极地展演为欲望与投资的对象。每一站凸显当代豪华住房的不同维度——从投机性景观、精心布置的室内空间到配套设施以及对精英买家的定位。本文以田野笔记的形式呈现，展示房地产专业人士如何集体展演并传播城市价值叙事，将金融化的城市增长过程转化为情境化的相遇。本文借用因豪华房地产媒体呈现而流行的“推销城市”（selling the city）这一说法，强调城市研究很少从内部获得这一视角。文章认为，看房之旅为理解城市在住房市场顶端如何被感知、评估与细分提供了独特的观察点。

### [21] The afterlives of the frontier: intersections of geopolitics and extended urbanization in Port Hedland

**边疆的余生：黑德兰港的地缘政治与延伸城市化的交汇**

- 作者：Ari Jerrems
- 发表：2026-11-10｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/02723638.2026.2758163](https://doi.org/10.1080/02723638.2026.2758163)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：延伸城市化、移民拘留、榨取主义、地缘政治

**Abstract:** This article theorizes how distinct world-making projects intersect and come into tension with each other through extended urbanization. It draws on archival and field research on the remnants of the Port Hedland immigration detention center in the Pilbara region of Western Australia. Port Hedland is geopolitically significant as the site of one of the first detention centers opened when mandatory immigration detention was introduced for irregular boat arrivals in the 1990s. At the same time, it was, and still is, part of vast transnational operational landscapes as the largest bulk port in the world. The town is, and historically has been, deeply implicated in processes of extended urbanization through the infrastructures of extractivism that connect iron-ore mines in the Pilbara to the port and the fly-in-fly out workers that circulate through it. Drawing on this history, the article situates the remnants of the detention center in relation to diverse territory-making projects, beginning with British colonization, passing through its construction as a miner’s barracks, and ending with its current use as workers’ accommodation. This history serves to underline how geopolitical borders and the operational landscapes of extractivism are the product of intertwined rather than easily separable spatial logics.

**摘要：** 本文对不同的世界营造工程如何通过延伸城市化（extended urbanization）相互交汇并产生张力进行理论化。文章基于对西澳大利亚皮尔巴拉地区黑德兰港（Port Hedland）移民拘留中心遗存的档案与田野研究。黑德兰港具有重要的地缘政治意义：20世纪90年代对非正规乘船抵达者实施强制移民拘留时，这里是最早开设的拘留中心之一所在地。与此同时，作为世界上最大的散货港口，它过去和现在都是庞大的跨国运营景观的一部分。通过连接皮尔巴拉铁矿与港口的榨取主义基础设施以及往返其间的飞进飞出（fly-in-fly-out）工人，该镇在历史上和当下都深度卷入延伸城市化进程。基于这段历史，本文将拘留中心遗存置于多样的领土营造工程中加以考察：始于英国殖民，经由其作为矿工营房的建造，直至其当前作为工人宿舍的用途。这段历史凸显了地缘政治边界与榨取主义的运营景观是相互交织而非易于分离的空间逻辑的产物。

### [22] Infrastructural entrepreneurialism as statecraft: transforming urban governance in infrastructure-led redevelopment

**作为治国术的基础设施企业主义：基础设施主导再开发中的城市治理转型**

- 作者：Yimeng Yang
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/02723638.2026.2754781](https://doi.org/10.1080/02723638.2026.2754781)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；能源转型与基础设施｜关键词：基础设施企业主义、城市治理、国家资本主义、海绵城市

**Abstract:** Amid the global turn toward infrastructure-led development and the resurgence of state capitalism, how is urban governance being restructured beyond neoliberal urban entrepreneurialism? This article argues that infrastructural entrepreneurialism is emerging as a new mode of urban governance, in which the crafting of urban infrastructure becomes a key vehicle for embedding state transformative strategies into urban entrepreneurial trajectories and socioeconomic development. Using a conjunctural case study of the Wuhan Sponge City project, the article argues that infrastructure plays a constitutive role in this post-neoliberal urban transition across discursive, financial, and material processes: (1) reframing the urban future through infrastructure as a discursive technology of party-statecraft; (2) reorganizing capital through infrastructure as a lever for equity-based reconfiguration of state power; and (3) making assets through infrastructure as a material practice of state rent formation. However, infrastructural entrepreneurialism does not necessarily fulfill state intentions. The article develops a 2 × 2 framework to conceptualize the inherent contradictory tendencies of infrastructural entrepreneurialism, rooted in the scalar politics of the state and the tension between state logics and capitalist logics. More broadly, the framework of infrastructural entrepreneurialism also seeks to offer a mid-level, comparative problem space for examining variegated urban governance under global infrastructure-led urbanization.

**摘要：** 在全球转向基础设施主导发展与国家资本主义复兴的背景下，城市治理如何在新自由主义城市企业主义之外被重构？本文认为，基础设施企业主义（infrastructural entrepreneurialism）正在成为一种新的城市治理模式，在其中，城市基础设施的营造成为将国家变革战略嵌入城市企业化轨迹与社会经济发展的关键载体。通过对武汉海绵城市项目的情势性（conjunctural）案例研究，本文论证基础设施在这一后新自由主义城市转型中，于话语、金融与物质过程中发挥着构成性作用：（1）通过基础设施重构城市未来，使其成为党国治理术的话语技术；（2）通过基础设施重组资本，使其成为以股权为基础重构国家权力的杠杆；（3）通过基础设施制造资产，使其成为国家租金形成的物质实践。然而，基础设施企业主义并不必然实现国家意图。本文构建了一个2×2框架，用以概念化基础设施企业主义内在的矛盾倾向，这些倾向根植于国家的尺度政治以及国家逻辑与资本逻辑之间的张力。更广泛地，基础设施企业主义框架还试图为考察全球基础设施主导城市化下多样化的城市治理提供一个中观层次的比较问题空间。

### [23] Car-free city politics and the urban commons

**无车城市政治与城市公地**

- 作者：Daniel Tjarks, Iolanda Bianchi
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/02723638.2026.2751947](https://doi.org/10.1080/02723638.2026.2751947)

**Abstract:** Automobility has come to constitute one of the most contentious issues in cities today. While research has provided valuable insights into the effects and conditions of motorized private transportation, it is precisely the local politics of the car’s future role and potential demise in cities – what we call car-free city politics – that has been conceptually and empirically neglected. In this Urban Pulse, we advance a novel conceptualization of struggles for car-free cities. Building on the burgeoning literature on the urban commons, we propose two commons-oriented perspectives to make sense of the stakes at play as well as the ideas and narratives employed in highly politicized debates around the car and the city: a liberal and a radical view. The radical view, in particular – recognizing the centrality of the car in capitalist urban development – allows us to grasp the deeply contentious and potentially transformative nature of car-free cities beyond the technicalities of urban mobility planning. We illustrate the framework's insights with evidence from two radical urban movements: “Barcelona en Comú” (Barcelona in Common) and “Berlin autofrei” (Berlin car-free).

**摘要：** 汽车化出行（automobility）已成为当今城市中最具争议的议题之一。尽管已有研究为机动化私人交通的影响与条件提供了宝贵见解，但恰恰是关于汽车在城市中未来角色及其可能消亡的地方政治——我们称之为无车城市政治——在概念和经验层面均被忽视。在这篇《城市脉动》（Urban Pulse）文章中，我们提出对无车城市斗争的一种新的概念化。基于蓬勃发展的城市公地文献，我们提出两种以公地为导向的视角——自由主义视角与激进视角——以理解围绕汽车与城市的高度政治化辩论中的利害关系及其所运用的观念与叙事。尤其是激进视角，认识到汽车在资本主义城市发展中的核心地位，使我们能够超越城市交通规划的技术细节，把握无车城市深刻的争议性与潜在的变革性。我们以两个激进城市运动——“共同巴塞罗那”（Barcelona en Comú）与“柏林无车”（Berlin autofrei）——的证据来阐明这一框架的洞见。

### [24] Walking the imperial axis: spatial governmentality and banal nationalism on Tokyo’s central pedestrian corridor

**行走帝国轴线：东京中央步行走廊上的空间治理术与平庸民族主义**

- 作者：Paanghi Park
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/02723638.2026.2722954](https://doi.org/10.1080/02723638.2026.2722954)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：空间治理术、平庸民族主义、步行基础设施、东京

**Abstract:** This article examines the approximately 3.5-kilometer pedestrian axis connecting Tokyo Station, the Imperial Palace, and Yasukuni Shrine as a material artifact of spatial governmentality. Drawing on Meiji – Shōwa-era urban planning documents, field observation, and GIS viewshed analysis, I trace how this corridor was sedimented through successive state interventions – the 1888 City Improvement Ordinance, the 1923 Imperial Capital Reconstruction Plan, and the integration of Mitsubishi capital with state spatial ambitions – into an axis-like infrastructural assemblage that channels pedestrian circulation toward spaces of imperial and national symbolism. The analysis then demonstrates how this historical infrastructure continues to operate as a mechanism of banal nationalism: without any single coordinating design, an Edo-period castle gate funnels crowds exiting the Nippon Budokan toward Kudanshita, where Yasukuni Shrine’s monumental torii enters the westward visual field, producing low-choice, routinely repeated encounters with commemorative landscape. By connecting Foucauldian governmentality with Billig’s banal nationalism through pedestrian infrastructure, the article shows how built environments structure visual encounters with nationalist symbolism beyond explicit political mobilization, with broader implications for ceremonial axes in East Asian capitals.

**摘要：** 本文将连接东京站、皇居与靖国神社、长约3.5公里的步行轴线视为空间治理术（spatial governmentality）的物质产物加以考察。借助明治至昭和时期的城市规划文件、实地观察与GIS视域分析，我追溯这条走廊如何经由一系列国家干预——1888年《东京市区改正条例》、1923年帝都复兴计划，以及三菱资本与国家空间抱负的结合——沉积为一个轴线式的基础设施组合，将步行流线引向帝国与国家象征空间。分析随后表明，这一历史基础设施如何持续作为平庸民族主义（banal nationalism）的机制运作：在没有任何单一协调设计的情况下，一座江户时代的城门将离开日本武道馆的人群导向九段下，靖国神社巨大的鸟居在此进入西向视野，从而产生低选择性、日常重复的与纪念性景观的相遇。通过借助步行基础设施将福柯式治理术与比利格（Billig）的平庸民族主义相连接，本文展示了建成环境如何在明确的政治动员之外构造与民族主义象征的视觉相遇，并对东亚首都的礼仪轴线具有更广泛的启示。

### [25] Mega Harbour sinking: speculative climate urbanism and mayoral power in Davao City, Philippines

- 作者：Ryan Tans
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/02723638.2026.2740547](https://doi.org/10.1080/02723638.2026.2740547)
