**Abstract:** In Southeast Asia’s coastal cities, powerful pressures favor land reclamation. Discursive practices of speculative and climate urbanism offer a set of ideological justifications, material infrastructures, and governance techniques to enclose common coastal areas and to build enclaves of apparent climate security in their place. Yet, the complexities of speculative government hint at the possibility of unexpected outcomes. In this paper, I interpret the case of the Mega Harbour project in Davao City, Philippines, which proposed to relocate 20,000 coastal residents to facilitate the reclamation and development of four islands in the Davao Gulf. However, the project was terminated due to concerns about “commercial viability, legal and social implications, and the project’s possible effects to the environment”. Drawing on interviews, news reports, and primary documents, I argue that Mayor Sara Duterte-Carpio benefited from a singularly powerful coalition based on decades of family rule in Davao and a filial relationship to the President of the Philippines, which endowed her with broad, unilateral discretion over reclamation policy. The metaphorical sinking of Mega Harbour demonstrates that urban officials mediate encounters between global discourses and local residents according to their personal ambitions, institutional powers, and political resources.

**摘要：** 在东南亚沿海城市，强大的压力有利于填海造地。投机性城市主义与气候城市主义的话语实践提供了一系列意识形态辩护、物质基础设施与治理技术，用以圈占公共沿海区域，并在其上建造表面上具有气候安全的飞地。然而，投机性治理的复杂性暗示了意外结果的可能。本文解读菲律宾达沃市“巨型港口”（Mega Harbour）项目的案例，该项目计划迁移2万名沿海居民，以推动达沃湾四座岛屿的填海与开发。然而，该项目因“商业可行性、法律与社会影响以及项目可能对环境造成的影响”等顾虑而被终止。基于访谈、新闻报道与一手文件，我认为市长萨拉·杜特尔特-卡皮奥（Sara Duterte-Carpio）受益于一个异常强大的联盟，该联盟建立在杜特尔特家族数十年统治达沃以及她与菲律宾总统的父女关系之上，赋予她对填海政策广泛的单方面裁量权。“巨型港口”的隐喻性沉没表明，城市官员会依据其个人抱负、制度权力与政治资源来中介全球话语与地方居民之间的相遇。

### [26] Crisis-led urban governance and the contingent reshaping of state-public encounters: the digitalizing South African state

**危机主导的城市治理与国家—公众互动的偶然重塑：数字化中的南非国家**

- 作者：Sophie Oldfield, Saskia Greyling
- 发表：2026-10-28｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/02723638.2026.2727115](https://doi.org/10.1080/02723638.2026.2727115)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：危机治理、国家数字化、开普敦、数字修复

**Abstract:** Digitalization is harnessed for crisis-led urban governance, from the COVID-19 pandemic to crises of migration management and housing delivery. This paper engages with the ways in which state actors, networks, and bureaucratic practices shape the adoption and institutionalization of digital technologies in Cape Town, South Africa, where crises are episodic and everyday. This paper contributes to understanding the layered practices of the “digitalizing” state by exploring digital interventions and the actors that produce them in and beyond the state. These include the COVID-19 dashboard, a public health tool; an online asylum seeker permit renewal system by the Department of Home Affairs; and a long-term digitalization of the housing allocation system to produce an equitable city-wide housing database. A conceptualization of layers unpacks and makes visible the critical work of digital interventions in urban governance. In their layers and logics, these interventions embody ideals of modernity, efficiency, and neutrality, but expose the contingencies inherent in the digitalization of the state across a terrain of crisis. For the South African state, crisis is not an exception to governance, it is a mode of governance itself– one that thrives on digital fixes that are never quite fixed.

**摘要：** 数字化被用于危机主导的城市治理，从新冠疫情到移民管理与住房供给危机。本文探讨在危机既是阶段性又是日常性的南非开普敦，国家行动者、网络与官僚实践如何塑造数字技术的采用与制度化。通过考察数字干预措施以及在国家内外生产这些措施的行动者，本文有助于理解“数字化中”国家的层叠实践。这些干预包括：作为公共卫生工具的新冠疫情仪表板；内政部的寻求庇护者许可证在线续签系统；以及为建立公平的全市住房数据库而对住房分配系统进行的长期数字化。“层”的概念化揭示并使数字干预在城市治理中的关键作用变得可见。在其层次与逻辑中，这些干预体现了现代性、效率与中立的理想，却暴露出在危机地带中国家数字化所固有的偶然性。对南非国家而言，危机并非治理的例外，而是治理模式本身——一种依赖于永远无法真正修复的数字修复（digital fixes）的模式。

### [27] The oscillating affects of smart city living: ambivalence and desire in Singapore

**智慧城市生活的摇摆情感：新加坡的矛盾心理与欲望**

- 作者：Nurul Amillin Hussain
- 发表：2026-10-22｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/02723638.2026.2744202](https://doi.org/10.1080/02723638.2026.2744202)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：智慧城市、情感、城市实验、新加坡

**Abstract:** This article explores the affects that emerge when smart city residents engage with the on-the-ground materialisations of smart urbanism, specifically through the different sites of experimentation in the smart city. Based on ethnographic fieldwork and interviews with residents living in smart estates in Singapore, this article studies how these sites, as part of the larger Smart Nation Project, operate. What happens when smart city residents are both officially experimented upon, and unofficially undertaking experiments of their own? This article discusses how this dynamic in the experimentation process produces two kinds of affective circulations. The first circulation of affect, ambivalence, emerges in the oscillating positive and negative affects generated through exciting and frustrating pilot-testing and the installation of new technologies within these estates. The second circulation of affect, desire, becomes paradoxical as it emerges in the simultaneous centrality of green spaces in residents’ enjoyment of their estates, and their calls for increased amenities and transport links that further development brings. These affective circulations demonstrate entanglements beyond the technological that implicate broader socio-political and cultural processes that may both limit and expand participation in the smart city, demonstrating how affect can be the analytical lens used in understanding what “smart” feels like.

**摘要：** 本文探讨智慧城市居民在接触智慧城市主义的落地实践——特别是智慧城市中各类实验场所——时所产生的情感。基于对居住在新加坡智慧屋邨居民的民族志田野工作与访谈，本文研究这些作为更大规模“智慧国”计划组成部分的场所如何运作。当智慧城市居民既被官方作为实验对象、又非官方地开展自己的实验时，会发生什么？本文讨论实验过程中的这一动态如何产生两种情感流通。第一种情感流通是矛盾心理（ambivalence），它产生于屋邨内令人兴奋又令人沮丧的试点测试和新技术安装所引发的正负情感之间的摇摆。第二种情感流通是欲望，它呈现出悖论性：一方面绿色空间在居民享受屋邨生活中处于核心地位，另一方面居民又呼吁增加进一步开发所带来的设施与交通连接。这些情感流通展示了超越技术层面的缠绕，牵涉更广泛的社会政治与文化过程，这些过程可能既限制又扩展对智慧城市的参与，表明情感可以作为理解“智慧”感受如何的分析视角。

## Urban Studies

*本期收录：15 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [28] Blind spots of state spatial selectivity: The left-behind secondary cities in Chinese mega-regionalisation

**国家空间选择性的盲区：中国巨型区域化进程中被遗落的次级城市**

- 作者：Yizhao Du, Rodrigo V. Cardoso, Roberto Rocco
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261498005](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498005)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：国家空间选择性、巨型区域化、次级城市、历史制度主义

**Abstract:** Chinese intra-regional unevenness remains unresolved, despite mega-regionalisation being promoted as the main instrument of state spatial selectivity aimed at alleviating polarisation and peripheralisation in secondary cities. We argue that a largely overlooked intermediate stage of such processes is partly responsible for the uneven outcomes of mega-regionalisation in tackling these challenges. This stage bridges mega-regional visions initiated by top-level authorities and practical interventions at the local scale, organised in action plans that determine relations between core and secondary cities by state actors at the provincial level. We select three representative Chinese mega-regions and examine the yearly reports from 2006 to 2024 of their provincial governments (acting as mega-regional coordinators) to summarise the various mechanisms and periodic trends of these plans through the analytical lens of historical institutionalism. We find that the intermediate stage, focused on spatial reconfiguration, redistribution of urban functions, and improving regional accessibility, emphasises cores as the driving force, leaving blind spots that allow only a few secondary cities to integrate and benefit from mega-regionalisation, whereas many more are further polarised and peripheralised. This is evidenced in all cases despite their different trends in specific action plans. We highlight the impact of intermediate-stage action plans on secondary cities to extend spatial selectivity theory to multi-scalar state-led regionalisation processes and call for policy and governance attention to intermediate-stage coordination mechanisms that allow representation of secondary cities in mega-regional planning.

**摘要：** 尽管巨型区域化（mega-regionalisation）被推崇为国家空间选择性（state spatial selectivity）的主要工具，旨在缓解次级城市的极化与边缘化，中国区域内部的不均衡问题依然悬而未决。本文认为，这一进程中一个长期被忽视的中间阶段，在一定程度上导致了巨型区域化在应对上述挑战时结果不均。该阶段衔接了由顶层权力机构发起的巨型区域愿景与地方尺度的实际干预，并以行动计划的形式加以组织，由省级国家行动者决定核心城市与次级城市之间的关系。我们选取中国三个具有代表性的巨型区域，考察其省级政府（作为巨型区域协调者）2006—2024年的年度报告，借助历史制度主义（historical institutionalism）的分析视角，总结这些计划的多种机制与阶段性趋势。研究发现，这一中间阶段聚焦于空间重构、城市功能再分配与区域可达性提升，强调核心城市作为驱动力量，从而留下盲区：只有少数次级城市得以融入巨型区域化并从中获益，而更多次级城市则进一步被极化和边缘化。尽管各案例在具体行动计划上呈现不同趋势，这一结论在所有案例中均得到印证。本文强调中间阶段行动计划对次级城市的影响，以将空间选择性理论拓展至多尺度的国家主导区域化进程，并呼吁政策与治理层面关注能够使次级城市在巨型区域规划中获得代表的中间阶段协调机制。

### [29] Companions, not just services: What do experiences of time teach us about urban vegetation?

**伙伴，而不只是服务：时间体验能告诉我们关于城市植被的什么？**

- 作者：Soledad Martínez Rodríguez
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261503483](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261503483)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：城市植被、超越人类、时间性、民族志

**Abstract:** Urban nature literature often describes urban vegetation in terms of its ‘ecosystem services’ such as carbon sequestration, air filtering, noise reduction and soil permeability, among others. Studies also note that green spaces foster social cohesion by providing places for social encounters and activities. Nevertheless, the bodily and sensorial relationships that urban dwellers create with urban vegetation are overlooked within these accounts. From an anthropological view that understands places as human and other-than-human entanglements, urban dwellers’ relationships with vegetation are poorly captured by the more transactional concept of ‘ecosystem services’. In this commentary, I bring to light shared temporalities between urban walkers and vegetation to argue for a closer look at the intertwined complexities of human and more-than-human cohabitation. Based on ethnographic research in Osorno, a small city in southern Chile – often referred to as a ‘green city’ because of its abundant vegetation – I delve into the experiences and narratives that emerge from people’s everyday contact with urban vegetation through their everyday journeys on foot. They invite us to rethink what urban nature does to urban places and what it means for these walkers, which is more than just providing humans with some ‘services’. Here, I show how everyday encounters with urban vegetation invite people to experience time differently. People can attune to more-than-human temporalities through senses and affects, allowing them to recall past times and also be part of a cyclical time we – humans and other-than-humans – all share.

**摘要：** 城市自然研究文献常以“生态系统服务”（ecosystem services）来描述城市植被，如碳封存、空气过滤、降噪和土壤渗透性等。相关研究也指出，绿色空间通过提供社会交往与活动场所促进社会凝聚力。然而，城市居民与城市植被之间所建立的身体性与感官性关系在这些论述中被忽视了。从将地方理解为人类与非人类相互缠绕的人类学视角来看，更具交易性质的“生态系统服务”概念难以充分把握城市居民与植被的关系。在这篇评论中，我揭示城市步行者与植被之间共享的时间性，主张更细致地审视人类与超越人类（more-than-human）共居的交织复杂性。基于在智利南部小城奥索尔诺（Osorno）——因植被丰富常被称为“绿色城市”——开展的民族志研究，我深入探讨人们在日常步行出行中与城市植被的日常接触所产生的经验与叙事。这些经验与叙事促使我们重新思考城市自然对城市地方的作用及其对步行者的意义，这远不止于为人类提供某些“服务”。本文展示了与城市植被的日常相遇如何使人们以不同方式体验时间。人们可以通过感官与情感调谐于超越人类的时间性，从而回忆过往，并成为我们——人类与非人类——共同分享的循环时间的一部分。

### [30] Veganism in the city: Capitalist assimilation or multispecies resistance?

**城市中的素食主义：资本主义同化还是多物种抵抗？**

- 作者：Corey Lee Wrenn
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261499626](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261499626)
- 主题：劳动、生计与日常生活；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：素食主义、消费主义、多物种、符号学分析

**Abstract:** Cities are spaces in which consumerism, culture, and protest must often comingle. Often concentrated in urban spaces, veganism—which advocates alternative consumption, multispecies multiculturalism, and resistance to harmful foodways and unequal multispecies relationships—offers an interesting case study of urban consumerist dynamics. Using semiotic analysis of signs, posted menus, advertisements, and window displays collected between 2021 and 2022 in one university town in southeast England, it is considered how veganism might be able to persist, perhaps even thrive, in capital-serving cityscapes. Findings suggest that veganism is strained by the marketplace function of the city. Veganism is reduced to a form of consumption that aligns with capitalist interests, and anti-speciesist representations of veganism are lacking. Yet, this study also considers if the prevalence of vegan signage could be moderately subversive in normalizing veganism. These complexities are considered in the context of geographical and sociological research on cities as sites of both corporate co-optation of protest and social change.

**摘要：** 城市是消费主义、文化与抗议往往必须交融共存的空间。素食主义（veganism）常集中于城市空间，倡导另类消费、多物种多元文化主义，并抵制有害的饮食方式与不平等的多物种关系，因而为考察城市消费主义动态提供了一个有趣的案例。本文对2021—2022年间在英格兰东南部一座大学城收集的标识、张贴菜单、广告和橱窗陈列进行符号学分析，探讨素食主义如何能够在服务于资本的城市景观中存续乃至兴盛。研究发现，素食主义受到城市市场功能的制约：它被简化为一种契合资本主义利益的消费形式，而反物种主义（anti-speciesist）的素食主义表征则较为缺乏。然而，本研究也探讨了素食标识的普遍存在是否可能通过使素食主义常态化而具有一定的颠覆性。上述复杂性被置于将城市视为抗议遭企业收编与社会变革双重场所的地理学和社会学研究语境中加以讨论。

### [31] Time as a commons: Collective temporal practices and the making of urban care infrastructures

**作为公地的时间：集体时间实践与城市照护基础设施的营造**

- 作者：Maro Pantazidou
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261503494](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261503494)
- 主题：劳动、生计与日常生活；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：时间公地、照护基础设施、共同化、集体时间工作

**Abstract:** The contemporary care crisis has prompted a surge of scholarly attention to urban care commons and bottom-up care infrastructures, with particular emphasis on their socio-spatial dynamics. By contrast, the temporal dynamics through which such formations are sustained have received limited attention. While previous research has engaged urban and institutional temporalities in relation to processes of commoning, the commoning of time itself remains underexplored as a pathway towards urban transformation. This article turns to the urban co-production of time, focusing on the temporal patterns that sustain one of the largest self-organised collective kitchens in crisis-ridden central Athens. The kitchen mobilises dozens of residents in daily food provisioning and redistribution, preparing and sharing hundreds of warm meals 6 days a week. The scale and continuity of the initiative provide a unique empirical lens for understanding the organisational and meaning-making practices, at the temporal level, that make informal urban care infrastructures possible. Drawing on 10 months of ethnographic fieldwork, the article illuminates both the temporal resources (clock-time hours) and the temporal orientations (valuations of time beyond clock-time) that underpin alternative arrangements of social provisioning. The findings introduce the concept of collective time work to describe how otherwise temporally fragmented urban communities pool time and remake temporal norms to create cohesive community care. Conceptually, the article advances time as a relational, co-produced good, rather than an individually managed resource, to foreground the politics of time as a vital vector for transforming how care is organised and sustained in cities.

**摘要：** 当代照护危机促使学界大量关注城市照护公地（care commons）与自下而上的照护基础设施，且尤其强调其社会—空间动态。相比之下，维系这些组织形态的时间动态较少受到关注。尽管已有研究探讨了与共同化（commoning）过程相关的城市及制度时间性，但时间本身的共同化作为城市变革路径仍未得到充分探讨。本文转向时间的城市共同生产，聚焦于危机重重的雅典市中心规模最大的自组织集体厨房之一，考察维系其运转的时间模式。该厨房动员数十名居民参与日常食物供给与再分配，每周6天准备并分享数百份热餐。该倡议的规模与持续性为理解在时间层面使非正式城市照护基础设施成为可能的组织实践与意义建构实践提供了独特的经验视角。基于10个月的民族志田野工作，本文揭示了支撑另类社会供给安排的时间资源（钟表时间小时数）与时间取向（超越钟表时间的时间价值判断）。研究提出“集体时间工作”（collective time work）概念，用以描述原本在时间上碎片化的城市社区如何汇集时间并重塑时间规范，以营造有凝聚力的社区照护。在概念上，本文将时间视为一种关系性的、共同生产的公共物品，而非个体管理的资源，从而凸显时间政治作为改变城市照护组织与维系方式的关键向量。

### [32] Provisioning otherwise: Platform co-operatives and the reworking of platform urbanism

**另类供给：平台合作社与平台城市主义的重塑**

- 作者：Jun Zhang
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261505798](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261505798)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：平台城市主义、平台合作社、供给政治、数字基础设施

**Abstract:** Urban life is increasingly organised through platforms, but platform urbanism has largely been understood through corporate intermediation, extraction and regulation. This article asks what becomes visible when platformisation is approached instead through the politics of provision. Drawing on 31 semi-structured interviews across a translocal platform co-operative ecosystem, it examines how co-operatively governed digital infrastructures make shared provision workable across places. The article develops platformed mutual provisioning to describe a recursive process through which dispersed capacities are assembled around situated needs, extended through federation and kept collectively governable through maintenance, democratic learning and data stewardship. The findings show that platform co-operatives matter less as ownership alternatives in themselves than as fragile experiments in organising the coordinating capacities on which food systems, logistics and civic economies increasingly depend. Their significance lies in keeping those capacities revisable as they become infrastructural to everyday provision. The article argues that this opens a wider politics of co-operative infrastructural urbanism, in which digital urbanisation is judged not by growth or technological novelty, but by whether the power to coordinate provision remains answerable to those who depend upon it.

**摘要：** 城市生活日益通过平台加以组织，但平台城市主义（platform urbanism）在很大程度上被理解为企业中介、榨取与监管问题。本文追问：若从供给政治而非上述角度切入平台化，将会看到什么？基于对一个跨地方平台合作社生态系统的31次半结构式访谈，本文考察合作治理的数字基础设施如何使跨地方的共享供给得以运作。本文提出“平台化互助供给”（platformed mutual provisioning）概念，用以描述一个递归过程：分散的能力围绕具体情境需求被组装，通过联合（federation）得以扩展，并通过维护、民主学习和数据管理（data stewardship）保持可集体治理。研究发现，平台合作社的意义与其说在于其本身作为所有权替代方案，不如说在于其作为组织协调能力的脆弱实验，而食物系统、物流和公民经济正日益依赖这些协调能力。其重要性在于，当这些能力成为日常供给的基础设施时，仍保持其可修正性。本文认为，这开启了一种更广泛的合作型基础设施城市主义政治，在其中，数字城市化的评判标准不是增长或技术新颖性，而是协调供给的权力是否仍对依赖它的人们负责。

### [33] Healthcare real estate and urban infrastructure: between public good and financial asset

**医疗房地产与城市基础设施：在公共物品与金融资产之间**

- 作者：Jenny Zhenyi Huang, Sotiris Tsolacos
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261497185](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261497185)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；健康、移动性与可达性｜关键词：医疗房地产、金融化、社会基础设施、影响力投资

**Abstract:** In contemporary urbanism, healthcare real estate plays the dual role of essential social infrastructure and financial asset. On the one hand, healthcare real estate functions as a vital form of social infrastructure, shaping patterns of accessibility, belonging, and resilience in cities. On the other hand, it is increasingly governed as a financial asset through public-private partnerships (PPPs), real estate investment trusts (REITs), and institutional investment. This article conceptualises healthcare real estate as a form of financialised social infrastructure situated at the intersection of public service provision, financial investment, and urban governance. Drawing on evidence from the UK healthcare estate, the article examines how healthcare real estate has become increasingly embedded within circuits of capital accumulation and integrated into broader processes of financialised urbanism. The analysis demonstrates that financialisation has reshaped the financing and governance of healthcare real estate, expanding the role of institutional investors and private capital and reinforcing investment logics within healthcare provision. While these developments can mobilise capital for infrastructure investment and estate modernisation, they may also generate governance challenges relating to ownership, accountability, accessibility, and spatial equity. The article contributes to urban studies by advancing understanding of healthcare real estate as a form of financialised social infrastructure and examining its implications for health equity and sustainable urban development. It further explores the potential of impact investment as a mechanism for reconciling financial and social objectives, highlighting the importance of impact evaluation frameworks in aligning private capital with broader social and spatial outcomes.

**摘要：** 在当代城市主义中，医疗房地产扮演着基本社会基础设施与金融资产的双重角色。一方面，医疗房地产作为一种重要的社会基础设施，塑造着城市中的可达性、归属感与韧性格局；另一方面，它日益通过公私合作（PPPs）、房地产投资信托（REITs）和机构投资而被作为金融资产加以治理。本文将医疗房地产概念化为一种金融化的社会基础设施，位于公共服务供给、金融投资与城市治理的交汇处。基于英国医疗房产的证据，本文考察医疗房地产如何日益嵌入资本积累循环，并融入更广泛的金融化城市主义进程。分析表明，金融化重塑了医疗房地产的融资与治理，扩大了机构投资者和私人资本的作用，并强化了医疗供给中的投资逻辑。尽管这些发展能够为基础设施投资与房产现代化调动资本，但也可能在所有权、问责、可达性和空间公平等方面带来治理挑战。本文通过推进将医疗房地产理解为金融化社会基础设施的认识，并考察其对健康公平与可持续城市发展的影响，为城市研究作出贡献。本文还进一步探讨了影响力投资（impact investment）作为调和财务目标与社会目标机制的潜力，强调影响评估框架在使私人资本与更广泛的社会及空间成果相一致方面的重要性。

### [34] Remembering what is: Indigenising reflections of an urban landscape

**铭记当下之所是：对一处城市景观的原住民化反思**

- 作者：Nicolette Duncan
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261503451](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261503451)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：原住民、殖民主义、地方营造、墨尔本

**Abstract:** Melbourne city as it exists today is a colonial project with a history of destruction, exclusion and denial of local First Nations sovereignty on Wurundjeri Country. This fact is taken for granted in the urban environment today whereby acknowledgement of Wurundjeri presence in this location is presented as in the past, with little explanation or accountability offered as to the transition from Wurundjeri custodianship of Country to present modern urbanity. Country, as understood by Wurundjeri people, extends beyond the landscape to include social, temporal and spiritual dimensions. Recent efforts to reconcile Melbourne’s colonial history reflect these multiple dimensions. In this brief article, I reflect on place-making and historical narrative in urban Melbourne on Wurundjeri Country from a non-Indigenous perspective. To combat narrow colonial understandings of Melbourne’s history, and complement reconciliatory efforts, Country is discussed as a multidimensional conceptual framework which could be applied to encourage a greater sense of appreciation for Wurundjeri Country, the past, and an inclusive social future on common ground.

**摘要：** 今日的墨尔本城是一项殖民工程，在乌伦杰里（Wurundjeri）故土（Country）上有着破坏、排斥以及否认当地第一民族主权的历史。这一事实在当今城市环境中被视为理所当然：对乌伦杰里人在此地存在的承认被呈现为过去之事，而对于从乌伦杰里人守护故土到当下现代城市性的转变，几乎没有给出解释或承担责任。乌伦杰里人所理解的“故土”超越景观本身，涵盖社会、时间与精神维度。近来弥合墨尔本殖民历史的努力体现了这些多重维度。在这篇短文中，我从非原住民的视角反思墨尔本城市中乌伦杰里故土上的地方营造与历史叙事。为对抗对墨尔本历史的狭隘殖民式理解并补充和解努力，本文将“故土”作为一种多维概念框架加以讨论，该框架可用于促进人们更加珍视乌伦杰里故土、过往历史，以及在共同基础上实现包容性的社会未来。

### [35] Solidarity kitchens and community gardens: Commoning practices of care in urban peripheries

**团结厨房与社区花园：城市边缘地区的照护共同化实践**

- 作者：Renata Motta, Anne Cathrin Ziegler, Maria Eugênia Trombini
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261504189](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261504189)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：城市食物公地、女性主义政治生态学、照护基础设施、社会再生产

**Abstract:** This article examines urban food commons by tracing the emergence of food cultivation and preparation initiatives in urban peripheries, including occupied and contested territories. Nutritional studies often classify urban occupations and housing projects as food deserts or food swamps. Drawing on feminist political ecology, decolonial feminist perspectives on urban commons and care and Black Food Geographies, our research reframes these environments not as deficient but as care infrastructures where commoning practices among plants, people and place are enacted. The research focuses on community gardens and solidarity kitchens in the Brazilian metropolitan region of Belo Horizonte, based on fieldwork and focus groups with members from a state–society network of food activists that have shaped the alternative food system in Belo Horizonte in the last three decades. Beyond the binary of ‘social fixes’ for capitalist urbanisation and localised prefigurative spaces of post-capitalist difference, we treat community gardens and kitchens as spaces for (re)organising social reproduction, reproducing a socio-natural network and providing care that can unlock transformative potentialities. Furthermore, we claim that these urban food commons sparked renewed state commitment, transforming not only the political ecology of food and the urban landscape in the peripheries of Belo Horizonte but also state policies, aiming at promoting state-commons. Tensions remain, however, considering the relationships between autonomy in commoning practices and the state. Our article contributes to discussions of urban food commons by examining their relational dynamics with the state.

**摘要：** 本文通过追溯城市边缘地区（包括被占领和存在争议的领地）食物种植与烹制倡议的兴起，考察城市食物公地。营养学研究常将城市占领地和住房项目归类为食物荒漠或食物沼泽。借鉴女性主义政治生态学、关于城市公地与照护的去殖民女性主义视角以及黑人食物地理学（Black Food Geographies），本研究将这些环境重新界定为照护基础设施，而非匮乏之地，在其中植物、人与地方之间的共同化实践得以展开。研究聚焦巴西贝洛奥里藏特（Belo Horizonte）都市区的社区花园与团结厨房，基于田野工作以及对一个国家—社会食物活动人士网络成员的焦点小组访谈，该网络在过去三十年间塑造了贝洛奥里藏特的另类食物系统。超越将其视为资本主义城市化的“社会修复”与后资本主义差异的地方化预示性空间这一二元对立，我们将社区花园和厨房视为（重新）组织社会再生产、再生产社会—自然网络并提供照护的空间，能够释放变革潜能。此外，我们认为这些城市食物公地激发了国家新的承诺，不仅改变了贝洛奥里藏特边缘地区的食物政治生态与城市景观，也改变了国家政策，旨在推动“国家—公地”（state-commons）。然而，考虑到共同化实践的自主性与国家之间的关系，张力依然存在。本文通过考察城市食物公地与国家之间的关系动态，为城市食物公地的讨论作出贡献。

### [36] The rubble and beyond: Expanding urbicide through affective geographies and the settler-colonial longue durée

**瓦砾及其之外：通过情感地理与定居者殖民长时段拓展城市灭绝概念**

- 作者：Ohoud Kamal, Yamen N. Al-Betawi
- 发表：2026-11-17｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261503755](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261503755)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：城市灭绝、情感地理、定居者殖民主义、加沙

**Abstract:** Urbicide scholarship has established, in varying ways, that ‘killing a city’ reaches beyond the material. Yet the concepts used to name this ‘reach’ do not describe the same object or make the same analytical claim. The question is therefore not only whether urbicide reaches beyond the material, but what a given conceptualisation allows us to understand about what destruction reaches. This article expands urbicide scholarship by theorising affect not as an additional dimension or effect of destruction, but as a capacity enabled by material and human–nonhuman relations through which violence can be reached. The argument is developed through a situated reading of contemporary urbicidal violence in Gaza (2023–ongoing), where recurrent military operations have compounded destruction while foreclosing reconstruction between them, within a longer settler-colonial history of displacement and enclosure. Drawing on Anderson’s account of morale and affective geographies literature, the article shows how destruction reaches the material and relational conditions through which affective capacities are constituted and exercised. It further proposes storytelling and counter-mapping as situated forms of evidence for tracing the reach of this destruction: memories, attachments, spatial knowledge and relations that conventional damage assessment cannot capture. Locating these processes within the settler-colonial longue durée expands the concept of urbicide showing how its reach is shaped by historically specific conditions of enclosure, cumulative destruction and the absence of a stable aftermath.

**摘要：** 城市灭绝（urbicide）研究已以不同方式确立，“杀死一座城市”的影响远超物质层面。然而，用于命名这种“触及”的概念并未描述同一对象，也未提出相同的分析主张。因此，问题不仅在于城市灭绝是否超越物质，还在于特定的概念化能让我们理解破坏究竟触及了什么。本文将情感（affect）理论化为一种由物质关系及人与非人关系所赋予的能力——暴力正是通过这种能力而得以触及——而非将其视为破坏的附加维度或效应，以此拓展城市灭绝研究。论证通过对加沙当代城市灭绝暴力（2023年至今）的情境化解读展开：在更长时段的定居者殖民驱逐与圈禁历史中，反复的军事行动不断叠加破坏，并在行动间隙阻断重建。借鉴安德森（Anderson）关于士气的论述与情感地理学文献，本文展示破坏如何触及构成并施展情感能力的物质与关系条件。本文进一步提出将讲故事与反制图（counter-mapping）作为追踪破坏影响范围的情境化证据形式：记忆、依恋、空间知识与关系，这些都是传统损害评估所无法捕捉的。将这些过程置于定居者殖民的长时段（longue durée）之中，拓展了城市灭绝概念，揭示其影响范围如何受到圈禁、累积性破坏以及缺乏稳定战后状态等历史特定条件的塑造。

### [37] Each building tells a story: A New York City neighborhood on the cusp of densification

**每栋建筑都在讲述故事：处于加密化前夕的纽约市街区**

- 作者：Mara Sidney, Tamara Velasquez Leiferman, Joshua McWhirter
- 发表：2026-11-17｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261500283](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261500283)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：加密化、重新区划、可负担住房、纽约

**Abstract:** This study seeks to understand the business of densification through analysis of public policies, property records, and “the everyday” in a rapidly changing Manhattan neighborhood. Our case study of Inwood uses a new conceptual framework for urban policy analysis to bring multiple data sources together to examine four sites that show how key elements of New York City’s rezoning strategy intersects with other housing policies. Our findings speak to the challenges of maintaining affordable housing stock amid densification and the importance of including everyday experiences in policy analysis. We find that new densification policies interact with pre-existing policies to produce complex, contradictory outcomes. For example, de-densification coexists with densification. Second, although some manifestations of densification are visible, such as high-rise buildings, others are not, including tenant displacement and apartment warehousing. Finally, we find that policy implementation works as ad hoc policy making because actors seek policy changes through alternative channels after rezoning is formally adopted.

**摘要：** 本研究通过分析公共政策、房产记录和“日常生活”，试图理解曼哈顿一个快速变化的街区中的加密化（densification）运作。我们对因伍德（Inwood）的案例研究采用一种新的城市政策分析概念框架，整合多种数据来源，考察四个地块，以展示纽约市重新区划策略的关键要素如何与其他住房政策相交织。研究结果揭示了在加密化过程中维持可负担住房存量所面临的挑战，以及将日常经验纳入政策分析的重要性。我们发现，新的加密化政策与既有政策相互作用，产生复杂而矛盾的结果。例如，去密化与加密化并存。其次，加密化的某些表现形式是可见的，如高层建筑，而另一些则不可见，包括租户迁离和公寓囤积（apartment warehousing）。最后，我们发现政策实施实际上是一种临时性的政策制定，因为在重新区划正式通过后，行动者会通过其他渠道寻求政策变更。

### [38] Planning for circular urban metabolism: Intertwining skills and spatial transformation for manufacturing transition in Padua and Venice, Italy

**面向循环城市代谢的规划：意大利帕多瓦与威尼斯制造业转型中技能与空间转型的交织**

- 作者：Claudia Faraone, Francesca Gambarotto, Luca Nicoletto et al.
- 发表：2026-11-11｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261499471](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261499471)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：循环经济、城市代谢、制造业转型、空间规划

**Abstract:** The shift from a linear to a circular economic structure needs a new urban metabolism that minimizes waste and the use of raw materials and energy. However, a city’s potential for circularity is contingent upon its manufacturing path-dependency, both from an economic and spatial perspective. The research looks at two Italian industrial areas—Padua and Venice—and proceeds to investigate the recent dynamics of their manufacturing spatial distribution at the “transect” level scale. Using a mixed-method approach that combines morphological-spatial analysis, urban planning assessment, and quantitative labor and waste data, the study investigates how manufacturing industries contribute to circular transitions and how planning policies can support or hinder this process. Findings show that technological manufacturing, supported by technical skills and green jobs, drives circular performance through waste prevention and recycling. Industrial diversification and firm relatedness further enable symbiotic material flows. However, both cities reveal a persistent misalignment between emerging circular dynamics and existing zoning frameworks. While Padua has begun adopting mixed-use zoning, planning strategies in both areas remain fragmented and reactive. The paper calls for more integrated, circularity-oriented spatial planning that mobilizes skills and proximity as key territorial resources.

**摘要：** 从线性经济结构向循环经济结构的转变需要一种新的城市代谢，以最大限度减少废弃物以及原材料和能源的使用。然而，一座城市的循环潜力取决于其制造业的路径依赖，这既涉及经济层面也涉及空间层面。本研究考察意大利两个工业地区——帕多瓦与威尼斯，并在“断面”（transect）尺度上探究其制造业空间分布的近期动态。研究采用混合方法，结合形态—空间分析、城市规划评估以及劳动力与废弃物的定量数据，探讨制造业如何促进循环转型，以及规划政策如何支持或阻碍这一过程。研究发现，在技术技能与绿色就业支撑下，技术型制造业通过废弃物预防与回收推动循环绩效。产业多样化与企业关联性进一步促成共生性物质流动。然而，两座城市均显示出新兴循环动态与既有分区框架之间持续存在错配。尽管帕多瓦已开始采用混合用途分区，但两地的规划策略仍然碎片化且被动应对。本文呼吁采取更加整合、以循环为导向的空间规划，调动技能与邻近性作为关键地域资源。

### [39] Wealth, homeownership, and land use regulatory changes

**财富、住房自有与土地利用管制变化**

- 作者：Diego Gil, Hugo E. Silva, Kenzo Asahi
- 发表：2026-11-11｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261498609](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261498609)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：土地利用管制、住房自有、增长机器、圣地亚哥

**Abstract:** This paper examines patterns of land use regulatory changes in Santiago, Chile, focusing on downzoning (stricter regulations) and upzoning (more flexible regulations), and their associations with sociodemographic factors. We analyze an original georeferenced dataset covering all 34 urban municipalities of Santiago over 20 years, together with 19 semi-structured interviews with key actors. The quantitative analysis suggests that high-income neighborhoods are associated with a greater likelihood of downzoning when homeownership is comparatively low. With our block-level measure of wealth, however, this association is not statistically distinguishable from zero at the sample-average homeownership rate and becomes negative at higher rates. Therefore, in affluent neighborhoods, the significant presence of homeowners is associated with less probability of downzoning. Outside high-income neighborhoods, higher homeownership is instead associated with downzoning. Qualitative evidence points to the growing role of organized residents, with socially connected groups using participatory reforms and informal ties to municipal decision-makers to shape local regulatory debates. This positive association is absent when the analysis is restricted to large-scale zoning changes. The findings contribute to debates between the “growth machine” and “homevoter” theories by suggesting that the relevance of each framework may vary across patterns of social segmentation and local demographic structures.

**摘要：** 本文考察智利圣地亚哥土地利用管制变化的模式，重点关注降低开发强度的分区调整（downzoning，即更严格的管制）与提高开发强度的分区调整（upzoning，即更灵活的管制），以及二者与社会人口因素的关联。我们分析了一个覆盖圣地亚哥全部34个城市市镇、时间跨度20年的原创地理参考数据集，并结合对关键行动者的19次半结构式访谈。定量分析表明，当住房自有率相对较低时，高收入街区与更高的降级分区可能性相关。然而，采用街区层面的财富测度时，在样本平均住房自有率水平下，这一关联在统计上与零无显著差异，而在更高的自有率下则转为负向。因此，在富裕街区，房主的大量存在与较低的降级分区概率相关；而在高收入街区之外，更高的住房自有率则与降级分区相关。定性证据表明有组织居民的作用日益增强，社会关系紧密的群体利用参与式改革以及与市政决策者的非正式联系来影响地方管制辩论。当分析仅限于大规模分区变化时，这一正向关联并不存在。研究结果为“增长机器”（growth machine）理论与“房主选民”（homevoter）理论之间的争论作出贡献，表明每种框架的适用性可能因社会分割模式与地方人口结构而异。

### [40] Activity spaces, community resource brokers, and real-time stress and affect: Evidence from the Boston Activity Space and Health Study

**活动空间、社区资源中介与实时压力和情绪：来自波士顿活动空间与健康研究的证据**

- 作者：Gregory Sharp, Emily Walton
- 发表：2026-11-11｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261500260](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261500260)
- 主题：健康、移动性与可达性；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：活动空间、社区资源中介、生态瞬时评估、健康与福祉

**Abstract:** While we know that the places where we live matter for our health and well-being, other spaces in the city we visit during the day—like places where we work, worship, exercise, and buy groceries—also matter. Little work, however, has explored how daily experiences with these routine community institutions and organizations shape our emotional responses. This study uses data from the Boston Activity Space and Health Study (BASHS), a smartphone-based data collection of adults’ GPS locations and ecological momentary assessments, to capture in-the-moment responses to their daily activity space environments for 7 days. We use random effects models to estimate associations between resource broker access and real-time accounts of stress and affect, and whether these relationships are conditional on the utilization of the resource brokers. Our findings suggest that beyond having regular access to community resource brokers, utilizing them on a regular basis is impactful for alleviating momentary emotional and physiological distress. By detailing the unique role of resource brokers, the current study makes substantive and methodological contributions to our understanding of how activity spaces matter for health and well-being.

**摘要：** 我们知道，居住地对健康和福祉至关重要，而我们白天在城市中造访的其他空间——如工作、礼拜、锻炼和购买食品杂货的场所——同样重要。然而，鲜有研究探讨与这些日常社区机构和组织的日常经历如何塑造我们的情绪反应。本研究利用波士顿活动空间与健康研究（BASHS）的数据——一项基于智能手机、收集成年人GPS位置和生态瞬时评估（ecological momentary assessment）的数据采集——捕捉受访者在7天内对其日常活动空间环境的即时反应。我们使用随机效应模型估计资源中介可及性与实时压力和情绪报告之间的关联，以及这些关系是否取决于对资源中介的使用。研究结果表明，除了能够经常接触社区资源中介之外，定期使用它们对于缓解瞬时的情绪和生理困扰具有显著作用。通过详细阐述资源中介的独特作用，本研究在实质和方法层面推进了我们对活动空间如何影响健康与福祉的理解。

### [41] The gendered right to the city: Mediated access, urban agriculture and women’s empowerment in Kumasi

**性别化的城市权利：库马西的中介性准入、城市农业与女性赋权**

- 作者：Mary Duah, John Boulard Forkuor, David Forkuor et al.
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261503405](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261503405)
- 主题：劳动、生计与日常生活；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：城市权利、城市农业、女性赋权、全球南方

**Abstract:** Urban space is a vital resource for city dwellers. While literature examines the use of formal public spaces by urban residents for commerce and social activities, scholarly attention to informal agricultural spaces remains limited. This study uses a synthesis of Kabeer’s empowerment framework and Lefebvre’s right to the city to analyse women’s access to and use of urban space for agriculture in Ghana as a transformative space where gender, body and empowerment intersect. Our findings reveal that male networks serve as gatekeepers through whom women access urban space for urban agriculture. While women’s access to this social capital is empowering, it indicates that women’s right to the city and to spatial justice is mediated, achieved through relational networks and experienced as a relational right. Our findings further reveal that women appropriate these urban spaces in ways that enhance their general well-being, bodily sovereignty and promote intergenerational rights to the city for their children. By demonstrating how women’s spatial access enables agency, bodily sovereignty and the pursuit of intergenerational rights, we argue for a more nuanced understanding of empowerment and rights within informal urban contexts in the Global South.

**摘要：** 城市空间是城市居民的重要资源。尽管已有文献考察了城市居民对正式公共空间用于商业和社会活动的使用，但学界对非正式农业空间的关注仍然有限。本研究综合卡比尔（Kabeer）的赋权框架与列斐伏尔（Lefebvre）的城市权利（right to the city）理论，分析加纳女性获取并利用城市空间从事农业的情况，将其视为性别、身体与赋权交汇的变革性空间。研究发现，男性网络充当守门人，女性须借助其获得用于城市农业的城市空间。尽管女性获得这种社会资本具有赋权作用，但这表明女性的城市权利与空间正义是经由中介实现的，是通过关系网络达成并作为一种关系性权利被体验的。研究进一步发现，女性以增进其总体福祉、身体主权的方式占用这些城市空间，并为其子女促进代际城市权利。通过展示女性的空间准入如何促成能动性、身体主权以及对代际权利的追求，本文主张对全球南方非正式城市情境中的赋权与权利形成更为细致的理解。

### [42] Limits on growth: Governing urban futures in Beijing

**增长的限度：北京城市未来的治理**

- 作者：Yiqiu Liu
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/00420980261501380](https://doi.org/10.1177/00420980261501380)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：增长限制、城市未来、国家治理、北京

**Abstract:** This paper conceptualises limits on growth in Beijing, whose 2016–2035 master plan constructs a highly selective national capital for the central state by removing functions and populations deemed non-essential. Drawing on archival analysis of planning documents since 1949, extensive expert and ethnographic interviews, and multi-sited fieldwork conducted between 2017 and 2023, it develops a Lefebvrian spatio-temporal framework with three relational dimensions for analysing urban future-making. Temporal entanglements show how a historically sedimented anti-metropolis disposition is reactivated amid contemporary state recentralisation. Performative governance examines how the diagnosis of ‘big city disease’ reframes demographic and functional pressures as urban pathology, legitimising relocation and greening as therapeutic intervention. Lived futurity reveals how officials, villagers and migrants negotiate these measures through procrastination, speculation and retreat. The article theorises limits on growth as an emergent mode of Chinese statecraft in which extra-economic priorities selectively guide the production of the urban future. Situating Beijing within a wider, politically heterogeneous turn that increasingly treats demographic and territorial growth as a crisis, it reconceives urban future-making as a politics of foreclosure: not only who profits from growth, but who is permitted to remain when growth is limited by design.

**摘要：** 本文对北京的“增长限制”进行概念化。北京2016—2035年总体规划通过疏解被视为非必要的功能与人口，为中央国家建构一个高度选择性的首都。基于对1949年以来规划文件的档案分析、大量专家访谈与民族志访谈，以及2017—2023年间开展的多点田野工作，本文发展出一个包含三个关系维度的列斐伏尔式时空框架，用于分析城市未来的营造。“时间缠绕”揭示了一种历史沉积的反大都市倾向如何在当代国家再集权中被重新激活；“表演性治理”考察“大城市病”诊断如何将人口与功能压力重构为城市病理，从而使搬迁与绿化作为治疗性干预获得合法性；“生活中的未来性”揭示官员、村民与流动人口如何通过拖延、投机与退守来应对这些措施。本文将增长限制理论化为一种新兴的中国治国术模式，在其中超经济的优先事项有选择地引导城市未来的生产。将北京置于一个更广泛、政治上异质的转向之中——该转向日益将人口与地域增长视为危机——本文将城市未来营造重新理解为一种排除的政治：问题不仅在于谁从增长中获利，更在于当增长被刻意限制时，谁被允许留下。

## Applied Geography

*本期收录：15 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [43] Economic benefits of ecological restoration in European Union rivers: a cultural ecosystem services valuation

**欧盟河流生态修复的经济效益：基于文化生态系统服务的价值评估**

- 作者：Francesc Comalada, Michael Sinclair, Xavier Garcia
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104247](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104247)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：文化生态系统服务、河流生态修复、旅行费用模型、社交媒体数据

**Abstract:** Rivers are among Europe's most valued and degraded ecosystems, yet the case for restoring them is usually made in ecological terms. One reason is that the non-material benefits people draw from rivers, cultural ecosystem services, are harder to quantify than ecological outcomes and rarely enter the economic case. We combine geotagged Flickr photographs (2022–2024), a deep-learning classifier and a spatial Bayesian hurdle travel-cost model to estimate the use value of these services across 22 EU Member States and simulate what a one-class improvement in ecological status would deliver. Ecological status predicts whether people visit a river reach at all, not how often they return once they do. A one-class improvement raises use value at every treated reach, but the gains concentrate close to cities rather than following regional population or river size. Over the 6650 reaches with observed activity, they amount to about 200 million euros per year. Reported at reach and basin scale, the results give planners a spatially explicit basis for prioritising river restoration under the Water Framework Directive and the Nature Restoration Regulation.

**摘要：** 河流是欧洲最受珍视、同时也是退化最严重的生态系统之一，然而支持河流修复的论证通常是从生态角度提出的。原因之一在于，人们从河流中获得的非物质惠益——即文化生态系统服务（cultural ecosystem services）——比生态成效更难量化，因而很少被纳入经济论证。本文结合带地理标签的Flickr照片（2022–2024年）、深度学习分类器以及空间贝叶斯障碍旅行费用模型（spatial Bayesian hurdle travel-cost model），估算了22个欧盟成员国中这些服务的使用价值，并模拟了生态状况提升一个等级所能带来的效益。生态状况能够预测人们是否会造访某一河段，但无法预测其造访后重访的频率。生态状况提升一个等级会提高每一个受处理河段的使用价值，但收益集中于城市周边，而非随区域人口或河流规模分布。在6650个有观测活动的河段中，这些收益合计约为每年2亿欧元。研究结果在河段和流域尺度上予以报告，为规划者依据《水框架指令》（Water Framework Directive）和《自然恢复法》（Nature Restoration Regulation）确定河流修复优先次序提供了空间明确的依据。

### [45] Perception and interregional flows of cultural ecosystem services in a megacity: Integrating large language models and the metacoupling framework

**特大城市文化生态系统服务的感知与区际流动：整合大语言模型与远程耦合框架**

- 作者：Wei Chen, Wanting Peng, Wenbo Cai et al.
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104248](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104248)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：文化生态系统服务、大语言模型、远程耦合、上海

**Abstract:** Cultural ecosystem services (CES) are fundamental to human well-being, yet their inherent subjectivity and non-material nature pose significant challenges for large-scale quantification and the measurement of interregional flows. To address these challenges, this study investigated the spatiotemporal perceptual patterns of CES and interregional cultural ecosystem service flows (CESF) in Shanghai by developing a definition-driven zero-shot classification workflow based on large language models (LLMs) using user-generated content from Ctrip. Review data from 153 points of interest in 2019 and 2023 were comparatively analyzed to examine intra-urban CES dynamics, and user IP location data were further integrated within a metacoupling framework to characterize the coupling structure and spatial spillover patterns of cross-provincial CESF. Results indicated that the LLM-CES approach achieved high accuracy and stability in multi-category identification, providing a scalable pathway for extracting CES perceptions from large-scale social media data. From 2019 to 2023, CES in Shanghai exhibited a pattern of quantitative decline, structural stability, and spatial contraction. At the national scale, CESF demonstrated a distance-decay pattern characterized by high-intensity short-distance interactions and broad long-distance diffusion. This study advances CES research by integrating perception and flow within a unified analytical framework and offers a transferable methodological approach for assessing megacity CES and their interregional impacts, with implications for well-being-oriented urban planning and cross-regional governance.

**摘要：** 文化生态系统服务（cultural ecosystem services, CES）是人类福祉的基础，但其固有的主观性和非物质性给大尺度量化及区际流动测度带来了重大挑战。为应对这些挑战，本研究利用携程用户生成内容，构建了一种基于大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）的定义驱动型零样本分类流程，考察了上海CES的时空感知格局以及区际文化生态系统服务流（CESF）。研究对2019年和2023年153个兴趣点的评论数据进行比较分析，以考察城市内部CES的动态变化，并进一步将用户IP属地数据纳入远程耦合框架（metacoupling framework），刻画跨省CESF的耦合结构与空间溢出格局。结果表明，LLM-CES方法在多类别识别中具有较高的准确性和稳定性，为从大规模社交媒体数据中提取CES感知提供了可扩展的路径。2019年至2023年间，上海CES呈现出数量下降、结构稳定和空间收缩的格局。在全国尺度上，CESF表现出距离衰减格局，其特征为高强度的短距离互动与广泛的长距离扩散。本研究将感知与流动整合于统一的分析框架之中，推进了CES研究，并为评估特大城市CES及其区际影响提供了可迁移的方法路径，对面向福祉的城市规划与跨区域治理具有启示意义。

### [46] Urban rail transit, economic activity, and land development: A spatial difference-in-differences analysis of the Bangkok Metropolitan Region

**城市轨道交通、经济活动与土地开发：曼谷大都市区的空间双重差分分析**

- 作者：Naravit Thongnak, Daniel del Barrio Alvarez, Prasongchai Setthasuravich et al.
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104235](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104235)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：城市轨道交通、空间双重差分、夜间灯光、土地开发

**Abstract:** Urban rail transit (URT) systems are widely promoted as instruments for improving urban mobility, stimulating local economic activity, and encouraging sustainable urban land development. However, empirical evidence on whether transit investments simultaneously promote both economic growth and land development remains limited, particularly in rapidly urbanizing cities where supporting land-use policies may be weak. This study examines the impacts of the Purple Line in the Bangkok Metropolitan Region (BMR), Thailand, on local economic activity and land development. Using satellite-based indicators, including Nighttime Light (NTL) and the Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI), as proxies for economic activity and land conversion, the analysis applies a spatial difference-in-differences (SDID) approach to identify causal effects while accounting for spatial spillovers. The results show that the introduction of the URT significantly increased local economic activity, with both direct and indirect spillover effects across neighboring areas. In contrast, land development near stations progressed more slowly relative to areas farther away, suggesting that improved accessibility alone does not necessarily accelerate physical development. These findings imply that land market dynamics, regulatory constraints, and speculative behavior may delay development responses following transit investments. Heterogeneity analysis further reveals stronger economic responses in initially underdeveloped areas. The study contributes to the literature by jointly evaluating economic and land development outcomes using SDID methods and satellite data. The results highlight the importance of integrating transportation investment with supportive land-use policies to ensure that accessibility improvements translate into sustainable urban development.

**摘要：** 城市轨道交通（urban rail transit, URT）系统被广泛推崇为改善城市交通、刺激地方经济活动和促进可持续城市土地开发的工具。然而，关于交通投资能否同时促进经济增长和土地开发的实证证据仍然有限，在配套土地利用政策可能较为薄弱的快速城市化城市中尤其如此。本研究考察了泰国曼谷大都市区（BMR）紫线对地方经济活动和土地开发的影响。研究以夜间灯光（Nighttime Light, NTL）和归一化植被指数（NDVI）等卫星指标分别作为经济活动和土地转换的代理变量，采用空间双重差分（spatial difference-in-differences, SDID）方法，在考虑空间溢出的同时识别因果效应。结果表明，URT的开通显著提升了地方经济活动，并对邻近地区产生了直接和间接的溢出效应。相比之下，车站附近的土地开发进展相对于较远地区更为缓慢，这表明可达性的改善本身并不必然加速物质开发。这些发现意味着，土地市场动态、监管约束和投机行为可能延缓交通投资后的开发响应。异质性分析进一步揭示，初始欠发达地区的经济响应更为强烈。本研究通过运用SDID方法和卫星数据对经济和土地开发成效进行联合评估，为相关文献作出了贡献。研究结果强调了将交通投资与支持性土地利用政策相结合的重要性，以确保可达性改善能够转化为可持续的城市发展。

### [53] Does telehealth narrow the urban-rural gap? A dual assessment of in-person and telehealth prenatal care accessibility

**远程医疗能否缩小城乡差距？面对面与远程产前保健可达性的双重评估**

- 作者：Dikshya Panta, Sicheng Wang, Yanfeng Xu et al.
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104216](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104216)
- 主题：健康、移动性与可达性；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：远程医疗、产前保健、城乡差异、浮动集水区法

**Abstract:** Telehealth has been proposed to reduce rural prenatal care barriers, but its equity effects depend on whether predicted accessibility translates into observed utilization. We examine whether predicted and revealed in-person and telehealth prenatal care accessibility diverge across the urban-rural continuum of South Carolina and whether these measures align with ZIP Code Tabulation Area (ZCTA)-level birth-related need, measured as average annual live births by residence. Using Medicaid claims data for 2020–2021, predicted accessibility was estimated using floating catchment area methods, with the telehealth specification incorporating broadband availability and subscription constraints; revealed accessibility was operationalized as observed prenatal visit volume within 30-min catchments across 424 ZCTAs. Urban-rural differences were assessed with Mann-Whitney U tests, and associations with birth volume were examined using spatial lag and spatial error models. Predicted in-person accessibility did not differ significantly between urban and rural ZCTAs (p = .67), whereas revealed in-person accessibility was significantly lower in rural areas (p < .001), indicating that predicted opportunity did not translate uniformly into observed utilization. Both predicted and revealed telehealth accessibility also showed significant urban-rural disparities (p < .001), with 36.76% of rural ZCTAs recording zero predicted telehealth accessibility compared with 7.53% of urban ZCTAs. These findings show that telehealth expanded potential access but operated within existing in-person care networks and did not equalize access. Improving rural prenatal care access will therefore require stabilizing in-person obstetric capacity, strengthening digital infrastructure, and supporting provider adoption of telehealth.

**摘要：** 远程医疗（telehealth）被认为可以减少农村地区的产前保健障碍，但其公平效应取决于预测的可达性能否转化为实际观测到的利用。本文考察了在南卡罗来纳州城乡连续体上，预测的与显示的面对面及远程产前保健可达性是否存在差异，以及这些测度是否与邮政编码统计区（ZIP Code Tabulation Area, ZCTA）层面的生育相关需求（以按居住地统计的年均活产数衡量）相匹配。研究利用2020–2021年医疗补助（Medicaid）理赔数据，采用浮动集水区方法估算预测可达性，其中远程医疗的设定纳入了宽带可用性和订阅约束；显示可达性则操作化为424个ZCTA在30分钟集水区内实际观测到的产前就诊量。研究采用Mann-Whitney U检验评估城乡差异，并采用空间滞后模型和空间误差模型考察其与生育数量的关联。预测的面对面可达性在城市与农村ZCTA之间无显著差异（p = .67），而显示的面对面可达性在农村地区显著较低（p < .001），表明预测的机会并未均匀地转化为实际利用。预测和显示的远程医疗可达性也均表现出显著的城乡差异（p < .001），其中36.76%的农村ZCTA预测远程医疗可达性为零，而城市ZCTA的这一比例为7.53%。这些发现表明，远程医疗扩大了潜在可及性，但其运作依附于既有的面对面医疗网络，并未实现可及性的均等化。因此，改善农村产前保健可及性需要稳定面对面产科服务能力、加强数字基础设施，并支持医疗服务提供者采用远程医疗。

### [56] Livestreaming E-commerce and the spatial reconfiguration of urban retail functions in China: An urban value chain approach

**直播电商与中国城市零售功能的空间重构：城市价值链视角**

- 作者：Yinghao Zhang, John R. Bryson, Yanping Wang
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104244](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.apgeog.2026.104244)
- 主题：全球化、地缘经济与产业网络；区域发展、创新与演化经济地理｜关键词：直播电商、城市价值链、城市等级体系、平台经济

**Abstract:** The rise of livestreaming e-commerce (LSE) is reshaping the spatial configuration of urban retail functions, yet its relationship to traditional urban hierarchies remains unclear. Drawing on urban value chain (UVC) theory, this study examines how LSE reconfigures retail functions in China's urban system. Based on data from 289 prefecture-level and above cities covering LSE sales, conventional and luxury retail, light manufacturing, logistics, and MCN agencies, the analysis suggests that LSE activity exhibits significant spatial clustering, with a global Moran's I of 0.272 for LSE and 0.513 for conventional retail, whilst luxury retail shows no significant clustering. This spatial pattern overlaps with but also diverges from traditional urban size hierarchies. Amongst the sixteen Tier I LSE cities, nine fall outside the megacity and very large city categories defined by urban district population, indicating partial decoupling between LSE positioning and conventional size hierarchies. Some medium-sized industrial-belt cities such as Jiaxing hold high LSE positions alongside strong industrial clusters, whilst some traditional core cities such as Chongqing underperform in LSE provision despite substantial population and consumption capacity. Through multidimensional cross-tabulation together with formal statistical and spatial regression analysis, a multidimensional urban retail function hierarchy is constructed, from which three typical UVC configurations of multi-reinforcement, external embedding, and platform upgrading are distinguished as exploratory ideal types. These findings suggest that digital platforms are reshaping the spatial division of labor in urban retail, contributing to an emerging research agenda on the geography of platform-mediated retail and its interaction with established urban hierarchies.

**摘要：** 直播电商（livestreaming e-commerce, LSE）的兴起正在重塑城市零售功能的空间格局，但其与传统城市等级体系的关系仍不明确。本研究借鉴城市价值链（urban value chain, UVC）理论，考察LSE如何重构中国城市体系中的零售功能。基于289个地级及以上城市的数据（涵盖LSE销售额、常规零售与奢侈品零售、轻工制造业、物流以及MCN机构），分析表明LSE活动呈现显著的空间集聚，LSE的全局Moran's I为0.272，常规零售为0.513，而奢侈品零售则无显著集聚。这一空间格局与传统城市规模等级既有重叠，也有偏离。在16个一级LSE城市中，有9个不属于按城区人口划定的超大城市和特大城市类别，表明LSE地位与传统规模等级之间存在部分脱钩。部分中等规模的产业带城市（如嘉兴）凭借强大的产业集群占据较高的LSE地位，而部分传统核心城市（如重庆）尽管拥有庞大的人口和消费能力，其LSE供给表现却不尽如人意。通过多维交叉制表以及正式的统计与空间回归分析，本文构建了多维城市零售功能等级体系，并据此区分出多重强化、外部嵌入和平台升级三种典型UVC配置，作为探索性的理想类型。这些发现表明，数字平台正在重塑城市零售的空间分工，为平台中介零售地理及其与既有城市等级体系相互作用这一新兴研究议程作出了贡献。

### [58] Incorporating technological discontinuity and cognitive coherence: Extra-regional knowledge spillovers and the New Energy Vehicle (NEV) innovation in the US

**纳入技术不连续性与认知一致性：区域外知识溢出与美国新能源汽车创新**

- 作者：Junyang Liu, Shengjun Zhu, Yongyuan Huang et al.
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/0308518x261491189](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261491189)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；能源转型与基础设施｜关键词：知识溢出、新能源汽车、技术不连续性、认知一致性

**Abstract:** Green industries represented by New Energy Vehicles have become the focus of academic and economic debate. Based on patent data from the USPTO from 1990 to 2019, this paper examines the critical role of extra-regional knowledge spillovers on New Energy Vehicle (NEV) innovation in the United States. We unpack the structure of extra-regional knowledge spillovers from the technological domain dimension and cognitive dimension, the latter of which remains insufficiently conceptualized and examined in existing studies. Empirical results disclose the role of technological discontinuity and cognitive coherence in the process of technical change, highlighting that a knowledge pool drawn from diverse and distant technological domains and organized around coherent innovation problems is crucial in NEV innovation. This paper deepens the understanding of the knowledge spillovers required for innovation in emerging technologies.

**摘要：** 以新能源汽车为代表的绿色产业已成为学术与经济讨论的焦点。本文基于1990–2019年美国专利商标局（USPTO）的专利数据，考察了区域外知识溢出对美国新能源汽车（NEV）创新的关键作用。我们从技术领域维度和认知维度剖析区域外知识溢出的结构，其中后者在现有研究中仍未得到充分的概念化和检验。实证结果揭示了技术不连续性与认知一致性在技术变迁过程中的作用，强调一个汲取自多样且相距较远的技术领域、并围绕一致的创新问题加以组织的知识池，对于新能源汽车创新至关重要。本文深化了对新兴技术创新所需知识溢出的理解。

### [59] The pre-oil boomtown: The anticipatory politics of oil in Uganda and urban transformation in Hoima City

**前石油繁荣城镇：乌干达石油的预期政治与霍伊马市的城市转型**

- 作者：Alexander Dodge, Mercy Arinaitwe
- 发表：2026-11-19｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/0308518x261499537](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261499537)
- 主题：能源转型与基础设施；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：前石油繁荣城镇、预期政治、开采前沿、乌干达

**Abstract:** “Oil City” may invoke imaginaries of opulence, high-rise buildings, men in suits, and limousines. Dubai, Riyadh, and Baku spring to mind. Yet the reality for many settlements near oil fields is a stark contrast. “Boomtown” scholars have documented the rapid growth in towns/cities accompanying oil developments, only to be followed by urban decay. While the literature accounts for the speculative frenzy in boomtowns that follow oil discoveries, less attention has been paid to the affective resonances and anticipatory politics that shape speculation in what we term “pre-oil boomtowns”: cities that experience both growth and decline before oil production begins. This study explores how the anticipatory politics surrounding prospective resource production shape urban transformation in extractive frontiers. Our study is informed by a case study of Hoima, which has been designated as Uganda’s “Oil City” under Uganda’s Vision 2040 national development blueprint and has boomed since the discovery of nearby oil fields in 2006. While the development of oil has been a long and protracted affair, first oil is now expected by 2027. Based on interviews with city officials, property developers, and the local community, we find that there are few direct economic linkages to the oil industry in Hoima city. Instead, we find that the boom is fueled by speculation and imagined futures of development among city planners, service companies, local entrepreneurs, and domestic migrants. At the same time, we observe anxieties and doubts among the local community surrounding uneven development and increasing migration, costs, and poverty.

**摘要：** “石油城市”或许会让人联想到富裕奢华、高楼林立、西装革履的男士和豪华轿车，迪拜、利雅得和巴库随即浮现脑海。然而，许多油田附近聚落的现实却截然不同。“繁荣城镇”（boomtown）研究者记录了伴随石油开发而来的城镇快速增长及随后的城市衰败。尽管现有文献阐述了石油发现之后繁荣城镇中的投机狂热，但对于塑造我们所称“前石油繁荣城镇”（pre-oil boomtowns）——即在石油生产开始之前便经历增长与衰退的城市——中投机行为的情感共鸣与预期政治（anticipatory politics），关注仍然较少。本研究探讨了围绕预期资源生产的预期政治如何塑造开采前沿地带的城市转型。研究以霍伊马（Hoima）为案例：该市在乌干达《2040愿景》国家发展蓝图中被定为乌干达的“石油城市”，自2006年附近发现油田以来经历了繁荣发展。尽管石油开发历程漫长而曲折，但首批石油预计将于2027年产出。基于对城市官员、房地产开发商和当地社区的访谈，我们发现霍伊马市与石油产业之间几乎没有直接的经济联系。相反，这一繁荣是由城市规划者、服务公司、本地企业家和国内移民的投机行为及其对发展的想象未来所驱动的。与此同时，我们也观察到当地社区对不均衡发展以及移民增加、生活成本上升和贫困加剧的焦虑与疑虑。

### [60] Safe bonds, risky debt? Covered bonds and the financialisation of housing in Sweden

**安全的债券，风险的债务？担保债券与瑞典住房金融化**

- 作者：Viktor Skyrman, Timothy Blackwell, Juan Barredo et al.
- 发表：2026-11-12｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/0308518x261499187](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261499187)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产｜关键词：担保债券、住房金融化、金融地理学、瑞典

**Abstract:** Contributing to the literatures on housing financialisation and financial geography, this article examines the growth and role of covered bonds in fuelling housing financialisation in Sweden. Lauded as safer and more efficient alternatives to residential mortgage-backed securities, covered mortgage bonds – on-balance-sheet debt securities issued by banks to fund mortgages – provide investors with robust protection through dual recourse to both the issuing bank and the segregated ‘cover pool’ of mortgage assets. While the structure of these instruments provides a derisking function for bondholders, they simultaneously reconfigure the architecture of housing finance in permissive mortgage regimes, such as Sweden’s, by intensifying the supply of mortgage credit in highly leveraged urban centres and concentrating credit risk on the balance sheets of domestic banks. Drawing on the historiography of regulatory change, policy debates and interviews with stakeholders, the article investigates how, despite resistance from state authorities, Swedish finance lobbyists were able to successfully frame covered bond legislation as essential to maintaining international financial competitiveness. The Swedish case illustrates how the interplay between the structural power of domestic financial elites and European financial integration, mediated through national policy settings, reshapes housing and finance systems, deepening systemic interdependencies between the financial system, households and the state, while reconfiguring the geographies of financial risk.

**摘要：** 本文为住房金融化和金融地理学文献作出贡献，考察了担保债券（covered bonds）在推动瑞典住房金融化中的增长与作用。担保抵押债券——由银行发行、记入资产负债表内、用于为抵押贷款融资的债务证券——被誉为比住房抵押贷款支持证券更安全、更高效的替代品，它通过对发行银行和隔离的抵押资产“担保池”的双重追索权为投资者提供稳健保护。尽管这些工具的结构为债券持有人提供了去风险功能，但在瑞典这类宽松的抵押贷款体制中，它们同时重构了住房金融的架构：加剧了高杠杆城市中心的抵押信贷供给，并将信用风险集中于国内银行的资产负债表上。基于监管变迁的史学梳理、政策辩论以及对利益相关者的访谈，本文探究了瑞典金融游说者如何在国家当局的抵制下，成功地将担保债券立法框定为维持国际金融竞争力所必需的举措。瑞典案例表明，国内金融精英的结构性权力与欧洲金融一体化之间的相互作用，经由国家政策环境的中介，重塑了住房与金融体系，加深了金融体系、家庭与国家之间的系统性相互依赖，同时重构了金融风险的地理格局。

### [61] Licensing liberal imperialism: The ‘rogue recruiter’ and the racial logic of low-wage labor supply chains

**为自由帝国主义发放许可：“流氓招募者”与低工资劳动力供应链的种族逻辑**

- 作者：Michelle Buckley, Paula Chakravartty
- 发表：2026-11-05｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/0308518x261489455](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261489455)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：劳务招募、契约劳工、种族辩证法、印度

**Abstract:** Within much contemporary migration policy, labor recruiters are often understood as unscrupulous and state-evading actors embedded in informal networks of brokerage who profit from the demand for cheap labor abroad. To historians and other scholars of the British imperial indenture system, however, private labor recruiters of the late 19th century are understood as firmly situated within the imperial state’s apparatus of plantation labor recruitment and management. This paper seeks to bring analyses of scholars of indenture recruitment into closer conversation with critical migration scholarship on the contemporary regulation of labor recruiters in India. Drawing insights from these literatures together with archival data primarily on the 19th century Allahabad-Trinidad and indenture migration corridor which illuminates the structure and operation of indenture recruitment networks in the region that is present-day Uttar Pradesh, we examine the colonial repertoires of recruiter regulation that form the foundations of subcontracted recruitment networks in the region today. In particular, we foreground how two mechanisms of control and regulation—pyramid subcontracting and licensing—were vital in establishing a class of informalized, commission-based recruiters at the lowest tiers of the network. Drawing upon Denise Ferreira da Silva’s notion of the racial dialectic, we highlight the enduring use of licensing and subcontracting as a “colonial racial arsenal” which has always failed to effectively regulate recruiters and their networks. We foreground how, both then and now, these tools have instead served to delineate first-mile recruiters as racial rule-breaking subjects in an otherwise orderly and fair migration regime.

**摘要：** 在许多当代移民政策中，劳务招募者常被视为嵌入非正式中介网络、逃避国家监管、从海外廉价劳动力需求中牟利的不择手段的行动者。然而，在研究英帝国契约劳工制度的历史学家及其他学者看来，19世纪末的私人劳务招募者牢固地处于帝国国家的种植园劳工招募与管理机构之中。本文旨在推动契约劳工招募研究与关于当代印度劳务招募者监管的批判性移民研究展开更密切的对话。综合这些文献的洞见，并主要借助关于19世纪阿拉哈巴德—特立尼达契约移民走廊的档案资料——这些资料揭示了今北方邦地区契约劳工招募网络的结构与运作——我们考察了构成该地区当今分包式招募网络基础的殖民时期招募者监管手段。特别地，我们突出了金字塔式分包与许可证制度这两种控制与监管机制，如何在确立处于网络最底层的一类非正规化、按佣金计酬的招募者方面发挥了关键作用。借鉴Denise Ferreira da Silva的“种族辩证法”概念，我们强调许可证与分包作为一种“殖民种族武器库”被持续使用，而它始终未能有效监管招募者及其网络。我们揭示出，无论过去还是现在，这些工具实际上都将“第一公里”招募者界定为在一个本应有序、公平的移民体制中违规越界的种族化主体。

### [62] When peripheral cities go viral: Platform interception, digital participation and the reconfiguration of city branding

**当边缘城市走红：平台截流、数字参与与城市品牌的重构**

- 作者：Xiaoyue Deng, Shenjing He
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/0308518x261491600](https://doi.org/10.1177/0308518x261491600)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：平台城市主义、网红城市、城市品牌、城市企业家主义

**Abstract:** The rise of ‘internet-celebrity cities’ ( wanghong ) has become a new emblem of platform urbanism. Yet existing platform studies offer limited insight into this phenomenon as a product of digitally enabled city branding, an oversight that obscures the new dynamics that digital platforms introduce into entrepreneurial governance. To address this gap, this paper proposes a physical–virtual complementary framework for examining how platforms’ operations and local practices coalesce to (re)shape the image of cities. The framework is illustrated through a case study of Rongjiang , a peripheral Chinese city distinguished by its dramatic internet virality. In Rongjiang ’s digital branding endeavour, digital platforms reconfigured the composition, roles, and power dynamics of the stakeholders involved in city branding, extended the co-creation of city branding by enabling massive and unsupervised participation, and consolidated their own unbalanced control over selective online visibility. These findings highlight a paradox of digital empowerment in city branding: platforms enabled more extensive and decentralised branding participation while introducing and reinforcing a new, algorithm-dominated pattern of participation that redefined local agency in city branding. This research extends platform-urbanism scholarship beyond its predominantly economic and cultural discourses to engage with city branding as an integral part of the urban entrepreneurialism literature. The findings advance understanding of urban entrepreneurialism with particular attention to transforming local practices around plural participation in a platform-mediated urban age.

**摘要：** “网红城市”（wanghong）的兴起已成为平台城市主义（platform urbanism）的新标志。然而，现有平台研究对这一作为数字化赋能的城市品牌塑造之产物的现象所提供的洞见有限，这一疏漏遮蔽了数字平台为企业家式治理所带来的新动态。为弥补这一空白，本文提出一个物理—虚拟互补框架，用以考察平台运作与地方实践如何相互结合以（重新）塑造城市形象。该框架通过对榕江——一个因其戏剧性网络走红而引人注目的中国边缘城市——的案例研究加以阐释。在榕江的数字品牌塑造中，数字平台重构了参与城市品牌塑造的利益相关者的构成、角色与权力关系，通过促成大规模且无监管的参与扩展了城市品牌的共同创造，并巩固了平台自身对选择性网络可见度的不平衡控制。这些发现凸显了城市品牌塑造中数字赋权的悖论：平台促成了更广泛、更去中心化的品牌参与，同时又引入并强化了一种新的、由算法主导的参与模式，重新界定了城市品牌塑造中的地方能动性。本研究将平台城市主义研究拓展到其以经济和文化为主的话语之外，将城市品牌塑造作为城市企业家主义文献的有机组成部分加以探讨。研究结果推进了对城市企业家主义的理解，尤其关注在平台中介的城市时代围绕多元参与而发生转变的地方实践。
