### [63] Rivers, peaks and the Car–Transit time gap: Evidence from cross-river travel in Wuhan, China

**河流、高峰与小汽车–公共交通出行时间差：来自中国武汉跨江出行的证据**

- 作者：Keyu Yao, Ran Peng
- 发表：2026-11-20｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261511039](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261511039)
- 主题：健康、移动性与可达性；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：跨江出行、出行时间差、公共交通、手机信令数据

**Abstract:** Rivers funnel urban travel onto a few crossings, but whether this increases the car–public transport (PT) travel-time gap, and how the gap changes in congestion, remains uncertain. We integrate one week of mobile phone origin–destination flows and route-planning travel times for 426 one-kilometre cells in central Wuhan. The modal competitiveness gap (MCG) is the demand-weighted mean log ratio of car to PT travel time. We contrast cross-Yangtze with other trips using fixed effects, measure peak narrowing of the gap (ΔMCG) and link it to place characteristics with spatially cross-validated random forests. PT takes 2.4 times as long as the car off-peak but 1.8 and 1.6 times in the morning and evening peaks, because car times rise by 34% and 43% while PT times change very little. For given origins and destinations, crossing the Yangtze raises the PT/car ratio by 9-12%; simple comparisons obscure this because cross-river trips end where PT is relatively good. At the morning peak PT still takes over twice the car’s time for 33% of cross-river trips, mostly far from direct cross-river metro stations. Static place characteristics explain little of the narrowing. Peak gains arise from car congestion, yet make metro-based PT an effective complement for river crossing.

**摘要：** 河流将城市出行汇聚到少数几个过江通道上，但这是否会扩大小汽车与公共交通（public transport, PT）之间的出行时间差距，以及该差距在拥堵时如何变化，仍不确定。本文整合了武汉中心城区426个1公里网格一周的手机起讫点（OD）流量数据与路径规划出行时间。出行方式竞争力差距（modal competitiveness gap, MCG）定义为小汽车与PT出行时间之比的对数经需求加权后的均值。我们采用固定效应模型比较跨长江出行与其他出行，测度高峰时段差距的收窄程度（ΔMCG），并利用空间交叉验证的随机森林将其与地方特征相关联。非高峰时段PT耗时为小汽车的2.4倍，而早晚高峰时段分别为1.8倍和1.6倍，原因在于小汽车出行时间分别增加了34%和43%，而PT出行时间几乎不变。在给定起讫点的条件下，跨越长江会使PT/小汽车出行时间比提高9–12%；简单比较掩盖了这一点，因为跨江出行的终点往往位于PT条件相对较好的地方。在早高峰时段，33%的跨江出行中PT耗时仍超过小汽车的两倍，这些出行大多远离直接跨江的地铁站。静态地方特征对差距收窄的解释力很弱。高峰时段的相对改善源于小汽车拥堵，但这使以地铁为主的PT成为跨江出行的有效补充。

### [64] Coordinating interactive and automated operations in generative urban design: The FLEXUrban prototype

**生成式城市设计中交互操作与自动化操作的协调：FLEXUrban原型**

- 作者：Baizhou Zhang, Biao Li, Yichen Mo et al.
- 发表：2026-11-19｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261505958](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261505958)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：生成式城市设计、参数化设计、城市形态、交互式工作流

**Abstract:** Generative urban design (GUD) offers an important methodological approach for exploring urban morphology configurations under complex constraints. Despite continued advances in generative techniques, coordinating automated operations with editable intermediate states and local intervention across multi-stage workflows remains a challenge. This paper presents FLEXUrban, a web-based GUD prototype that connects interactive editing and batch parameter assignment within a shared workflow. The system comprises three interrelated modules: tensor-field-guided hierarchical site subdivision organized around division-line dependencies; type-based parametric massing that maps planning controls to building geometry and calculated metrics; and grayscale image mapping that distributes area weights, target FAR, and buildable-area settings across regions. Controlled system-level tests examined local subdivision edits, massing responses to parameter changes, and batch generation under multiple grayscale fields. The results show that local edits reconstructed indexed dependency branches while retaining unaffected regions, planning-control inputs produced corresponding geometric and metric responses, and grayscale fields generated distinct spatial distributions with approximate target matching. FLEXUrban demonstrates how established GUD techniques can be organized around explicit parameter mappings, editable intermediate objects, and inspectable outputs for early-stage morphological prototyping.

**摘要：** 生成式城市设计（generative urban design, GUD）为在复杂约束下探索城市形态配置提供了重要的方法路径。尽管生成技术不断进步，但在多阶段工作流中协调自动化操作与可编辑的中间状态及局部干预仍是一项挑战。本文提出了FLEXUrban——一个基于网络的GUD原型，它在共享工作流中连接了交互式编辑与批量参数赋值。该系统包含三个相互关联的模块：围绕分割线依赖关系组织的张量场引导的层级式地块细分；将规划控制指标映射为建筑几何形体及计算指标的基于类型的参数化体量生成；以及在各区域间分配面积权重、目标容积率（FAR）和可建设面积设定的灰度图像映射。受控的系统级测试考察了局部细分编辑、体量对参数变化的响应以及多重灰度场下的批量生成。结果表明，局部编辑重构了带索引的依赖分支，同时保留了未受影响的区域；规划控制输入产生了相应的几何与指标响应；灰度场生成了各异的空间分布，并近似匹配目标。FLEXUrban展示了如何围绕明确的参数映射、可编辑的中间对象和可检视的输出来组织既有的GUD技术，以服务于早期阶段的形态原型设计。

### [65] AGORA cities: The hidden infrastructure shaping urban fragility

**AGORA城市：塑造城市脆弱性的隐性基础设施**

- 作者：Eleonora Andreotti, Munazza Usmani, Alice Barsanti et al.
- 发表：2026-11-17｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261509701](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261509701)
- 主题：城市空间、社会分异与不平等；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：城市脆弱性、城市服务、兴趣点、绿色基础设施

**Abstract:** Urban services are unevenly distributed across the city, yet it is unclear when their joint presence signals fragility and when it instead outlines a neighbourhood’s vocation. For Bologna and Florence, we integrate Foursquare and OpenStreetMap points of interest with Sentinel-2-derived green indicators based on the 3–30–300 rule, cluster urban areas accordingly, and benchmark the clusters against the demographic, social, economic, and composite fragility indices produced by the municipal statistical offices, which are exogenous to the analysis. In both cities, we identify a subset of infrastructures, termed AGORA (Arts, Green environments, and Open Routes for Access and mobility), whose joint provision is associated with lower fragility. The AGORA Alignment score (AA), based on the rank correlation between AGORA endowment and composite fragility, indicates full alignment in Florence (AA = 1.00) and strong but incomplete alignment in Bologna (AA = 0.80). The remaining service categories instead show wide and heterogeneous distributions: they differentiate areas without tracking fragility and can be interpreted as expressions of urban vocations. The least fragile cluster, the only one with full AGORA endowment, hosts just 3.3% of residents in Bologna and 6.3% in Florence. The framework offers a procedure transferable to other contexts and identifies the expansion of AGORA infrastructures as a candidate lever for reducing fragility without altering existing vocations.

**摘要：** 城市服务在城市中分布不均，但尚不清楚这些服务的共同存在何时意味着脆弱性，何时又勾勒出一个街区的功能定位（vocation）。针对博洛尼亚和佛罗伦萨，我们整合了Foursquare和OpenStreetMap兴趣点数据以及基于“3–30–300规则”的Sentinel-2绿色指标，据此对城市区域进行聚类，并将聚类结果与市政统计部门编制的人口、社会、经济及综合脆弱性指数（这些指数对本分析而言是外生的）进行对照。在两座城市中，我们识别出一组基础设施，称之为AGORA（艺术、绿色环境以及通达与出行的开放路径，Arts, Green environments, and Open Routes for Access and mobility），其共同供给与较低的脆弱性相关。基于AGORA禀赋与综合脆弱性之间秩相关的AGORA一致性得分（AA）表明，佛罗伦萨完全一致（AA = 1.00），博洛尼亚则高度但不完全一致（AA = 0.80）。其余服务类别则呈现出广泛且异质的分布：它们区分了不同区域，却不追踪脆弱性，可被解读为城市功能定位的体现。脆弱性最低的聚类——也是唯一具备完整AGORA禀赋的聚类——在博洛尼亚仅容纳3.3%的居民，在佛罗伦萨仅为6.3%。该框架提供了一套可迁移至其他情境的程序，并将扩展AGORA基础设施确定为在不改变既有功能定位的前提下降低脆弱性的潜在杠杆。

### [66] When does post-demolition vacancy become persistent, and how much vacant land remains? Nationwide evidence from South Korea

**拆除后空置何时变得持久，又有多少空置土地留存？来自韩国全国的证据**

- 作者：Sunjae Lee, Minju Shin, Bumjoon Kang
- 发表：2026-11-14｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261507758](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261507758)
- 主题：城市空间、社会分异与不平等；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：空置土地、拆除、生存分析、韩国

**Abstract:** Vacant-land inventories show where parcels remain vacant, but not how long they have remained without built reuse after demolition. Using nationwide administrative histories of 435,702 demolished parcels in South Korea (2005–2024), we link demolition dates to subsequent occupancy approvals and estimate Kaplan–Meier still-vacant curves. We identify when these curves enter a sustained period of slow decline, yielding two measures: stabilization time (ST), when the slowdown begins, and stabilization survival (SS), the share remaining without registered built reuse at that point. Under the main three-year persistence criterion, the pooled national curve reaches this phase 1055 days after demolition, with an estimated still-vacant share of 56.4%. Among parcels without observed registered built reuse at the end of 2024, 29% had elapsed durations shorter than this benchmark. Across 1148 neighborhoods, similar ST values can correspond to different SS values, and vice versa. Neighborhood and municipal conditions are more systematically associated with SS than with ST. These findings show that the timing of slowing reuse and the amount of vacancy remaining capture different aspects of post-demolition change.

**摘要：** 空置土地清单能显示哪些地块仍处于空置状态，但无法显示它们在拆除后多长时间内未经建设再利用。本文利用韩国全国435,702个已拆除地块（2005–2024年）的行政历史记录，将拆除日期与后续使用批准相关联，并估计Kaplan–Meier仍空置曲线。我们识别这些曲线何时进入持续的缓慢下降阶段，由此得出两个测度：稳定时间（stabilization time, ST），即放缓开始的时间；以及稳定存续率（stabilization survival, SS），即此时仍无登记建设再利用的地块比例。在主要的三年持续性标准下，全国汇总曲线在拆除后1055天进入该阶段，估计的仍空置比例为56.4%。在截至2024年底仍未观测到登记建设再利用的地块中，有29%的已历经时长短于这一基准。在1148个街区中，相似的ST值可能对应不同的SS值，反之亦然。街区和市级条件与SS的关联比与ST的关联更具系统性。这些发现表明，再利用放缓的时机与剩余空置的数量反映了拆除后变化的不同方面。

### [67] Predicting economies of scale in Brazilian municipal expenditure: Panel econometrics and interpretable machine learning

**巴西市政支出规模经济的预测：面板计量经济学与可解释机器学习**

- 作者：Khaled Toffaha, Luan V. Bernardelli, Michael A. Kortt et al.
- 发表：2026-11-07｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261502609](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261502609)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：规模经济、市政支出、可解释机器学习、巴西

**Abstract:** Municipal size is a persistent question in urban planning and public finance because small jurisdictions may face high per capita costs, whereas larger jurisdictions may face congestion and administrative complexity. This study compares panel econometric and interpretable machine-learning models for real municipal operational expenditure per capita in Brazil. The analysis uses 49,228 municipality-year observations for 5,568 municipalities from 2013 to 2021 and evaluates prediction through municipality-holdout, temporal-holdout, and state-block designs. Histogram Gradient Boosting performs best for unseen municipalities and held-out states, while a municipality fixed-effects-style predictor performs best for later-year prediction of municipalities already observed in the panel. The results indicate strong economies of scale among small municipalities followed by a broad low-cost range rather than one universal optimum. Controlled econometric and spatial-error models imply minima near 143,000 to 176,000 residents, while a reduced-form pooled OLS model excluding grants implies about 232,000 residents. The strongest boosting models show no clear interior upturn within the evaluated grid, with predicted expenditure remaining within 10% of the minimum above approximately 48,000 to 53,000 residents. Grouped SHAP results rank population first, followed by grants and local economic capacity. Residual Moran tests show that unexplained expenditure differences remain spatially clustered. The findings support spatially informed expenditure benchmarking and caution against consolidation policies based on one fixed population threshold.

**摘要：** 市政规模是城市规划和公共财政中一个长期存在的问题，因为小型辖区可能面临较高的人均成本，而大型辖区则可能面临拥挤和行政复杂性。本研究比较了面板计量经济模型与可解释机器学习模型对巴西实际人均市政运营支出的预测表现。分析使用了2013年至2021年5,568个市镇的49,228个市镇-年份观测值，并通过市镇留出、时间留出和州区块三种设计评估预测效果。直方图梯度提升（Histogram Gradient Boosting）在预测未见市镇和留出州时表现最佳，而市镇固定效应式预测器在预测面板中已观测市镇的后续年份时表现最佳。结果表明，小型市镇之间存在显著的规模经济，随后是一个宽泛的低成本区间，而非单一的普遍最优点。控制变量的计量经济模型和空间误差模型意味着最小值位于约143,000至176,000居民之间，而排除转移支付的简约式混合OLS模型则意味着约232,000居民。表现最强的提升模型在所评估的网格内未显示出明显的内部回升，在约48,000至53,000居民以上，预测支出始终保持在最小值的10%以内。分组SHAP结果显示，人口位列第一，其次是转移支付和地方经济能力。残差Moran检验表明，未被解释的支出差异仍呈空间集聚。研究结果支持具有空间意识的支出基准设定，并警示不应基于单一固定人口阈值制定合并政策。

### [68] Quantifying uncertainty in deep learning models for urban safety perception: An MC Dropout approach

**城市安全感知深度学习模型的不确定性量化：一种MC Dropout方法**

- 作者：Vicente Larraín, Billy Peralta, Hans Lobel
- 发表：2026-11-05｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261504070](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261504070)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：城市安全感知、不确定性量化、MC Dropout、深度学习

**Abstract:** Deep learning models have revolutionized the automated analysis of urban perception. However, these models typically provide point predictions without associated uncertainty estimates, limiting their application in critical urban planning and policy decisions. This research explores the implementation of Monte Carlo Dropout (MC Dropout) as a method for quantifying uncertainty in deep learning models for urban safety perception. Using ResNet-based convolutional neural networks, we evaluated this approach on both global urban datasets (PlacePulse 2.0) and a specific case study of Santiago, Chile, focusing specifically on the safety dimension of urban perception. Our findings reveal that MC Dropout yields per-image uncertainty estimates without requiring significant architectural modifications, alongside a modest improvement in predictive accuracy (from 66.81% to 67.64%). We show that these estimates vary systematically with urban characteristics, with distinct patterns observed between different urban districts, and that the MC Dropout spread is systematically narrower than the discrepancy between the stochastic and deterministic predictions. We are explicit about what the study does not establish: none of our analyses tests the uncertainty estimates against predictive error, so we do not claim that they are calibrated or that they rank predictions by reliability, and we specify in detail the risk–coverage protocol and pairwise proper scoring rule that such a validation would require. The potential implications for urban analytics—triage of cases for human verification, mapping the spatial distribution of predictive dispersion within cities, and improved interpretability of model decision-making—are presented as prospective applications conditional on that validation. The methodological framework established here can be adopted and extended for future studies and practical applications in urban environments.

**摘要：** 深度学习模型彻底改变了城市感知的自动化分析。然而，这些模型通常只提供点预测而不附带不确定性估计，限制了其在关键城市规划和政策决策中的应用。本研究探讨了将蒙特卡洛随机失活（Monte Carlo Dropout, MC Dropout）作为量化城市安全感知深度学习模型不确定性的方法。我们使用基于ResNet的卷积神经网络，在全球城市数据集（PlacePulse 2.0）以及智利圣地亚哥的具体案例中评估了该方法，并特别聚焦于城市感知的安全维度。研究发现，MC Dropout无需对架构进行重大修改即可得出逐图像的不确定性估计，同时预测准确率略有提升（从66.81%提高至67.64%）。我们表明，这些估计值随城市特征系统性变化，不同城区之间呈现出不同模式，且MC Dropout的离散程度系统性地小于随机预测与确定性预测之间的差异。我们明确说明本研究未能证实的内容：我们的分析均未以预测误差检验不确定性估计，因此我们并不声称这些估计是经过校准的，或能够按可靠性对预测进行排序；我们还详细说明了此类验证所需的风险–覆盖率协议和成对适当评分规则。其对城市分析的潜在意义——对需人工核验的案例进行分诊、绘制城市内部预测离散度的空间分布图，以及提升模型决策的可解释性——均作为以上述验证为前提的前瞻性应用提出。本文建立的方法框架可供未来研究和城市环境中的实际应用采纳与拓展。

### [69] Urban form typologies in Seoul since 1960: A framework for block-level classification and their changing prevalence over time

**1960年以来首尔的城市形态类型：街区尺度分类框架及其流行度的历时变化**

- 作者：Junhyeong Shim, Bumjoon Kang
- 发表：2026-11-02｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261502967](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261502967)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：城市形态类型学、街区、高斯混合模型、首尔

**Abstract:** This study proposes a framework for classifying urban form typologies at the block level and examining changes in their prevalence in Seoul since 1960. The study identifies major typologies aligned with urban planning trends and analyzes their morphological characteristics. Key questions addressed include: how many distinct urban form types can be identified in Seoul; what unique features define each type; and how changes in the prevalence of these types over time reflect broader urban development trends. The analytical procedure consisted of three steps. First, block units were defined based on infrastructure boundaries such as arterial roads. Second, eight indicators were established to capture the morphological features of blocks, parcels, streets, and buildings—the fundamental components of urban structure. Third, Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) clustering was applied to classify blocks with similar numerical indicators into five distinct types. The result effectively captures how many morphological features aligned with Seoul’s urban planning objectives by era. Compared with previous typologies, the study integrates multiple scales of urban form and examines changes in typology prevalence across different periods, providing a basis for interpreting the historical process of urbanization.

**摘要：** 本研究提出了一个在街区尺度上对城市形态类型进行分类的框架，并考察了1960年以来首尔各类型流行度的变化。研究识别出与城市规划趋势相契合的主要类型，并分析其形态特征。所探讨的关键问题包括：首尔可识别出多少种不同的城市形态类型；每种类型具有哪些独特特征；以及这些类型流行度的历时变化如何反映更宏观的城市发展趋势。分析过程包括三个步骤。首先，依据主干道等基础设施边界界定街区单元。其次，建立八项指标，以刻画街区、地块、街道和建筑——城市结构的基本组成部分——的形态特征。第三，应用高斯混合模型（Gaussian Mixture Model, GMM）聚类，将数值指标相近的街区划分为五种不同类型。结果有效捕捉了各时期与首尔城市规划目标相契合的形态特征。与以往的类型学相比，本研究整合了城市形态的多个尺度，并考察了不同时期类型流行度的变化，为解读城市化的历史进程提供了基础。

### [70] Generative AI for urban planning: Designing streetscapes from street-view imagery using diffusion models

**面向城市规划的生成式人工智能：基于扩散模型从街景图像设计街道景观**

- 作者：Yuzhou Chen, Yuebing Liang, Lingqian Hu et al.
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261500883](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500883)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：生成式人工智能、扩散模型、街景图像、街道景观设计

**Abstract:** Street-view imagery (SVI) is widely used to quantify key indicators of urban environment, such as greenery, sky, or road view indices. However, relatively few studies have examined controlled SVI synthesis that combines explicit target proportions of trees, sky, buildings, and roads with road-layout masks, while also testing conflicts between textual and spatial controls. To address this gap, we propose a generative multimodal AI framework that synthesizes alternative streetscapes conditioned on targeted visual metrics, enabling direct visual exploration of urban scenarios. We first construct a multimodal dataset that aligns SVIs with textual descriptions, segmentation maps, road masks, and quantitative metrics of visual elements in Chicago and Orlando. Using this dataset, we demonstrate that diffusion models can produce realistic and semantically consistent streetscape imagery while responding to both textual and imagery controls. Our quantitative evaluations show that incorporating visual controls can improve semantic consistency, reducing the LPIPS index by approximately 6% while maintaining global visual realism. In addition, overall semantic consistency increases by 23.7% in Orlando and 46.4% in Chicago, as measured by the mIoU index, with class-wise gains exceeding 100% improvement for building view indices. Streetscape generation shows directional and interpretable responses to both visual and textual prompts, and when textual and visual controls conflict, imagery controls tend to dominate, indicating a clear control hierarchy and the importance of further developing visual controls for urban scene generation. Overall, this work establishes an important benchmark for streetscape generation using SVIs and diffusion models, and illustrates how generative AI can support controllable and exploratory urban scenario visualization.

**摘要：** 街景图像（street-view imagery, SVI）被广泛用于量化城市环境的关键指标，如绿视率、天空可视率或道路可视指数。然而，鲜有研究考察将树木、天空、建筑和道路的明确目标比例与道路布局掩膜相结合的受控SVI合成，同时检验文本控制与空间控制之间的冲突。为填补这一空白，我们提出了一个生成式多模态人工智能框架，以目标视觉指标为条件合成替代性街道景观，从而实现对城市情景的直接视觉探索。我们首先构建了一个多模态数据集，将芝加哥和奥兰多的SVI与文本描述、语义分割图、道路掩膜以及视觉要素的定量指标对齐。基于该数据集，我们证明扩散模型能够生成逼真且语义一致的街道景观图像，并对文本控制和图像控制均有响应。定量评估表明，引入视觉控制可提升语义一致性，使LPIPS指数降低约6%，同时保持全局视觉真实感。此外，以mIoU指数衡量，整体语义一致性在奥兰多提升了23.7%，在芝加哥提升了46.4%，其中建筑可视指数的分类别提升超过100%。街道景观生成对视觉提示和文本提示均表现出有方向性且可解释的响应；当文本控制与视觉控制发生冲突时，图像控制往往占主导地位，这表明存在明确的控制层级，也说明进一步发展用于城市场景生成的视觉控制十分重要。总体而言，本研究为利用SVI和扩散模型生成街道景观建立了重要基准，并展示了生成式人工智能如何支持可控的、探索性的城市情景可视化。

### [71] What defines the linear city? Examining accessibility premises with novel flow-based linearity indices

**何为线性城市？基于新型流量线性指数检验可达性前提**

- 作者：Yongki Kim
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261500494](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500494)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法；健康、移动性与可达性｜关键词：线性城市、空间句法、可达性、道路网络

**Abstract:** This study re-examines the widely held assumption that the linear city model enhances both efficiency and equity of accessibility. From a space syntax perspective, we introduce two flow-based linearity indices: the Morphological Linearity Index, which captures how strongly path-implied trips align along a single axis, and the Structural Linearity Index, which captures how strongly paths converge on shared street segments. We then relate these indices to accessibility measures that are likewise derived from network configuration alone. Applying these indices to 60 South Korean cities, we find that corridor-centered structures alone do not guarantee efficient or equitable accessibility. Networks with higher morphological linearity show lower mean detour rates yet no corresponding reduction in average trip distance, a directness-distance trade-off that can offset net efficiency gains. Higher structural linearity is associated with both greater detour rates and longer trip distances, suggesting that corridor-centered designs can undermine overall accessibility. By translating the “linear city” into measurable indices, this study provides an operational basis for evidence-based road network planning and assessment.

**摘要：** 本研究重新审视了一个广为接受的假设，即线性城市模型能够同时提升可达性的效率与公平。我们从空间句法（space syntax）视角出发，提出了两个基于流量的线性指数：形态线性指数（Morphological Linearity Index），用于刻画路径隐含的出行沿单一轴线排列的程度；以及结构线性指数（Structural Linearity Index），用于刻画路径在共享街道段上汇聚的程度。随后，我们将这些指数与同样仅从网络构型推导的可达性测度相关联。将这些指数应用于韩国60座城市后，我们发现仅靠以走廊为中心的结构并不能保证高效或公平的可达性。形态线性较高的网络平均绕行率较低，但平均出行距离并未相应缩短，这种直达性与距离之间的权衡可能抵消净效率收益。结构线性较高则与更高的绕行率和更长的出行距离均相关，表明以走廊为中心的设计可能削弱整体可达性。通过将“线性城市”转化为可测度的指数，本研究为基于证据的道路网络规划与评估提供了可操作的依据。

### [72] Assessing the association between street view–derived perceived liveliness and street thefts

**街景感知活力与街头盗窃之间的关联评估**

- 作者：Hanlin Zhou, Lin Liu, Minxuan Lan et al.
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261502943](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261502943)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：犯罪地理、感知活力、街景图像、街头盗窃

**Abstract:** Crime geographers are attentive to how the spatial characteristics of the environment affect criminal behaviors. Recently, scholars have found that aside from physical and social attributes, the perception of the environment, such as safety perception, is also associated with crime occurrence. However, perceived liveliness, a visual perceptual proxy for potential routine activity settings that are closely linked to routine activity theory and crime pattern theory, is rarely explored in prior research. This study examines the association between perceived liveliness and street thefts in the City of Cincinnati, the United States. The liveliness perception scores are derived from street view imagery using a support vector machine model trained on the MIT Place Pulse V2 crowdsourcing image survey. Negative binomial regression models are used to test the relationships between perceived liveliness and street theft, with social and physical environmental attributes controlled. Results show that perceived liveliness significantly improves model performance and is positively associated with street theft. This study provides the first evidence that perceived liveliness helps explain crimes. The positive relationship between perceived liveliness of the built environment and crime adds a new dimension to the routine activity theory and the crime pattern theory. It also offers a scalable and cost-effective measure that can assist urban planners and law enforcement in identifying potential theft hotspots using readily available street view imagery.

**摘要：** 犯罪地理学者关注环境的空间特征如何影响犯罪行为。近来，学者们发现，除物质和社会属性外，对环境的感知（如安全感知）也与犯罪发生相关。然而，感知活力（perceived liveliness）——作为潜在日常活动场所的视觉感知代理变量，与日常活动理论和犯罪模式理论密切相关——在既有研究中鲜有探讨。本研究考察了美国辛辛那提市感知活力与街头盗窃之间的关联。活力感知得分通过支持向量机模型从街景图像中获得，该模型基于MIT Place Pulse V2众包图像调查数据训练。研究采用负二项回归模型，在控制社会和物质环境属性的条件下检验感知活力与街头盗窃之间的关系。结果表明，感知活力显著提升了模型表现，且与街头盗窃呈正相关。本研究首次提供了感知活力有助于解释犯罪的证据。建成环境感知活力与犯罪之间的正向关系为日常活动理论和犯罪模式理论增添了新的维度。研究还提供了一种可扩展且具成本效益的测度方法，可帮助城市规划者和执法部门利用现成的街景图像识别潜在的盗窃热点。

### [73] Exploratory geoplay in metaverse fieldwork: Avatar movement as learning data for geographic understanding

**元宇宙野外考察中的探索式地理游戏：作为地理理解学习数据的化身移动**

- 作者：Kotaro Iizuka, Hiroyuki Yamauchi, Takuro Ogura
- 发表：2026-10-26｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261501338](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261501338)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：地理游戏、元宇宙、虚拟野外考察、化身轨迹

**Abstract:** Geogames are increasingly recognized as playful systems based on real-world geographic data and spatial reasoning, yet open-ended geographic play remains underexplored. This study examines immersive virtual fieldwork as exploratory geoplay, where participants engage with geographic environments through autonomous observation, movement, and interaction rather than scores, competition, or fixed missions. We developed a multiplayer metaverse environment based on a life-scale 3D reconstruction of the Byobugaura Sea Cliff in Japan. Sixty-two participants explored it in two rounds: one unguided and one supported by light interpretive prompts. Avatar positions were recorded using the YAIBA behavior-logging system and analyzed through space-time clustering, PCA, trajectory-grid aggregation, and cluster-duration analysis. During unguided exploration, participants clustered around geomorphologically significant areas, while interpretive supports shifted visits toward breakwater and terrace zones. Cluster durations increased in the smaller sub-instance but remained stable in the larger main instance. Questionnaire responses reflected positive experiences and perceived behavioral changes. By framing avatar movement as learning data, while recognizing that movement does not directly measure knowledge acquisition, this study proposes exploratory geoplay as a model for studying locational attention and geographic inquiry within immersive environments.

**摘要：** 地理游戏（geogames）日益被视为基于真实世界地理数据和空间推理的游戏化系统，但开放式的地理游戏仍未得到充分探讨。本研究将沉浸式虚拟野外考察视为探索式地理游戏（exploratory geoplay），参与者通过自主观察、移动和互动——而非得分、竞争或固定任务——与地理环境进行互动。我们基于日本屏风浦海崖的真实比例三维重建，开发了一个多人元宇宙环境。62名参与者分两轮进行探索：一轮无引导，另一轮辅以轻量的解说提示。研究使用YAIBA行为记录系统记录化身位置，并通过时空聚类、主成分分析（PCA）、轨迹网格聚合以及聚类持续时间分析进行分析。在无引导探索中，参与者聚集于地貌意义显著的区域，而解说支持则使访问转向防波堤和阶地区域。聚类持续时间在较小的子实例中增加，在较大的主实例中则保持稳定。问卷回答反映出积极的体验以及感知到的行为变化。本研究将化身移动视为学习数据，同时承认移动并不能直接测度知识获取，进而提出探索式地理游戏作为研究沉浸式环境中区位注意与地理探究的一种模式。

### [74] A national dataset of downtown business district boundaries for pre-automobile U.S. cities and towns

**美国前汽车时代城镇市中心商业区边界的全国数据集**

- 作者：Andrew J. Van Leuven
- 发表：2026-10-26｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261500874](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500874)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法；区域发展、创新与演化经济地理｜关键词：市中心商业区、开放数据、核密度估计、美国小城镇

**Abstract:** The downtown business district is a fundamental unit of analysis in urban revitalization research, yet no nationally consistent, publicly available dataset of downtown boundaries exists for small and mid-sized U.S. cities and towns. This paper introduces an open data product containing 6,551 downtown polygons for pre-automobile-era places spanning 47 contiguous states. The dataset is constructed from a fully reproducible, open-source R workflow: a national universe of historically significant places is assembled using historical census records, contemporary population data, and metropolitan classification criteria; points-of-interest data from the Overture Maps Foundation are collected within each place boundary; and a kernel density estimation algorithm identifies the highest-concentration commercial cluster as the downtown core. The resulting boundaries are validated against Main Street program districts, which the polygons cover at high rates in the two states examined. The polygons are deposited at Harvard Dataverse under a CC0 1.0 license, with all R code available on GitHub. The dataset supports research on downtown revitalization, Main Street commercial activity, and the economic geography of small and mid-sized American cities.

**摘要：** 市中心商业区是城市复兴研究中的基本分析单元，但目前尚无针对美国中小城镇的、全国一致且公开可得的市中心边界数据集。本文介绍了一个开放数据产品，包含覆盖本土47个州的前汽车时代地方的6,551个市中心多边形。该数据集通过完全可复现的开源R工作流构建：利用历史人口普查记录、当代人口数据和都市区分类标准汇编出全国范围内具有历史意义的地方总体；在各地方边界内收集Overture Maps基金会的兴趣点数据；并通过核密度估计算法将商业集聚度最高的聚类识别为市中心核心。所得边界以“主街”（Main Street）计划街区为参照进行验证，在所考察的两个州中，多边形对这些街区的覆盖率很高。多边形以CC0 1.0许可存放于哈佛Dataverse，全部R代码可在GitHub上获取。该数据集可支持有关市中心复兴、主街商业活动以及美国中小城市经济地理的研究。

### [75] Optimising resource management in data-scarce environments: A modelling approach for Greater Cairo

**数据稀缺环境下的资源管理优化：大开罗的建模方法**

- 作者：Eman Abou Ziyan, Richard Kingston, Joe Ravetz
- 发表：2026-10-22｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23998083261500938](https://doi.org/10.1177/23998083261500938)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：资源管理、空间微观模拟、多尺度地理加权回归、大开罗

**Abstract:** Pressing environmental challenges present significant obstacles to cities’ sustainable development and underscore the need for effective resource management. However, resource management studies often overlook or oversimplify the spatial and social dimensions of resource use, limiting their ability to target interventions to specific population groups and integrate them into urban planning. Addressing this requires disaggregated data that capture spatial variation and interdependencies between socio-economic factors and resource consumption. Yet such data are often scarce in Global South contexts, where data limitations frequently hinder meaningful resource management research. This research develops a hybrid multi-scale analysis framework that integrates Spatial Microsimulation, Multi-Scale Geographically Weighted Regression (MGWR), and Geodemographic Classification to help researchers address data limitations and generate policy-relevant insights. The framework offers three contributions: methodological, empirical, and policy translation. The research provides the first systematic, household-level analysis of food-water-energy consumption patterns across Greater Cairo. Spatial microsimulation creates robust synthetic household datasets by integrating multiple surveys, while MGWR reveals spatially varying drivers of consumption. The geodemographic classification delivers the first empirically grounded typology of Greater Cairo households based on demographic, socio-economic, and consumption characteristics. Lastly, the applied probability-based allocation of clusters to neighbourhoods offers an indicative tool for exploring differentiated tariff structures and area-specific demand management, validated through expert workshops. By addressing Greater Cairo’s data limitations, the framework offers a replicable approach for other cities facing similar constraints, supporting the transition to evidence-based, spatially differentiated resource management in the Global South.

**摘要：** 紧迫的环境挑战对城市可持续发展构成重大障碍，凸显了有效资源管理的必要性。然而，资源管理研究常常忽视或过度简化资源利用的空间和社会维度，限制了其针对特定人群定向干预并将干预纳入城市规划的能力。解决这一问题需要能够捕捉空间差异以及社会经济因素与资源消费之间相互依存关系的分解数据。然而，此类数据在全球南方情境中往往十分稀缺，数据局限常常阻碍有意义的资源管理研究。本研究构建了一个混合多尺度分析框架，整合空间微观模拟（Spatial Microsimulation）、多尺度地理加权回归（Multi-Scale Geographically Weighted Regression, MGWR）和地理人口分类（Geodemographic Classification），以帮助研究者应对数据局限并产出与政策相关的洞见。该框架提供了方法、实证和政策转化三方面的贡献。本研究首次对大开罗地区的食物–水–能源消费模式进行了系统的家庭层面分析。空间微观模拟通过整合多项调查构建出稳健的合成家庭数据集，MGWR则揭示了消费驱动因素的空间差异。地理人口分类首次基于人口、社会经济和消费特征，提出了具有实证基础的大开罗家庭类型学。最后，所应用的基于概率的聚类到街区分配方法，为探索差异化收费结构和分区需求管理提供了一种指示性工具，并通过专家研讨会得到验证。通过应对大开罗的数据局限，该框架为面临类似约束的其他城市提供了可复制的方法，支持全球南方向基于证据、空间差异化的资源管理转型。

## Environment and Planning C: Politics and Space

*本期收录：9 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [76] Surveilled bodies, normalized violence: Migrant women’s everyday experiences with digital-material surveillance in Singapore

**被监视的身体，常态化的暴力：新加坡移民女性日常遭遇的数字—物质监视**

- 作者：Junjia Ye, Kelly Merie Portscher
- 发表：2026-11-17｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261505464](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261505464)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：数字监视、移民女性、性别化不稳定性、新加坡

**Abstract:** This paper examines how digital monitoring operates alongside face-to-face surveillance across socio-material-digital spaces, as contemporary migration to Singapore intersects with intensifying digital surveillance. Focusing on two non-elite, working-class groups of migrant women – migrant domestic workers and marriage migrants married to Singaporean men – we illustrate how entangled digital and non-digital surveillance practices produce gendered precarity through the mundane violence of everyday monitoring in both public and private spheres. We draw on qualitative interview and ethnographic data to demonstrate how surveillance reconfigures gendered boundaries of care and control while normalizing everyday violence against migrant bodies. Crucially, we reveal the ambivalent nature of surveillance as both a mechanism of control and a domain of migrant agency. Women experience and comply with surveillance to be seen as “good migrants,” while simultaneously engaging in lateral surveillance and reverse surveillance as tactics of negotiation and resistance. Ultimately, we argue that the normalization of hybrid surveillance conducts political work within Singapore’s constrained public spheres, producing working-class migrant women as active surveillance subjects whose sense of belonging remains deeply conditional.

**摘要：** 随着当代向新加坡的移民与日益强化的数字监视相交织，本文考察数字监控如何与面对面监视在社会—物质—数字空间中协同运作。聚焦两类非精英的工人阶级移民女性群体——外籍家政工人与嫁给新加坡男性的婚姻移民——我们阐明相互缠绕的数字与非数字监视实践如何通过公共与私人领域中日常监控的平常暴力，生产出性别化的不稳定性（precarity）。我们借助定性访谈与民族志数据，展示监视如何重构照护与控制的性别边界，同时使针对移民身体的日常暴力常态化。关键在于，我们揭示了监视的双重性质：它既是控制机制，也是移民能动性的场域。女性体验并服从监视，以被视为“好移民”，同时又将横向监视与反向监视作为协商与抵抗的策略。最终，我们认为混合监视的常态化在新加坡受限的公共领域中发挥着政治作用，将工人阶级移民女性塑造为积极的监视主体，而她们的归属感仍然深受条件限制。

### [77] A climate of countering extremism? Exploring the enrolment of climate activism into UK counter-extremism policy discourses

**反极端主义的气候？探讨气候行动主义被纳入英国反极端主义政策话语**

- 作者：Andrew Telford
- 发表：2026-11-14｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261507567](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261507567)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：反极端主义、气候行动主义、政治技术、话语—历史分析

**Abstract:** Climate activists are increasingly branded as ‘extremists’, ‘terrorists’, and ‘zealots’ by political actors hostile to decarbonisation, including the enrolment of climate activism into preventing/countering violent extremism (P/CVE) policies. Drawing inspiration from political geographic theorisations of territory ( Elden, 2010 ) and security ( Amoore, 2013 ), I conceptualise (counter-)extremism as a political technology which partially functions through the construction of spectrums (calculative discursive tools which take the form of an ‘array’ of possible positions). The paper draws on a discourse-historical analysis (DHA) and semi-structured interviews with activists based in the UK. I contend that two spectrums are constructed, each of which works according to a spatial logic: the first is an ideological spectrum, which positions climate activists as part of an extreme ‘far left’; and second is a spectrum of political practices which proximises physical violence to climate activists. I conclude with reflections on the implications of counter-extremism for climate activism, particularly in the context of the increasing repression of climate (and other) activists worldwide.

**摘要：** 敌视脱碳的政治行动者日益将气候活动人士冠以“极端分子”“恐怖分子”和“狂热分子”之名，包括将气候行动主义纳入预防/打击暴力极端主义（P/CVE）政策。受政治地理学关于领土（Elden, 2010）和安全（Amoore, 2013）的理论化启发，我将（反）极端主义概念化为一种政治技术，其部分运作方式是构建谱系（spectrums）——以可能立场“阵列”形式呈现的计算性话语工具。本文借助话语—历史分析（DHA）以及对英国活动人士的半结构式访谈。我认为，有两种谱系被构建出来，且各自依循一种空间逻辑：第一种是意识形态谱系，将气候活动人士定位为极端“极左”的一部分；第二种是政治实践谱系，使身体暴力与气候活动人士相邻近。最后，本文反思反极端主义对气候行动主义的影响，尤其是在全球范围内对气候（及其他）活动人士压制日益加剧的背景下。

### [78] Branding cities or citizens? Virtue-based branding as a technology of subjectification in Louisville

**品牌化城市还是品牌化市民？路易斯维尔以美德为基础的品牌塑造作为主体化技术**

- 作者：Ekramul Islam
- 发表：2026-11-12｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261499130](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261499130)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：城市品牌、主体化、治理术、路易斯维尔

**Abstract:** City branding is increasingly understood as shaping not only how cities are seen but who their residents become. This paper contributes to the growing scholarship that conceptualizes place branding as a technology of subjectification, arguing that when a city is branded with a human attribute, it is in effect its citizens who are branded. Drawing on a qualitative case study of Compassionate Louisville, the research traces how compassion was institutionalized as a governing discourse following the city’s formal adoption of the brand identity. The study reveals how the brand narrative operates simultaneously as a moral ideal, a performative expectation, and a political resource used to legitimize policy and manage social contradictions. By framing compassion as an apolitical, universal virtue, the brand establishes a disciplinary framework that encourages self-regulation and moral conformity while depoliticizing systemic social suffering. The paper contributes to debates within political geography and urban studies by demonstrating how virtue-based branding acts as an affective technology of power, reshaping urban subjectivities.

**摘要：** 城市品牌塑造日益被理解为不仅塑造城市如何被看待，也塑造其居民成为什么样的人。本文为将地方品牌化概念化为主体化技术（technology of subjectification）的日益增长的研究作出贡献，认为当一座城市被赋予某种人的属性进行品牌化时，实际上被品牌化的是其市民。基于对“慈悲路易斯维尔”（Compassionate Louisville）的定性案例研究，本研究追溯了在该市正式采纳这一品牌形象后，慈悲如何被制度化为一种治理话语。研究揭示，品牌叙事同时作为道德理想、表演性期望以及用于使政策合法化和管理社会矛盾的政治资源而运作。通过将慈悲建构为一种去政治化的普遍美德，该品牌建立了一个规训框架，鼓励自我规制与道德服从，同时使系统性的社会苦难去政治化。本文通过展示以美德为基础的品牌塑造如何作为一种情感性权力技术重塑城市主体性，为政治地理学与城市研究的讨论作出贡献。

### [79] On the intersection of Black geographies and policy mobilities: Tracing circuits of anti-racism in Newcastle upon Tyne

**论黑人地理学与政策流动性的交汇：追溯纽卡斯尔的反种族主义回路**

- 作者：Astrid Wood
- 发表：2026-11-12｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261502934](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261502934)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：黑人地理学、政策流动性、反种族主义、纽卡斯尔

**Abstract:** This paper reflects theoretically on the intersection of Black geographies and policy mobilities by tracing the historical and contemporary movements of Black activists, intellectuals and anti-racist struggles in Newcastle upon Tyne. While Black geographies foregrounds Black spatial imaginaries, resistance and epistemologies rooted in racial capitalism and coloniality, it has insufficiently interrogated how racialized policy formations and their circulations function transjurisdictionally. Likewise, policy mobilities, which charts the movement and mutation of urban policy models across contexts, offers an opportunity to expose the racialized structures underpinning these movements. This paper develops a conceptual approach that examines how race and power circulate through circuits of racialized spatial practices, Black actors and political thought and anti-racist struggles. The paper traces how Black individuals, ideas and infrastructures – routed through historical visits by Martin Luther King Jr., Frederick Douglass and Muhammad Ali as well as through contemporary cultural programs like Freedom City and protest movements like Black Lives Matter – are mobilized within and beyond the urban. In so doing, it advances a global, relational understanding of Black spatial politics that provokes a rethinking of urban theory’s Euro-American biases.

**摘要：** 本文通过追溯泰恩河畔纽卡斯尔（Newcastle upon Tyne）黑人活动人士、知识分子与反种族主义斗争的历史与当代流动，从理论上反思黑人地理学（Black geographies）与政策流动性（policy mobilities）的交汇。黑人地理学凸显植根于种族资本主义与殖民性的黑人空间想象、抵抗与认识论，但对种族化政策形态及其流通如何跨辖区运作的追问尚不充分。同样，追踪城市政策模式跨情境流动与变异的政策流动性研究，为揭示支撑这些流动的种族化结构提供了契机。本文发展出一种概念路径，考察种族与权力如何通过种族化空间实践、黑人行动者与政治思想以及反种族主义斗争的回路而流通。本文追溯黑人个体、观念与基础设施——经由马丁·路德·金、弗雷德里克·道格拉斯与穆罕默德·阿里的历史访问，以及“自由之城”（Freedom City）等当代文化项目和“黑人的命也是命”（Black Lives Matter）等抗议运动——如何在城市内外被动员。由此，本文推进了对黑人空间政治的全球性、关系性理解，促使人们重新思考城市理论的欧美中心偏见。

### [80] The material infrastructure of privatization, dispossession and resistance at the Zambian land frontier

**赞比亚土地边疆上私有化、剥夺与抵抗的物质基础设施**

- 作者：Linus Rosén
- 发表：2026-11-02｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261502933](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261502933)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：土地私有化、剥夺、法律地理学、赞比亚

**Abstract:** The privatization of communal lands continues to be a central tenet of capitalist and settler-colonial appropriations with devastating implications for the dispossessed and land denied. This article traces how dispossession and resistance become bound up in the material infrastructure of privatization – fences, concrete, rebar and survey lines – and the colonial histories sedimented within them. In dialogue with legal-geographical work on property and materiality, I show how such artefacts become the nodes through which law’s abstraction gives way to struggle; once law is materialized in objects, these become susceptible to alteration and enrolment in new lives of legal association that can subvert – and creatively invert – the power trafficked through property. The analysis draws from ethnographic fieldwork in Mulonga village, central Zambia, where a privatization scheme evolved into a localized struggle between creative cultivators who reworked the things of property to resist displacement, and a bureaucracy that struggled to make them perform the political work assigned to them. Abstract property, I conclude, must be read alongside the contingency and resistant reworkings of its material articulation, which creates new legal surfaces upon which people can act, redirect legal signs, and unsettle translations through which private property achieves exclusionary effect.

**摘要：** 公共土地的私有化仍是资本主义与定居者殖民占有的核心原则，对被剥夺者和被拒绝获得土地者造成毁灭性影响。本文追溯剥夺与抵抗如何与私有化的物质基础设施——围栏、混凝土、钢筋和测量线——以及沉积其中的殖民历史缠结在一起。通过与关于财产与物质性的法律地理学研究对话，我展示这些人工物如何成为法律的抽象性让位于斗争的节点：一旦法律被物化于物件之中，这些物件便容易被改造，并被纳入新的法律关联生命之中，从而颠覆乃至创造性地反转通过财产传导的权力。分析基于在赞比亚中部穆隆加（Mulonga）村的民族志田野工作。在那里，一项私有化计划演变为一场地方化的斗争：一方是富于创造力的耕作者，他们改造财产之“物”以抵抗迁离；另一方是官僚机构，它竭力使这些物件履行所赋予的政治功能。本文的结论是，抽象财产必须与其物质表达的偶然性及抵抗性改造结合起来解读，这种物质表达创造出新的法律表面，人们可以在其上行动、改变法律符号的方向，并扰乱私有财产借以实现排他效果的转译过程。

### [81] Saving lives or freeing territory? Sacred values and tragic trade-offs in wartime Ukraine

**拯救生命还是解放领土？战时乌克兰的神圣价值与悲剧性权衡**

- 作者：Gerard Toal, Karina Korostelina, John O’Loughlin
- 发表：2026-11-02｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261499153](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261499153)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：神圣价值、领土、战争、乌克兰

**Abstract:** President Zelensky has asserted: “We do not trade our lands and our people.” Both territorial integrity and human lives are considered ‘sacred values’ in wartime Ukraine, values beyond cost-benefit and exchange calculations. Yet, the human costs of continuing war to retake territory are so great in Ukraine that these sacred values are forced into a tragic trade-off. Using a survey of frontline Ukrainians from 2022 made up of residents and displaced persons equally (n = 1800), this paper shows that how the ‘lives versus territory’ dilemma divided respondents. We examined the degree to which wartime hatred of the Russian leadership and material insecurity explain people’s choices, and the role that personal stress plays as a mediator for these attitudes. The results reveal that respondents with higher levels of stress were more likely to prioritize saving lives over freeing territory. Displaced persons were also more likely to prioritize lives. The respondent’s material insecurity had modest impacts. For those most stressed during wartime, lives come before land.

**摘要：** 泽连斯基总统曾断言：“我们不拿我们的土地和人民做交易。”在战时乌克兰，领土完整与人的生命都被视为“神圣价值”（sacred values），即超越成本—收益与交换计算的价值。然而，在乌克兰为收复领土而持续战争的人员代价如此巨大，以致这些神圣价值被迫陷入悲剧性权衡。本文利用2022年对乌克兰前线民众的调查（居民与流离失所者各半，n = 1800），展示“生命与领土”困境如何使受访者产生分歧。我们考察了战时对俄罗斯领导层的仇恨与物质不安全在多大程度上解释人们的选择，以及个人压力作为这些态度中介变量的作用。结果显示，压力水平较高的受访者更倾向于将拯救生命置于解放领土之上。流离失所者也更倾向于优先考虑生命。受访者的物质不安全影响较为有限。对于战时压力最大的人而言，生命先于土地。

### [82] Walter Benjamin on natural history and place

**瓦尔特·本雅明论自然史与地方**

- 作者：Jordan Daniels
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261494364](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261494364)
- 主题：批判理论、知识生产与学科反思；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：瓦尔特·本雅明、自然史、灾难、地方

**Abstract:** Taking his radio broadcast on the Mississippi flood of 1927 as a point of departure, this article argues that Walter Benjamin has much to contribute to current debates concerning space, nature, and place. Benjamin’s episode on the devastating flood in the American South was his final transmission for Berlin Radio’s Youth Hour in 1932, and it concludes a series of episodes on disasters that are both natural and social at once. This article situates the radio broadcast in relation to two catastrophic ways of seeing: first, in Benjamin’s work on the German mourning play , where the spatializing function of allegory transposes the temporal events of history into a spatial image, and second, in the weakly messianic vision of history as “one single catastrophe.” Drawing on these reorienting modes of vision, I argue that Benjamin’s curation of ancient and recent catastrophes across the world prompts his young listeners to recognize interlinked moments of danger spanning both historical time and global space.

**摘要：** 本文以瓦尔特·本雅明关于1927年密西西比洪水的广播节目为出发点，认为本雅明能够为当前关于空间、自然与地方的讨论作出重要贡献。本雅明关于美国南方毁灭性洪水的这一期节目是他1932年为柏林电台“青少年时间”所做的最后一次播送，为一系列兼具自然与社会属性的灾难节目画上句号。本文将该广播置于两种灾难性观看方式的关系中加以考察：其一是本雅明关于德国悲苦剧（mourning play）的研究，其中寓言的空间化功能将历史的时间性事件转置为空间图像；其二是将历史视为“唯一一场灾难”的弱弥赛亚式历史观。借助这些重新定向的观看模式，我认为本雅明对世界各地古代与近期灾难的编排，促使其年轻听众认识到跨越历史时间与全球空间的相互关联的危险时刻。

### [83] Producing spatial justice: Scalar governance and territorial differentiation in coastal China’s nuclear development

**生产空间正义：中国沿海核电开发中的尺度治理与地域差异化**

- 作者：Xiuyun Yang, Kewei Huang, Qiuping Chen
- 发表：2026-10-28｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261500382](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261500382)
- 主题：能源转型与基础设施；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：空间正义、尺度治理、核电、漳州

**Abstract:** Nuclear infrastructure development highlights an important empirical case in environmental governance: nuclear facilities concentrate environmental and social burdens locally, yet many projects proceed without sustained opposition. In the context of China, this paper argues that such outcomes are produced through county-level scalar governance strategies. Focusing on the Zhangzhou Nuclear Power Project, the study reconstructs how local authorities designed four concentric governance zones (20 km, 10 km, 2 km, 200 m) and calibrated distance-based interventions—compensation and relocation, tiered emergency planning and monitoring, graded communication and participation, and differentiated infrastructure and welfare investments—so that governance intensity corresponded to spatial exposure. Drawing on archival materials, policy documents, and semi-structured interviews with officials and project actors, corroborated by administrative records and implementation milestones, the analysis shows that scalar governance pursued distributive, procedural, and recognition objectives across zones. Importantly, the absence of organized resistance is interpreted not as conflict resolution but as the managed incorporation of grievances into institutional channels within a constrained political environment. By reframing nuclear legitimacy as the outcome of territorially differentiated state action, the paper contributes to debates on territory, scale, and environmental governance and positioning spatial justice as a conditional mechanism for managing uneven infrastructural risk.

**摘要：** 核基础设施开发为环境治理提供了一个重要的经验案例：核设施将环境与社会负担集中于地方，然而许多项目在推进中并未遭遇持续的反对。本文认为，在中国情境下，这一结果是通过县级尺度治理策略生产出来的。聚焦漳州核电项目，本研究重构了地方政府如何设计四个同心治理圈层（20公里、10公里、2公里、200米），并校准基于距离的干预措施——补偿与搬迁、分级应急规划与监测、分级沟通与参与，以及差异化的基础设施与福利投资——使治理强度与空间暴露程度相对应。基于档案资料、政策文件以及对官员和项目相关行动者的半结构式访谈，并辅以行政记录与实施节点的佐证，分析表明尺度治理在不同圈层中追求分配、程序与承认三方面的目标。重要的是，有组织抵抗的缺失并不被解读为冲突的解决，而是在受限政治环境中将诉求有管理地纳入制度渠道。通过将核电合法性重新界定为地域差异化国家行动的结果，本文为领土、尺度与环境治理的讨论作出贡献，并将空间正义定位为管理不均衡基础设施风险的一种有条件机制。

### [84] Contested spatialities of renewable energy expansion—Introduction to the theme issue

**可再生能源扩张的争议性空间——主题专刊导言**

- 作者：Thilo Wiertz, Benjamin Schuetze, Rosa Lehmann et al.
- 发表：2026-10-21｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/23996544261497568](https://doi.org/10.1177/23996544261497568)
- 主题：能源转型与基础设施；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：可再生能源、能源转型、尺度、能源冲突

**Abstract:** Renewable energy increasingly complements fossil fuels in meeting rising global energy demand. Yet its rollout is neither even nor free of conflict. This theme issue foregrounds the spatialities and scales of renewable energy infrastructures and examines the deeply contested nature of energy transition efforts. Drawing on case studies from Morocco, Jordan, Namibia, Mexico, Brazil, Peru and Ecuador, the contributions offer insights into recent regional developments and highlight transregional and cross-scalar interrelations. They connect place-based research on contested energy projects in political ecology, geography and anthropology with nation-focused analyses of energy transition policy, and approach scale as an unfolding field of contestation. In doing so, they challenge notions of a homogeneous “local” in analyses of energy conflict and move beyond methodological nationalism while still recognizing the significance of the national scale. Together, the theme issue demonstrates the value of relational approaches for disentangling and understanding the contested geographies of contemporary renewable energy expansion.

**摘要：** 可再生能源日益与化石燃料相互补充，以满足不断增长的全球能源需求。然而，其推广既不均衡，也并非没有冲突。本主题专刊聚焦可再生能源基础设施的空间性与尺度，考察能源转型努力深刻的争议性。基于来自摩洛哥、约旦、纳米比亚、墨西哥、巴西、秘鲁和厄瓜多尔的案例研究，各篇文章提供了对近期区域发展的洞见，并凸显跨区域与跨尺度的相互关联。它们将政治生态学、地理学和人类学中关于争议性能源项目的地方性研究与以国家为中心的能源转型政策分析相联系，并将尺度视为一个不断展开的争夺场域。由此，它们挑战了能源冲突分析中同质化“地方”的观念，在承认国家尺度重要性的同时超越方法论民族主义。总体而言，本主题专刊展示了关系性方法在梳理和理解当代可再生能源扩张的争议性地理方面的价值。
