### [87] Statistical and Perceived Ethnic Diversity in Urban Activity Spaces: Evidence from Auckland, New Zealand

**城市活动空间中的统计族裔多样性与感知族裔多样性：来自新西兰奥克兰的证据**

- 作者：Olena Hołubowska, Katarzyna Siła-Nowicka, I-Ting Chuang et al.
- 发表：2026-11-04｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2740623](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2740623)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：活动空间、族裔多样性、感知多样性、群体间接触

**Abstract:** The study of segregation in activity spaces has received renewed attention, fueled by the increasing availability of mobility data from mobile phones and social media. These new data sources enable high-resolution analysis of how people move through cities and interact across diverse neighborhoods. A key goal of this work is to inform the design of inclusive spaces that foster intergroup contact, which is presumed to enhance social cohesion. Much of the existing research, however, has focused on statistical diversity (typically measured using data collected from mobile devices), giving less consideration to perceived diversity. This study examines the extent to which these two forms of diversity, statistical and perceived, align. Using aggregated mobility data from approximately 90,000 people in Auckland, New Zealand, we estimated mobility flows and computed the ethnic diversity of visitors across six neighborhoods. In parallel, we conducted 249 on-street surveys in those neighborhoods to capture perceptions of diversity. Our findings reveal only a weak correlation between statistical and perceived diversity, suggesting that individuals’ perceptions do not simply reflect demographic compositions. Instead, perceived diversity is more strongly predicted by self-reported intergroup contact (both within the specific neighborhood and across the city) and by affective factors such as space affinity and attitudes toward outgroup members. These results underscore the importance of integrating multiple data sources to better understand how people perceive the diversity of the spaces they visit.

**摘要：** 随着手机和社交媒体移动数据的日益普及，活动空间中的隔离研究重新受到关注。这些新数据源使人们得以对人们如何在城市中移动以及如何在多样化社区间互动进行高分辨率分析。这类研究的一个关键目标是为设计促进群体间接触的包容性空间提供依据，而群体间接触被认为能够增强社会凝聚力。然而，现有研究大多关注统计多样性（通常以移动设备采集的数据来测度），对感知多样性的关注较少。本研究考察统计多样性与感知多样性这两种多样性形式在多大程度上相互一致。利用新西兰奥克兰约9万人的聚合移动数据，我们估算了移动流，并计算了六个社区访客的族裔多样性。同时，我们在这些社区开展了249份街头问卷调查，以获取人们对多样性的感知。研究发现，统计多样性与感知多样性之间仅存在弱相关，表明个体感知并非简单反映人口构成。相反，感知多样性更强地受到自我报告的群体间接触（包括特定社区内和全市范围内）以及空间亲近感和对外群体成员态度等情感因素的预测。这些结果凸显了整合多种数据源以更好理解人们如何感知其所到访空间之多样性的重要性。

### [88] Electric Youth: Young People, Energy Infrastructures, and Power Cuts

**电力青年：年轻人、能源基础设施与停电**

- 作者：John Horton, Peter Kraftl, José Antonio Perrella Balestieri et al.
- 发表：2026-11-02｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2738593](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2738593)
- 主题：能源转型与基础设施；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：能源地理、停电、青年地理、电力基础设施

**Abstract:** Drawing on an interdisciplinary study that explored how large-scale regional, national, and supranational infrastructural crises are experienced, felt and lived with by young people, this article extends research and practice in energy geographies, sustainability, environmental education, and interdisciplinary childhood and youth studies through three interventions. First, we argue that many normative contemporary forms of energy studies, environmental education, and education for sustainability (EfS) could productively engage with children and young people’s everyday geographies. Second, we highlight the surprising absence of literal, embodied, affecting encounters with electricity and electrical infrastructures in contemporary practice and literature, which is ostensibly about environmental education and energy geographies. Third, we question the limited consideration of energy in interdisciplinary childhood and youth studies, despite the predicative importance of electrical infrastructures and technologies for many young people’s everyday geographies. We advance these themes through qualitative and quantitative data evidencing the prevalence of power cuts in many young people’s lives, and the multiple bodily and material ways in which frequent power cuts are noticed and lived with. We call for major bodies of work in energy studies, environmental education and EfS, and childhood and youth geographies to be brought into more sustained conversation.

**摘要：** 本文基于一项跨学科研究，该研究探讨了年轻人如何经历、感受并应对大规模区域性、国家性和超国家性的基础设施危机。文章通过三项介入，拓展了能源地理学、可持续性、环境教育以及跨学科儿童与青年研究的研究与实践。第一，我们认为许多规范性的当代能源研究、环境教育和可持续发展教育（education for sustainability, EfS）可以富有成效地关注儿童和年轻人的日常地理。第二，我们指出，在表面上关于环境教育和能源地理的当代实践与文献中，令人意外地缺少与电力及电力基础设施的切实、具身和情感性遭遇。第三，尽管电力基础设施和技术对许多年轻人的日常地理具有决定性的重要意义，我们仍对跨学科儿童与青年研究对能源的有限关注提出质疑。我们通过定性和定量数据推进上述主题，这些数据表明停电在许多年轻人生活中普遍存在，以及频繁停电以多种身体和物质方式被察觉和应对。我们呼吁让能源研究、环境教育与可持续发展教育以及儿童与青年地理学的主要研究体系展开更持久的对话。

### [89] Detecting AI-Generated Maps Using Vision-Language Models with Context-Aware Demonstration Retrieval

**基于情境感知示例检索的视觉—语言模型AI生成地图检测**

- 作者：Yifan Zhang, Wenbo Zhang, Zhiyun Wang et al.
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2744266](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2744266)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：AI生成地图、视觉—语言模型、示例检索、地图检测

**Abstract:** Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) generative models enable the rapid creation of map-like images by simply providing textual prompts, raising growing concerns about the authenticity and reliability of geospatial information. Detecting AI-generated maps is challenging because synthetic maps can closely mimic the visual style and cartographic elements of conventionally produced maps, making them difficult to distinguish through visual inspection. Recent progress in vision-language models (VLMs) has demonstrated strong capabilities in multimodal understanding and has been widely applied in various visual analysis tasks, including scene interpretation and map-related applications. Directly applying VLMs to identify AI-generated maps remains difficult, though, as subtle visual cues and misleading visual similarities could lead to unreliable judgments. To address this issue, we propose a framework that leverages VLMs with context-aware demonstration retrieval. Specifically, a reference database containing map samples and their corresponding labels (AI-generated or non-AI-generated) is constructed. For a given query map, a set of contextually consistent examples is retrieved from the database to serve as demonstrations for in-context reasoning. Instead of relying solely on pairwise visual similarity, the proposed retrieval strategy considers relationships among candidate samples, enabling the model to access a broader set of retrieval contexts beyond direct pairwise similarity. Experimental results show that incorporating retrieved demonstrations improves the reliability of AI-generated map detection compared with direct zero-shot inference. In addition, the retrieved examples provide more informative contextual evidence than simple similarity-based retrieval, supporting more robust reasoning.

**摘要：** 人工智能（AI）生成模型的最新进展使人们只需提供文本提示即可快速生成类地图图像，引发了人们对地理空间信息真实性和可靠性日益增长的担忧。检测AI生成地图具有挑战性，因为合成地图能够高度模仿传统制作地图的视觉风格和制图要素，难以通过目视检查加以区分。视觉—语言模型（vision-language models, VLMs）的最新进展已展现出强大的多模态理解能力，并被广泛应用于各种视觉分析任务，包括场景解译和地图相关应用。然而，直接应用VLMs识别AI生成地图仍然困难，因为细微的视觉线索和误导性的视觉相似性可能导致不可靠的判断。为解决这一问题，我们提出一个利用VLMs并结合情境感知示例检索（context-aware demonstration retrieval）的框架。具体而言，我们构建了一个包含地图样本及其对应标签（AI生成或非AI生成）的参考数据库。对于给定的查询地图，从数据库中检索一组情境一致的样例，作为上下文推理的示例。所提出的检索策略并非仅依赖成对视觉相似性，而是考虑候选样本之间的关系，使模型能够获得超越直接成对相似性的更广泛检索情境。实验结果表明，与直接零样本推理相比，引入检索示例提高了AI生成地图检测的可靠性。此外，检索到的样例比简单的基于相似性的检索提供了更具信息量的情境证据，从而支持更稳健的推理。

### [90] Decisions in Context: Toward an Integrative Behavioral Geography

**情境中的决策：迈向整合性行为地理学**

- 作者：Kurt Waldman
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2744264](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2744264)
- 主题：批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：行为地理学、决策、认知、情境

**Abstract:** Although several geographers have lamented the effective demise of behavioral geography as a subdiscipline, advances within geography and across the behavioral sciences have created new opportunities for its renewal. This article revisits theoretical debates that shaped the emergence and subsequent decline of behavioral geography and argues that advances in research on cognition, sociality, and institutional decision-making have since addressed many critiques of the field. Building on this foundation, I propose a broader, more integrative conception of behavioral geography as the study of how decisions emerge from interactions among cognitive, social, and institutional processes operating within behaviorally meaningful contexts. Rather than defining behavioral geography through specific domains or methods, I argue that a new and reinvigorated behavioral geography is better understood as an analytical orientation that offers a uniquely integrative approach to the study of decision-making, equally emphasizing both cognition and context. Given geography’s long-standing contributions to these areas, the discipline is well positioned to make distinctive interdisciplinary contributions to contemporary behavioral research and to further integrate behavioral theory with geographic scholarship.

**摘要：** 尽管一些地理学家曾哀叹行为地理学作为一个分支学科已实际走向衰落，但地理学内部及各行为科学领域的进展为其复兴创造了新的机遇。本文重新审视了塑造行为地理学兴起及其后衰落的理论争论，并认为有关认知、社会性和制度决策研究的进展已回应了对该领域的诸多批评。在此基础上，我提出一种更宽泛、更具整合性的行为地理学构想，即研究决策如何在具有行为意义的情境中，从认知、社会和制度过程的相互作用中产生。我认为，与其通过特定领域或方法来界定行为地理学，不如将一种新的、重焕活力的行为地理学理解为一种分析取向，它为决策研究提供了一种独特的整合性路径，同等重视认知与情境。鉴于地理学在这些领域的长期贡献，该学科有条件为当代行为研究作出独特的跨学科贡献，并进一步将行为理论与地理学研究相融合。

### [91] Participatory Drawing and Geoeconomic Mapping in Paris–La Chapelle: A Conjunctural Approach for Research Beyond Design

**巴黎拉沙佩勒的参与式绘画与地缘经济制图：超越设计的研究之形势分析路径**

- 作者：Rachele Shamouni-Naghde
- 发表：2026-10-26｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2744267](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2744267)
- 主题：批判理论、知识生产与学科反思；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：参与式绘画、形势分析、移民接待、巴黎

**Abstract:** This article critically reflects on research into a daily free food distribution in the inner-city Parisian neighborhood of La Chapelle. Ongoing since 2016, the Solidarity Breakfast presents itself as the legacy of “acts of hospitality” developed in response to the 2015 “migrant crisis,” claiming to welcome migrants by offering them a meal in the park. This article analyzes participatory drawing activities organized during the breakfast for the purposes of the research project, and discusses a selection of the drawings created there. It considers how internalist depictions of the microscale of the everyday make visible the relational space, whereas map-like drawings show a broader perspective in which power relations linked to the wider context of geopolitical and geo-economic entanglements are reiterated and reinscribed. By articulating these perspectives, the article examines how social practices and imaginaries are subsumed by neoliberal regimes. It acknowledges the feminist insight that “the personal is political,” but argues that local social practices do not necessarily produce wider emancipatory impacts, and that participatory research can be used to sustain the routine rather than represent its political relevance. However hospitable the breakfast might be, its relational dimension might perpetuate rather than address injustice. The article concludes that the nature of the knowledge that participatory approaches elicit from the field can be unpredictable, and is therefore key to broader conjunctural analysis, provided that its critical scope is broad enough to question the conditions from which the research itself has emerged.

**摘要：** 本文对巴黎内城拉沙佩勒（La Chapelle）街区每日免费食物发放活动的研究进行批判性反思。“团结早餐”（Solidarity Breakfast）自2016年起持续至今，自我定位为回应2015年“移民危机”而发展起来的“好客行动”的延续，声称通过在公园提供餐食来欢迎移民。本文分析了为研究项目在早餐期间组织的参与式绘画活动，并讨论了其中创作的部分画作。文章考察了对日常微观尺度的内在性描绘如何使关系空间变得可见，而类地图式绘画则展现出更宽广的视角，其中与更广泛地缘政治和地缘经济纠缠相关的权力关系被重申和重新铭刻。通过联结这些视角，本文探讨社会实践与想象如何被新自由主义体制所吸纳。文章承认“个人的即政治的”这一女性主义洞见，但认为地方社会实践未必产生更广泛的解放性影响，参与式研究可能被用于维持日常惯例，而非呈现其政治意义。无论早餐多么好客，其关系维度可能是在延续而非应对不公。本文得出结论：参与式方法从田野中引出的知识性质可能难以预料，因此对于更宏观的形势分析（conjunctural analysis）至关重要，前提是其批判视野足够宽广，能够质疑研究本身得以产生的条件。

### [92] Exploring Sense of Community and Place Identity via Murals: A Photovoice Study in the Anthracite Coal Region of Northeastern Pennsylvania, USA

**通过壁画探索社区感与地方认同：美国宾夕法尼亚州东北部无烟煤产区的影像发声研究**

- 作者：Lisa Murphy, Shamayeta Bhattacharya
- 发表：2026-10-23｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2728721](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2728721)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：影像发声、壁画、地方认同、社区感

**Abstract:** The symbols and imagery painted in public murals play a powerful role in shaping a sense of community and place identity. This study within the anthracite coal region of northeastern Pennsylvania employed a photovoice method in which participants took photographs for critical discussion, analyzed them, and presented findings in an art installation. Participants’ photography of murals and other subjects served as a foundation for a community-engaged exploration of sense of community and place identity, with the overall aim of examining how residents interpret, respond to, and coproduce meaning from the content and themes found within local murals. The creative acts involved in the photovoice process offered an accessible way to engage in a higher form of critical analysis and communicate findings in modes beyond written academic language. The results were presented in the form of an immersive canopy-style art installation. Artists, policymakers, and community members walked beneath woven yarn canopies that showed key themes of social responsibility, holistic well-being, and the history and future of the region resonated the most with participants. The immersive art installation turned research findings into a public conversation, highlighting how intentional knowledge translation could promote community dialogue about sense of community and place identity.

**摘要：** 公共壁画中描绘的符号和图像在塑造社区感和地方认同方面发挥着重要作用。本研究在宾夕法尼亚州东北部无烟煤产区采用影像发声（photovoice）方法，参与者拍摄照片用于批判性讨论，对其进行分析，并以艺术装置的形式呈现研究发现。参与者对壁画及其他主题的拍摄构成了以社区参与方式探索社区感和地方认同的基础，总体目标是考察居民如何解读、回应地方壁画的内容与主题，并从中共同生产意义。影像发声过程中的创造性行为提供了一种易于参与的方式，使人们能够开展更高层次的批判分析，并以超越书面学术语言的方式传达研究发现。研究结果以沉浸式天篷风格艺术装置的形式呈现。艺术家、政策制定者和社区成员在编织毛线天篷下穿行，这些天篷展示了最能引起参与者共鸣的关键主题：社会责任、整体福祉以及该地区的历史与未来。这一沉浸式艺术装置将研究发现转化为公共对话，凸显了有意识的知识转化如何能够促进关于社区感和地方认同的社区对话。

### [93] Homescapes: A Lefebvrian Analysis of Iranian Immigrants’ Residential Behavior in Suburban Toronto

**家园景观：多伦多郊区伊朗移民居住行为的列斐伏尔式分析**

- 作者：Negar Valizadeh, Luisa Veronis
- 发表：2026-10-23｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2740657](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2740657)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；金融化、住房与房地产｜关键词：列斐伏尔空间三元组、移民居住行为、伊朗移民、多伦多

**Abstract:** Research on immigrant residential behavior has tended to be divided between structural approaches emphasizing housing markets, policy, and discrimination, and cultural-experiential approaches foregrounding preferences, networks, and agency. These orientations operate largely in parallel. We propose Lefebvre’s spatial triad (conceived, perceived, and lived space) as an integrative framework that bridges them by foregrounding the process through which residential space is socially produced: the dialectical interplay of institutional logics, everyday practices, and lived meanings. We demonstrate this framework through the case of established Iranian immigrants in suburban Toronto, Canada, drawing on spatial analysis of 2021 census data, interviews with fifty-four Iranian immigrants and seven real estate agents, and ethnographic observation, and examine how residential space is produced at two scales. At the neighborhood scale, safety, social class, and ethnoracial belonging intersect with school catchments, infrastructure, and ethnic networks to produce distinct “Iranian neighborhoods” in the affluent northern suburbs. At the dwelling scale, cultural imperatives—hospitality, privacy, and aberoo—draw families into engagement with mortgage and investment logics. We argue that these multiscalar patterns are best understood as dialectically produced homescapes. Because the framework captures the process of spatial production rather than predicting a particular outcome, it offers a transferable lens for studying how diverse immigrant groups actively produce (sub)urban space.

**摘要：** 关于移民居住行为的研究往往分为两类：强调住房市场、政策和歧视的结构性路径，以及突出偏好、网络和能动性的文化—经验路径。这两种取向在很大程度上平行运作。我们提出以列斐伏尔（Lefebvre）的空间三元组（构想空间、感知空间与生活空间）作为整合性框架，通过突出居住空间被社会生产的过程——即制度逻辑、日常实践与生活意义之间的辩证互动——来弥合二者。我们以加拿大多伦多郊区已定居的伊朗移民为案例展示这一框架，基于2021年人口普查数据的空间分析、对54名伊朗移民和7名房地产经纪人的访谈以及民族志观察，考察居住空间如何在两个尺度上被生产。在社区尺度上，安全、社会阶层和族裔—种族归属与学区、基础设施和族裔网络相互交织，在富裕的北部郊区形成了独特的“伊朗社区”。在住宅尺度上，好客、隐私和体面（aberoo）等文化要求，促使家庭参与到抵押贷款和投资逻辑之中。我们认为，这些多尺度模式最好被理解为辩证生产的家园景观（homescapes）。由于该框架捕捉的是空间生产的过程而非预测特定结果，它为研究不同移民群体如何积极生产（郊区）城市空间提供了可迁移的视角。

### [94] Paradigms of Spatial Statistics as Footnotes to Tobler’s First Law of Geography

**作为托布勒地理学第一定律脚注的空间统计范式**

- 作者：Bin Li, Daniel A. Griffith
- 发表：2026-10-23｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2742353](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2742353)
- 主题：城市分析、GIS与空间方法｜关键词：地理学第一定律、空间统计、地理加权回归、地理空间人工智能

**Abstract:** Tobler’s First Law of Geography (TFL) is foundational to spatial analysis. Responding to Sui’s (Citation2004) invitation to consider whether geography is just a footnote to TFL, we develop a five-axis framework to compare major spatial statistical paradigms. Four axes cover spatiotemporal interconnectedness, distance and nearness, heterogeneity and scale, and temporal dynamics, with a methodological layer on estimation, inference, and learning. We examine five paradigms in depth: geostatistics and Gaussian random fields, lattice and spatial econometrics, Moran eigenvector spatial filtering, geographically weighted regression, and geospatial artificial intelligence and spatial learning. The framework also extends to additional paradigms. This synthesis supports an expanded TFL in which spatial relatedness varies with distance, connectivity, context, scale, and time, and highlights implications for spatial statistical modeling in the artificial intelligence era.

**摘要：** 托布勒地理学第一定律（Tobler's First Law of Geography, TFL）是空间分析的基础。为回应Sui（2004）提出的思考地理学是否只是TFL之脚注的邀请，我们构建了一个五轴框架，用于比较主要的空间统计范式。其中四个轴涵盖时空关联性、距离与邻近性、异质性与尺度以及时间动态，另有一个关于估计、推断与学习的方法论层面。我们深入考察了五种范式：地统计学与高斯随机场、格网模型与空间计量经济学、Moran特征向量空间滤波、地理加权回归，以及地理空间人工智能与空间学习。该框架还可扩展至其他范式。这一综合支持一种扩展的TFL，即空间关联随距离、连通性、情境、尺度和时间而变化，并突出了人工智能时代空间统计建模的启示。

### [95] Social FinTech: Localizing Finance Through Selective Folding and Reconfiguration of Market-Social Boundaries

**社会金融科技：通过选择性折叠与市场—社会边界重构实现金融地方化**

- 作者：Daniela Ferreira
- 发表：2026-10-23｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/24694452.2026.2742348](https://doi.org/10.1080/24694452.2026.2742348)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；全球化、地缘经济与产业网络｜关键词：社会金融科技、金融普惠、社区数字银行、巴西

**Abstract:** FinTech has transformed financial systems by expanding credit access, simplifying digital payments, and reducing transaction costs. Yet, its long-term contribution to financial inclusion, particularly in vulnerable contexts of the Global South, remains uncertain, as conventional models often reproduce inequalities through standardized, top-down approaches that overlook local social, economic, and territorial specificities. This article critically examines social FinTech as an alternative, emphasizing the reconfiguration of boundaries between social and market objectives. Drawing on semistructured interviews with managers of community digital banks in Brazil and coordinators of their national network, the study provides empirical insights into locally grounded financial technologies. Building on Langley’s (Citation2020) concept of folding, the article argues that social FinTech selectively folds social and market logics in territorially embedded and locally oriented ways, reconfiguring market–social boundaries through bottom-up arrangements that prioritize long-term inclusion over profit maximization. Community digital banks adapt services to specific local contexts, strengthen social networks, and provide guidance, focusing on local engagement, financial and digital literacy, and tailored support rather than scale or standardization. By highlighting the territorial, social, and relational dimensions of digital finance, the article contributes both theoretically and empirically, showing how bottom-up, context-aware FinTech can foster more equitable, resilient, and sustainable financial inclusion.

**摘要：** 金融科技（FinTech）通过扩大信贷获取、简化数字支付和降低交易成本改变了金融体系。然而，其对金融普惠的长期贡献，尤其是在全球南方的脆弱情境中，仍不确定，因为传统模式往往通过标准化、自上而下的方式再生产不平等，忽视了地方社会、经济和领土的特殊性。本文批判性地考察作为替代方案的社会金融科技（social FinTech），强调社会目标与市场目标之间边界的重构。基于对巴西社区数字银行管理者及其全国网络协调人员的半结构式访谈，本研究为扎根地方的金融技术提供了经验洞见。借鉴Langley（2020）的“折叠”（folding）概念，本文认为社会金融科技以领土嵌入和地方导向的方式选择性地折叠社会逻辑与市场逻辑，通过优先考虑长期普惠而非利润最大化的自下而上安排，重构市场—社会边界。社区数字银行使服务适应特定地方情境，加强社会网络并提供指导，侧重地方参与、金融与数字素养以及有针对性的支持，而非规模或标准化。通过突出数字金融的领土、社会和关系维度，本文在理论和经验上均有所贡献，表明自下而上、具有情境意识的金融科技能够促进更加公平、有韧性和可持续的金融普惠。

## Progress in Human Geography

*本期收录：8 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先；Vol. 50(6)*

### [96] Geographies of production I: Generative and agentic artificial intelligence

**生产地理学（一）：生成式与智能体人工智能**

- 作者：Martina Fuchs
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/03091325261501157](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261501157)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；全球化、地缘经济与产业网络｜关键词：生产地理学、生成式人工智能、数字化、进展报告

**Abstract:** Generative and agentic artificial intelligence (AI) has increasingly shaped the landscapes of production since the early 2020s. This report reviews the scholarly debate on geographies of production and includes further developments in the spatiality of digitalisation. It draws on literature on firms that develop and offer AI as a product innovation (geographies of production of AI) and firms that implement and use AI (geographies of production with AI). The report highlights current trends, reveals research gaps, and finally addresses some implications for public debate.

**摘要：** 自2020年代初以来，生成式与智能体人工智能（generative and agentic artificial intelligence, AI）日益塑造着生产的格局。本报告综述了关于生产地理学的学术讨论，并纳入数字化空间性方面的进一步进展。报告借鉴了两类文献：一类关注将AI作为产品创新进行开发和提供的企业（AI的生产地理学），另一类关注实施和使用AI的企业（借助AI的生产地理学）。报告突出了当前趋势，揭示了研究空白，并最后探讨了对公共讨论的若干启示。

### [97] Towards a new age of renting: Reimagining housing geographies beyond generation rent

**迈向租房新时代：超越“租房一代”重新构想住房地理学**

- 作者：Santiago del Río
- 发表：2026-10-27｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/03091325261500694](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261500694)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：租房、住房不安全、租房一代、资产型福利

**Abstract:** This paper critically reviews post-2008 housing geographies while reframing the field around the socio-spatial implications and possibilities of enduring housing insecurity. I introduce the new age of renting as a politico-economic conjuncture and demographic shift marked by an ageing and increasingly heterogeneous renter population. Against the logics of homeownership ideology, family-mediated asset-based welfare, and the digital governance of rental markets, I examine trajectories obscured by the generation rent framing as a window onto unfolding, though not inevitable, precarious futures. From this vantage point, I explore how changes in tenants’ social composition create possibilities to challenge housing structures and reimagine futures.

**摘要：** 本文批判性地回顾了2008年后的住房地理学研究，同时围绕持久性住房不安全的社会空间影响及其可能性对该领域进行重新框定。我提出“租房新时代”（new age of renting）这一概念，将其视为一种政治经济局势与人口结构转变，其标志是租房人口日益老龄化和异质化。针对房屋自有意识形态、以家庭为中介的资产型福利以及租赁市场数字化治理的逻辑，我考察了被“租房一代”（generation rent）框架所遮蔽的发展轨迹，以此洞察正在展开但并非不可避免的不稳定未来。从这一视角出发，我探讨了租户社会构成的变化如何为挑战住房结构和重新构想未来创造可能。

### [98] Abolitionist feminist geography: A framework for investigating the spatial dimensions of carceral reproductive injustice

**废除主义女性主义地理学：考察监禁性生殖不公正空间维度的框架**

- 作者：Clare Heggie, Tobin LeBlanc Haley, Petrea Taylor et al.
- 发表：2026-10-12｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/03091325261496308](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261496308)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：监禁地理学、生殖正义、废除主义、女性主义地理学

**Abstract:** Carceral spaces and practices threaten the reproductive rights of women and gender diverse people. As the global population of people in prisons for women is growing, there is a need for research exploring how the spaces and practices of detainment produce reproductive injustice. This article provides an overview of existing geographic and reproductive justice scholarship within the context of incarceration and reproductive rights, identifies potential convergences between reproductive justice theory and fields of abolition and carceral geography, and proposes a research agenda of abolitionist feminist geography to guide inquiry into the experience of carceral spaces as sites of reproductive injustice.

**摘要：** 监禁空间与实践威胁着女性和性别多元者的生殖权利。随着全球女子监狱在押人口不断增长，有必要开展研究，探讨羁押的空间与实践如何制造生殖不公正。本文在监禁与生殖权利的背景下概述了既有的地理学与生殖正义（reproductive justice）研究，识别了生殖正义理论与废除主义及监禁地理学领域之间的潜在交汇点，并提出了废除主义女性主义地理学（abolitionist feminist geography）的研究议程，以指导将监禁空间作为生殖不公正场所的经验研究。

### [99] Psychedelic geographies

**迷幻地理学**

- 作者：Oli Mould
- 发表：2026-10-12｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/03091325261496311](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261496311)
- 主题：批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：迷幻地理学、微观政治、殖民资本主义、情动

**Abstract:** This paper introduces psychedelic geographies as micropolitical praxis for unsettling the spatiotemporal closures of colonial capitalism, and enlivening alternative ways of being in the present. Building from Indigenous practices, countercultural histories, and the contemporary ‘psychedelic renaissance’, the paper argues that psychedelic geographies, in conjunction with (but distinct from) other radical geographical literatures, can provide important interjection into debates about how radically different experiences of space are experienced and prefigurative. Psychedelic geographies are situated alongside literatures on affect, hauntology, pluriverse imaginaries, and quantum geographies, not to supplant them but to bring them into productive resonance critiquing capitalistic spatial and temporal logics.

**摘要：** 本文提出迷幻地理学（psychedelic geographies），将其视为一种微观政治实践，用以动摇殖民资本主义的时空封闭，并激活当下存在的替代性方式。本文以原住民实践、反文化历史以及当代的“迷幻复兴”（psychedelic renaissance）为基础，认为迷幻地理学与其他激进地理学文献相结合（但又有所区别），能够为以下讨论提供重要介入：截然不同的空间经验如何被体验并具有预示性。迷幻地理学与情动、幽灵学（hauntology）、多元世界想象和量子地理学等文献并置，并非要取代它们，而是使它们形成富有成效的共鸣，以批判资本主义的空间与时间逻辑。

### [100] Tourism geography I: Capitalist accumulation and the socioecological limits of tourism

**旅游地理学（一）：资本积累与旅游的社会生态极限**

- 作者：Mary Mostafanezhad
- 发表：2026-10-11｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/03091325261496307](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261496307)
- 主题：批判理论、知识生产与学科反思；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：旅游地理学、资本积累、过度旅游、去增长

**Abstract:** Over the past two decades, tourism geographers have approached tourism as a mode of capitalist accumulation that produces space, reorganizes labor and social reproduction, and appropriates nature. This report traces that shift through work on uneven development, financialization, platform capitalism, labor, and political ecology. It distinguishes limits to growth from the contradictions and limits generated through accumulation. These tensions become visible in debates over overtourism, while degrowth, post-capitalist, and regenerative approaches ask what contraction, redistribution, and ecological repair might require. I argue that tourism offers human geography a critical vantage point for examining how these relations might be organized otherwise.

**摘要：** 过去二十年来，旅游地理学者将旅游视为一种资本积累模式，它生产空间、重组劳动与社会再生产，并占有自然。本报告通过有关不均衡发展、金融化、平台资本主义、劳动和政治生态学的研究追溯了这一转向。报告区分了增长的极限与积累过程中所产生的矛盾和极限。这些张力在关于过度旅游（overtourism）的争论中显现出来，而去增长、后资本主义和再生性路径则追问收缩、再分配和生态修复可能需要什么。我认为，旅游为人文地理学提供了一个批判性视角，用以考察这些关系如何能够以另一种方式组织。

### [101] Recentering property’s relations: An agenda for geographies of homelessness

**重新聚焦财产关系：无家可归地理学的研究议程**

- 作者：Stephen Przybylinski
- 发表：2026-10-09｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/03091325261496309](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261496309)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：无家可归、财产关系、关系性方法、种族建构

**Abstract:** Property is of concern to human geographers because of how it shapes the social circumstances and physical conditions of places. Given property’s role in structuring the socio-spatial conditions of homelessness, geographic research on homelessness has analyzed property for decades. However, with advances in property scholarship emerging within the broader discipline, geographies of homelessness can recenter property in its analyses. To do so, this paper promotes a relational approach to analyzing property by highlighting key features of property’s formations: home-making, race-making, and political subject-making, formations that cannot only inform and guide homelessness research, but research concerned with the puzzle of possession in shaping more just geographies.

**摘要：** 财产之所以受到人文地理学者的关注，在于它如何塑造地方的社会状况和物质条件。鉴于财产在构建无家可归的社会空间条件中的作用，关于无家可归的地理学研究已对财产进行了数十年的分析。然而，随着更广泛学科内财产研究的进展，无家可归地理学可以在其分析中重新聚焦财产。为此，本文倡导一种分析财产的关系性方法，突出财产形成的关键特征：家园营造、种族建构和政治主体建构。这些形成过程不仅能够启发和指导无家可归研究，也能指导那些关注占有之谜如何塑造更公正地理的研究。

### [102] Cultural geography I: Relationality’s ‘scenes of concern’

**文化地理学（一）：关系性的“关切场景”**

- 作者：Mitch Rose
- 发表：2026-09-29｜Vol. 50(6)｜DOI: [10.1177/03091325261475502](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261475502)
- 主题：批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：文化地理学、关系转向、过程本体论、组合

**Abstract:** Reflecting on twenty-five years of the relational turn, this report explores how cultural geography has been shaped by its longstanding engagement with theories that emphasise becoming, liveliness and relationality. Specifically, it focuses on four ‘scenes of concern’ that stem from the subfield’s assimilation of process-oriented ontologies: (1) an attention to liveliness, (2) decentring the human, (3) tracing assemblages and (4) the call to creativity. In this manner, the report sets the stage for future reports that will explore work questioning relationality and/or its founding presumptions.

**摘要：** 本报告回顾关系转向（relational turn）的二十五年历程，探讨文化地理学如何受到其长期以来与强调生成、活力和关系性的理论之互动的塑造。具体而言，报告聚焦于该分支学科吸收过程导向本体论所衍生的四个“关切场景”（scenes of concern）：（1）对活力的关注，（2）去人类中心化，（3）追踪组合（assemblages），以及（4）对创造性的呼唤。由此，本报告为后续报告奠定基础，后续报告将探讨质疑关系性及/或其基本预设的研究。

### [103] Encountering stories: Migration, belonging and geography’s ‘stories-so-far’

**遭遇故事：移民、归属与地理学的“迄今为止的故事”**

- 作者：Olivia Sheringham, Sarah M. Hughes
- 发表：2026-09-23｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/03091325261489808](https://doi.org/10.1177/03091325261489808)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：移民、归属、讲故事、边界化

**Abstract:** In this paper we extend Cameron’s (2012) call for closer scrutiny of the political, ethical and intellectual possibilities of stories and storytelling within geography. Whilst an interest in stories and storytelling remains prevalent – both through attention to the multilayered stories underpinning the subjects of research, as well as the burgeoning of different modes of storytelling as method and the ethics of telling “othered” stories – we argue that the roles and meanings of “story” and “storytelling” remain under-theorised. Bringing scholarship on stories and storytelling from geography and cultural and literary studies into conversation with critical migration research, we examine how stories shape and are shaped by the politics of bordering, belonging, and encounter. Doing so, we explore the potential of stories to resist and disrupt dominant narratives and ideologies that seek to define, to (b)order and to exclude. The paper puts forward an argument for treating stories as relational and ongoing encounters through which regimes of mobility, bordering, and belonging are contested, and where possibilities for hope and resistance may emerge.

**摘要：** 本文拓展了Cameron（2012）的呼吁，即更仔细地审视地理学中故事与讲故事的政治、伦理和智识可能性。尽管对故事与讲故事的兴趣依然普遍——既体现在对支撑研究对象的多层次故事的关注上，也体现在作为方法的各种讲故事模式的蓬勃发展以及讲述“他者化”故事的伦理问题上——但我们认为，“故事”与“讲故事”的角色和意义仍缺乏理论化。通过将地理学及文化与文学研究中关于故事与讲故事的学术成果与批判性移民研究进行对话，我们考察了故事如何塑造边界化、归属与遭遇的政治，又如何被其所塑造。在此基础上，我们探讨了故事抵抗和扰乱那些试图界定、划界/规制（(b)order）和排斥的主导叙事与意识形态的潜力。本文主张将故事视为关系性的、持续进行的遭遇，流动性、边界化与归属的制度正是在其中受到争夺，希望与抵抗的可能性也可能由此浮现。

## Dialogues in Human Geography

*本期收录：14 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [104] Who cares for water? Capital, the state and the plural waters of Thirst

**谁来关怀水？资本、国家与《干渴》中的多元之水**

- 作者：Filippo Menga
- 发表：2026-11-19｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261510159](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261510159)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：水、关怀、资本主义、尺度

**Abstract:** In this response to the generous commentaries on Thirst , I clarify the book's religious register and reflect on how capitalism captures care and where its burdens fall. I then turn to the state and the problem of scale, and to the question of what water needs, which I read as a call to return to specific waters, places, and people, arguing that care for water must be reclaimed as a collective and public practice.

**摘要：** 在对《干渴》（Thirst）一书诸多慷慨评论的回应中，我阐明了该书的宗教维度，并反思资本主义如何捕获关怀以及其负担落于何处。随后，我转向国家与尺度问题，以及“水需要什么”这一问题——我将其解读为回归具体的水、地方与人的呼唤，并主张必须将对水的关怀重新确立为一种集体的、公共的实践。

### [105] Cartographies of sacrifice: Mnemonic geography, martyr commemoration and the making of national space in palestine

**牺牲的制图：记忆地理、烈士纪念与巴勒斯坦民族空间的塑造**

- 作者：Mohammed Hamdan, Bayan AlAmmouri, Lindsey Moore
- 发表：2026-11-10｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502888](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502888)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：记忆地理、烈士纪念、巴勒斯坦、坚守

**Abstract:** This article theorizes Nablus, a Palestinian city in the contemporary West Bank, as a cultural geographic site that exemplifies a rich cartography of sacrifice wherein urban spaces become inseparable from memory and identity. Drawing on Palestinian scholarship on memory, spatial practice and everyday life, we argue that the Old City of Nablus functions as a counter-geography in which martyr memory, survival and belonging are continually inscribed and lived. Its memorial walls and improvised shrines operate as mnemonic devices and spatial archives that reflect a long history of collective loss and resilience. Space is often weaponized in Palestine: checkpoints disrupt continuity, surveillance causes anxiety and settlements continue to radically change topography. However, in Nablus, ṣumūd or steadfastness allows people to reclaim their city and reconstruct its national value by turning geography into a narrative of commemoration. Drawing on interviews with residents and analysis of graffiti, heritage signs and commemorative sites in Nablus's Old City (al-Balda al-Qadīma), we suggest that memory is actively performed when mourning is intertwined with everyday life, thereby materializing a living geography of sacrifice and endurance. The article concludes that Nablus is an enduring testimony to how Palestinian urban spaces resist erasure by transforming martyrdom into a spatial language that embodies persistence.

**摘要：** 本文将当代约旦河西岸的巴勒斯坦城市纳布卢斯（Nablus）理论化为一个文化地理场所，它例证了一种丰富的“牺牲制图”，在其中城市空间与记忆和身份密不可分。借鉴巴勒斯坦关于记忆、空间实践与日常生活的学术研究，我们认为纳布卢斯老城发挥着一种“反地理”（counter-geography）的作用，烈士记忆、生存与归属在其中被持续地铭刻和经历。其纪念墙和临时神龛作为记忆装置和空间档案，映照出漫长的集体丧失与坚韧历史。在巴勒斯坦，空间常常被武器化：检查站破坏了连续性，监控引发焦虑，定居点持续彻底改变地形。然而，在纳布卢斯，“坚守”（ṣumūd）使人们能够夺回自己的城市，并通过将地理转化为纪念叙事来重建其民族价值。基于对居民的访谈以及对纳布卢斯老城（al-Balda al-Qadīma）涂鸦、遗产标识和纪念场所的分析，我们认为，当哀悼与日常生活交织在一起时，记忆被积极地展演，从而物质化了一种关于牺牲与坚忍的活的地理。本文的结论是，纳布卢斯是巴勒斯坦城市空间如何抵抗抹除的持久见证：它将殉难转化为一种体现坚持的空间语言。

### [106] Animals’ territories

**动物的领域**

- 作者：Jamie Lorimer, Tom Fry, Oscar Hartman-Davies et al.
- 发表：2026-11-10｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261506619](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261506619)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：领域、动物地理学、超越人类、多物种共存

**Abstract:** This paper develops a conceptual framework for research on animals’ territories. It starts from a genealogy of the fraught traffic of territorial ideas between ethology and geography, in which the disavowal of biological determinism led to a stark humanist divide in territorial research in political geography. It argues that this division is founded on an anachronistic understanding of animal behaviour, which hinders the development of a more-than-human conception of territory in political geography, ultimately limiting critical analysis of the widespread and often devastating human impacts on animals’ territories. To redress these problems, the second and third sections draw on recent ethological research to introduce animals’ terrain – defined as the material properties of a territory sensed in relation to animals’ Umwelten – and animals’ territorialities or territorial practices. It shows how analyses of animals help further calls for plural and material accounts of territory. A fourth section outlines and evaluates a model of territory for a practical politics of multispecies coexistence premised on wild diplomacy through interspecies communication. The conclusions identify key points for dialogue with both animal and political geographers. The analysis is illustrated with examples of the territorial birds and mammals that predominate in research in ethology and animal geography.

**摘要：** 本文为动物领域研究构建了一个概念框架。文章首先梳理了领域观念在动物行为学与地理学之间充满张力的往来谱系：对生物决定论的否认导致了政治地理学领域研究中鲜明的人文主义分野。本文认为，这一分野建立在对动物行为的过时理解之上，阻碍了政治地理学中“超越人类”（more-than-human）领域观念的发展，最终限制了对人类对动物领域广泛且往往是毁灭性影响的批判性分析。为解决这些问题，第二和第三部分借鉴近期动物行为学研究，引入“动物的地形”（animals’ terrain）——定义为相对于动物的周遭世界（Umwelten）而被感知的领域的物质属性——以及动物的领域性或领域实践。本文表明，对动物的分析如何有助于推进对领域的多元与物质性阐释的呼吁。第四部分概述并评估了一种领域模型，该模型服务于以跨物种交流为基础的“野性外交”（wild diplomacy）的多物种共存实践政治。结论部分指出了与动物地理学家和政治地理学家展开对话的关键议题。本文的分析以在动物行为学和动物地理学研究中占主导地位的具领域性的鸟类和哺乳动物为例加以说明。

### [107] On hinterlands

**论腹地**

- 作者：Jim Thatcher
- 发表：2026-11-07｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502884](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502884)
- 主题：全球化、地缘经济与产业网络；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：腹地、数字资本主义、计算倒置、类型学

**Abstract:** Rosen and Alvarez León's typology of “variegated hinterlands” provides a powerful and timely analytic for examining digital capitalism. This commentary pushes the authors on two fronts: first, I question the fluidity between types of hinterlands and their related cores, warning against a typological collapse in which all spaces become hinterlands to some other; second, I push the authors to further define the differences in kind from previous iterations of capitalist expansion and exploitation. To do so, I highlight the growing computational inversion in which data is extracted from urban cores to be processed, analyzed, and decided upon by algorithmic agents located in distant, traditionally defined hinterlands. To better understand this inversion and its already-arriving impacts, I argue geographers must use the typology not simply to map the traces of these relations, but to build a topology of contestation.

**摘要：** Rosen与Alvarez León提出的“杂色腹地”（variegated hinterlands）类型学为考察数字资本主义提供了一个有力且适时的分析工具。本评论从两个方面对作者提出进一步要求：首先，我质疑各类腹地与其相关核心之间的流动性，警惕一种类型学坍塌——即所有空间都成为另一空间的腹地；其次，我促使作者进一步界定其与以往资本主义扩张和剥削形态之间的本质差异。为此，我强调一种日益增长的计算倒置（computational inversion）：数据从城市核心被提取出来，由位于遥远的、传统意义上的腹地中的算法代理进行处理、分析和决策。为更好地理解这种倒置及其已然显现的影响，我认为地理学家不应仅仅利用该类型学来描绘这些关系的痕迹，而应借此构建一种争夺的拓扑学。

### [108] Volumetric commensuration and its remainders

**体积通约及其剩余**

- 作者：Hung-Ying Chen
- 发表：2026-11-03｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502903](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502903)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：空中权、大气权、通约、公地

**Abstract:** This commentary responds to Daniel Paiva's call for the right to atmosphere by advancing the framework of following air rights . I treat breathable air, buildable volume and emission allowances as claims held apart by three interrelated operations: differentiation, detachment and conversion. Comparing two instruments, I argue that the property/commons contradiction is produced by design and not just given in nature. Admissibility – whether claims are admitted for political consideration – is a prior condition of meaningful participation.

**摘要：** 本评论回应Daniel Paiva关于“大气权”的呼吁，提出“追踪空中权”（following air rights）的框架。我将可呼吸的空气、可建设体积和排放配额视为由三种相互关联的操作——区分、剥离与转换——所分隔开的权利主张。通过比较两种工具，我认为财产/公地矛盾是由设计所生产的，而不仅仅是自然给定的。可受理性（admissibility）——即权利主张是否被纳入政治考量——是有意义参与的先决条件。

### [110] The everyday as ecological archive

**作为生态档案的日常**

- 作者：Yogesh Mishra
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502904](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502904)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：种姓、感官地理、日常生活、印度

**Abstract:** Growing up in a small village in northern India, one of my earliest memories of caste is inseparable from smell.On the outskirts of my village stood a tannery along the main road connecting the village to the nearby city.Whenever we returned home from the city by bus, the pungent odor of decomposing hides, flesh, and blood became an unmistakable sign that we were nearing our village.The tannery and its stench marked the village's imagined boundary.Looking back, I realize that what I had acquired was not simply an awareness of caste but a sensorial imagination of it.Yet while the smell remained unforgettable, the people who worked there were almost entirely absent from our conversations.I only recall hearing about a young man who possessed the remarkable skill of skinning an animal with nothing more than a simple blade.His labor was recognized as technical expertise, yet his life, the lives of those who lived in that settlement, and the knowledge embedded in their work remained invisible.Long before I encountered caste as a sociological concept, I had learned to associate certain smells, occupations, objects, and bodies with pollution and hierarchy, while remaining largely oblivious to the lives and labor of those who inhabited those spaces.As Kapoor (2021: 166) argues, caste is experienced through the sensory ordering of bodies, objects, and spaces according to the logics of purity and pollution.The sayings I grew up hearing, "jaati hai woh jo kabhi nahin jaati" ("Caste is that which never goes away") and "badi jaati samjhavae se, chot ̣i jaati latiyavae se" ("Those from the higher castes are reasoned with; those from the lower castes are kicked"), reinforced an understanding of caste as enduring, hierarchical, and deeply embedded in everyday social relations.At the time, they seemed

**摘要：** 我在印度北部的一个小村庄长大，我对种姓最早的记忆之一与气味密不可分。在我们村子的外围，连接村庄与附近城市的主干道旁有一家制革厂。每当我们乘公交车从城里回家，腐烂的兽皮、血肉散发出的刺鼻气味便成为我们即将抵达村子的明确信号。制革厂及其恶臭标示着村庄想象中的边界。回首往事，我意识到我所习得的不仅是对种姓的认知，更是一种关于种姓的感官想象。然而，尽管那股气味令人难忘，在那里工作的人们却几乎完全缺席于我们的谈话。我只记得听说过一个年轻人，他拥有仅凭一把简单的刀片就能剥下动物皮的非凡技艺。他的劳动被视为技术专长，但他的生活、那片聚居地居民的生活，以及蕴含在他们工作中的知识，却始终不为人所见。早在我将种姓作为一个社会学概念来接触之前，我就已学会将某些气味、职业、物品和身体与污秽和等级联系起来，而对生活和劳作于这些空间中的人们的生活与劳动则基本视而不见。正如Kapoor（2021: 166）所指出的，种姓是通过依据洁净与污秽逻辑对身体、物品和空间进行感官排序而被经验的。我从小听到的俗语——“jaati hai woh jo kabhi nahin jaati”（“种姓是永远不会消失的东西”）和“badi jaati samjhavae se, choṭi jaati latiyavae se”（“对高种姓的人要讲道理，对低种姓的人要用脚踢”）——强化了一种将种姓理解为持久的、等级化的、深深嵌入日常社会关系之中的观念。在当时，它们似乎

### [111] Atmospheric fury, shadows of agonism, and the ethics of abolishing worlds

**大气之怒、竞争主义的阴影与废除世界的伦理**

- 作者：Mikko Joronen
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502906](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502906)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：大气权、竞争主义、废除主义、元素地理

**Abstract:** This commentary engages Daniel Paiva’s (2026) proposal to think the ‘right to atmosphere’ in agonistic terms. First, drawing on Michel Serres, it elaborates the role of turbulent ‘fury’ in the elemental constitution of atmospheres. It argues the fury fundamentally troubles agonistic pluralism by showing how worlds are broken, ended and produced through materialities that do not easily lend themselves to what remains central to the ‘right to atmosphere’. After looking at the collective equivalence of fury, the commentary turns, second, to the three shadows of agonism – intrinsic atmospheric seductions, unruly movements irreducible to identifiable origins or parties to a conflict, and brutal colonial shadows shattering the promises of productive conflict – problematising the assumption that conflict necessarily functions as a creative, productive and capacitating force. Finally, drawing on abolitionist thought, it asks whether the solution to unequal access to atmospheres, one capable of acknowledging the anti-oppressive ethics of care underlined by Paiva, is inclusion and participation, or the abolition of oppressive worlds.

**摘要：** 本评论回应Daniel Paiva（2026）以竞争主义（agonistic）视角思考“大气权”（right to atmosphere）的主张。首先，借鉴米歇尔·塞尔（Michel Serres），本文阐述了湍动的“狂怒”（fury）在大气的元素构成中所扮演的角色。本文认为，这种狂怒从根本上困扰着竞争性多元主义，因为它表明世界是如何经由某些物质性而被打破、终结和生产的，而这些物质性并不容易契合“大气权”的核心要素。在考察狂怒的集体等价性之后，本评论其次转向竞争主义的三重阴影——内在的大气诱惑、无法还原为可识别的源头或冲突方的不羁运动，以及粉碎生产性冲突之承诺的残酷殖民阴影——从而质疑冲突必然作为一种创造性、生产性和赋能性力量而发挥作用的假设。最后，借鉴废除主义（abolitionist）思想，本文追问：对于大气获取不平等的问题，一种能够承认Paiva所强调的反压迫关怀伦理的解决方案，究竟是包容与参与，还是废除压迫性的世界。

### [112] Planetary 'Singaporeanization': Geopolitics within planetary rural/urban geographies

**行星尺度的“新加坡化”：行星乡村/城市地理中的地缘政治**

- 作者：James D. Sidaway, Dariusz Wójcik
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261479812](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261479812)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：新加坡、城市国家、行星乡村地理、主权

**Abstract:** This commentary engages the article by Chi-Mao Wang, Damien Maye, and Michael Woods (2025) on ‘Planetary rural geographies’ through the vantage point of the city-state of Singapore. We argue that viewing the themes developed by Wang et al. from and through Singapore and other city-states brings out (geo)political frames – notably sovereignty, empires, and their aftermaths – with implications for other cases, sites, and the wider conceptual debate.

**摘要：** 本评论从城市国家新加坡的视角回应王志懋（Chi-Mao Wang）、Damien Maye和Michael Woods（2025）关于“行星乡村地理”的文章。我们认为，从新加坡及其他城市国家出发并透过它们来审视Wang等人提出的主题，能够凸显（地缘）政治框架——尤其是主权、帝国及其后续影响——这对其他案例、地点以及更广泛的概念讨论均具有启示意义。

### [113] LGBTphobia as a multiscale assemblage: Rural LBT women’s everyday geographies

**作为多尺度集合体的LGBT恐惧：乡村LBT女性的日常地理**

- 作者：Gilly Hartal, Yael Bar-Tzedek
- 发表：2026-10-30｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502892](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502892)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：LGBT恐惧、乡村地理、酷儿地理、集合体

**Abstract:** This article examines LGBTphobia as experienced by lesbian, bisexual, and transgender (LBT) women in Israeli rural space. Conceptualized here as a multiscale assemblage, five manifestations of LGBTphobia are presented: (1) Whispering, giggling and gossip; (2) insults related to sexual and gender identity; (3) discrimination and exclusion; (4) intrusion, sexualization, and eroticization; and (5) Condescension and boundary construction. Although neoliberal discourses often assume that LGBTQ + acceptance has become increasingly widespread, particularly in urban settings, this analysis demonstrates that inclusion does not eliminate LGBTphobia. Rather, inclusion and LGBTphobia coexist, with narratives of progress and acceptance frequently obscuring more subtle yet persistent forms of exclusion, marginalization, and prejudice. Relatedly, whereas recent scholarship suggests that rural areas may serve as spaces of tolerance and safety for LGBTQ + s thanks to the diffusion of urban discourses of acceptance into rural contexts, the findings show how LGBTphobia becomes internalized, embodied, and affectively reproduced in everyday life. Among LBT women interviewed, the anticipation of LGBTphobia prompted self-censorship even before any overt incidents occurred. These dynamics reveal LGBTphobia as an embodied and spatial structure rather than a solely interpersonal phenomenon, continuing to shape participants’ everyday rural lives despite apparent social progress.

**摘要：** 本文考察以色列乡村空间中女同性恋、双性恋和跨性别（LBT）女性所经历的LGBT恐惧（LGBTphobia）。本文将其概念化为一种多尺度集合体（multiscale assemblage），并呈现了LGBT恐惧的五种表现形式：（1）窃窃私语、嗤笑与闲言碎语；（2）与性取向和性别认同相关的侮辱；（3）歧视与排斥；（4）侵扰、性化与色情化；以及（5）居高临下与边界建构。尽管新自由主义话语常常假定对LGBTQ+群体的接纳日益普遍，尤其是在城市环境中，但本分析表明，包容并不能消除LGBT恐惧。相反，包容与LGBT恐惧并存，关于进步与接纳的叙事往往遮蔽了更为隐蔽却持久的排斥、边缘化和偏见形式。与此相关，近期研究认为，由于城市接纳话语向乡村语境扩散，乡村地区可能成为LGBTQ+群体的宽容与安全空间；然而，研究结果表明LGBT恐惧如何在日常生活中被内化、具身化并在情感上被再生产。在受访的LBT女性中，对LGBT恐惧的预期甚至在任何公开事件发生之前就促使她们进行自我审查。这些动态揭示出，LGBT恐惧是一种具身的、空间性的结构，而不仅仅是一种人际现象；尽管社会表面上在进步，它仍持续塑造着受访者的乡村日常生活。

### [114] Before and beyond the rural rent gap: Brokered legibility and authorised asset value in China’s rural revitalisation

**乡村租隙之前与之外：中国乡村振兴中的经纪化可读性与授权资产价值**

- 作者：Ying Wang, Tin-Yuet Ting
- 发表：2026-10-27｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502896](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502896)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：乡村士绅化、资产化、乡村振兴、可读性

**Abstract:** This commentary extends Phillips and Smith's comparative agenda for rural gentrification by putting it in dialogue with Birch and Ward's account of assetization. We argue that rural gentrification and assetization require attention to a prior institutional passage: the conversion of ambiguous rural resources into authorised objects of operation. Drawing on China's rural revitalisation, we call this process brokered legibility. The concept identifies a legibility gap between potential value and value that can be contracted, funded, audited, governed and publicly defended. By foregrounding quasi-state intermediaries and delegated operation, the commentary shifts analysis from value capture alone to the contested authorisation of rural asset value, including the de-authorisation of alternative rural futures.

**摘要：** 本评论通过将Phillips与Smith关于乡村士绅化的比较研究议程与Birch和Ward关于资产化的论述相对话，对前者加以拓展。我们认为，乡村士绅化与资产化需要关注一个先行的制度环节：将模糊的乡村资源转化为经授权的运营对象。基于中国乡村振兴的经验，我们将这一过程称为“经纪化可读性”（brokered legibility）。该概念识别出潜在价值与可签约、可融资、可审计、可治理且可公开辩护的价值之间存在的“可读性缺口”。通过突出准国家中介和委托运营，本评论将分析焦点从单纯的价值攫取转向对乡村资产价值的争议性授权，包括对其他乡村未来的去授权。

### [116] On seasonal influences, refrains, and weirdness: A response to Kaya Barry’s geographies of the seasons

**论季节影响、叠歌与怪异：回应Kaya Barry的季节地理学**

- 作者：Derek McCormack
- 发表：2026-10-27｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502883](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502883)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：季节性、叠歌、时空、气候变化

**Abstract:** Responding to the invitation offered by Kaya Barry to think more about seasonality, in this article I offer three supplements to her timely and important project. First, I revisit earlier geographical work on seasonality in order to think about the question of influence. Second, I consider how the shifting space–times of seasonality might be understood as refrains of a sort. Third, I reflect on what it might mean to think with/in the latitude-specific weirdness of un/seasonality.

**摘要：** 应Kaya Barry关于进一步思考季节性的邀请，本文为她这一适时且重要的研究计划提供三点补充。首先，我回顾地理学早期关于季节性的研究，以思考“影响”问题。其次，我探讨如何将季节性不断变化的时空理解为某种“叠歌”（refrains）。第三，我反思在特定纬度下以“非/季节性”的怪异（weirdness）进行思考、并置身其中意味着什么。

### [117] Layered proximity: Distant time and deferred temporality in circular bioeconomy

**层叠的邻近性：循环生物经济中的遥远时间与延宕时间性**

- 作者：Goshu Wolde Tefera
- 发表：2026-10-27｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1177/20438206261502879](https://doi.org/10.1177/20438206261502879)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：循环生物经济、时间性、时空压缩、殖民资本主义

**Abstract:** This commentary engages Datta's concept of distant time, which explains how colonial statecraft governs subaltern populations through temporal deferral, keeping them suspended in conditions of waiting while framing displacement and demolition as development. Building on Datta's work, I argue that her framework under-theorizes the complementary process of temporal compression. Using Harvey's concept of time-space compression as a conceptual lens and drawing on scholarship on the circular bioeconomy, I show how contemporary colonial-capitalist governance not only defers justice but also compresses marginalized communities into global circuits of value in real time. In the circular bioeconomy, forests, farmers, and waste pickers are rendered immediately governable and economically productive, even as the benefits they help generate are projected into an indefinitely deferred future. Rather than replacing Datta's framework, I propose a reorientation toward a theory of layered proximity, where immediate material incorporation coexists with the continuous postponement of justice.

**摘要：** 本评论回应Datta提出的“遥远时间”（distant time）概念，该概念解释了殖民治国术如何通过时间延宕来治理从属人口，使其悬置于等待状态，同时将迁离和拆除包装为发展。在Datta研究的基础上，我认为其框架对互补性的时间压缩过程理论化不足。以哈维的时空压缩概念为分析视角，并借鉴关于循环生物经济（circular bioeconomy）的研究，我表明当代殖民—资本主义治理不仅延宕正义，而且将边缘化社群实时压缩进全球价值循环之中。在循环生物经济中，森林、农民和拾荒者被即时转化为可治理的、具有经济生产力的对象，而他们参与创造的收益却被投射到一个无限延宕的未来。本文并非要取代Datta的框架，而是提出向“层叠的邻近性”（layered proximity）理论的重新定向：即时的物质纳入与正义的持续延宕并存。

## Antipode

*本期收录：15 篇｜卷期：Vol. 58(6)*

### [118] The Limits of Cash: Guaranteed Income, Basic Income, and the Reproduction of the Precarious City

**现金的局限：保障性收入、基本收入与不稳定城市的再生产**

- 作者：James C. Fraser, Deirdre A. Oakley
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70248](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70248)
- 主题：城市空间、社会分异与不平等；金融化、住房与房地产｜关键词：保障性收入、可负担住房、常态化、种族化住房市场

**Abstract:** Guaranteed income pilots and COVID‐era relief payments share a political form: they are time‐limited. This paper asks what that form does in a racialized housing market undergoing restructuring. Drawing on a five‐year participatory panel study of low‐income, predominantly Black women in Preservation of Affordable Housing properties in Cincinnati's Over‐the‐Rhine, we find that a bounded infusion of unconditional cash left the distributive register unmoved while the relational one eroded around it. Within‐person, neighborhood attachment declined significantly over the payment period and did not recover; social cohesion held through the infusion and then fell sharply after it ended; financial strain moved in neither direction. We read the pattern as normalization, a governing technology that holds precarity within a manageable range through statutory sunsets, tax‐credit covenants, and a resumed eviction docket. The argument is not against cash but against the assumption that a distributive instrument can reach a relational injury.

**摘要：** 保障性收入（guaranteed income）试点与新冠疫情时期的救济款项具有共同的政治形式：它们都是有时限的。本文探讨这一形式在一个正经历重构的种族化住房市场中产生了何种作用。基于对辛辛那提Over-the-Rhine地区“可负担住房保护”（Preservation of Affordable Housing）物业中低收入、以黑人女性为主的居民开展的为期五年的参与式面板研究，我们发现，有限度的无条件现金注入并未改变分配层面（distributive register），而关系层面却在其周围遭到侵蚀。在个体内部，邻里依恋在支付期间显著下降且此后未能恢复；社会凝聚力在现金注入期间得以维持，但在其结束后急剧下降；财务压力则既未改善也未恶化。我们将这一模式解读为“常态化”（normalization），即一种治理技术，它通过法定日落条款、税收抵免契约以及恢复的驱逐案件审理程序，将不稳定性维持在可控范围之内。本文的论点并非反对现金发放，而是反对这样一种假设：分配性工具能够触及关系性伤害。

### [119] Black and Pan‐African Joy and the Specter of Fascism

**黑人与泛非洲的喜悦和法西斯主义的幽灵**

- 作者：Horace G. Campbell
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70233](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70233)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：种族资本主义、新法西斯主义、黑人喜悦、黑人音乐

**Abstract:** This paper situates contemporary neofascism within the history of racial capitalism, arguing that Black Joy and “Emancipation Circuits” constitute material practices of resistance to dehumanization. Grounding capitalist transformations in the systematic negation of Black humanity, it challenges abstract accounts of valorization and explores joy's ontological foundations in African traditions of optimism and collective struggle. It critiques Eurocentric interpretations of Nazism and fascist terror, examining how Cold War deployments of authoritarianism obscured the historical relations through which fascism emerged and consolidated. It identifies sonic landscapes, from jazz to R&B, as “rival geographies” of autonomous expression and resistance. Contrasting Wagnerian aesthetic closure with John Coltrane's improvisational freedom, it locates Black music within emancipation struggles. Finally, it advocates a “popular multiracial front from below” uniting anti‐racist, feminist, and Indigenous struggles against the normalization of financial warfare and the deployment of technological force that facilitate the maturation of neofascism into fully consolidated fascist rule.

**摘要：** 本文将当代新法西斯主义置于种族资本主义（racial capitalism）的历史之中，认为“黑人喜悦”（Black Joy）与“解放回路”（Emancipation Circuits）构成了抵抗非人化的物质实践。本文将资本主义转型的根基置于对黑人人性的系统性否定之中，挑战了关于价值增殖的抽象论述，并探讨了喜悦在非洲乐观主义与集体斗争传统中的本体论基础。本文批判了对纳粹主义和法西斯恐怖的欧洲中心主义诠释，考察了冷战时期对“威权主义”概念的运用如何遮蔽了法西斯主义得以产生和巩固的历史关系。本文将从爵士乐到节奏布鲁斯（R&B）的声音景观视为自主表达与抵抗的“对抗性地理”（rival geographies）。通过对比瓦格纳式的美学封闭与约翰·柯川（John Coltrane）的即兴自由，本文将黑人音乐置于解放斗争之中。最后，本文倡导建立一个“自下而上的人民多种族阵线”，联合反种族主义、女性主义和原住民斗争，以对抗金融战争的常态化和技术暴力的部署——正是这些因素促使新法西斯主义成熟为全面巩固的法西斯统治。

### [120] Subalternity in an Age of Planetary Crisis

**行星危机时代的从属性**

- 作者：Alex Loftus
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70226](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70226)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：葛兰西、从属性、气候危机、化石资本

**Abstract:** This paper argues that Gramsci's conceptualisation of subalternity offers crucial resources for theorising and intervening in the global climate crisis to popular democratic ends. While ‘the subaltern’ has proven highly generative in postcolonial writings—and in geographical thought more widely—Gramsci's own conceptualisation has been relatively neglected. Nevertheless, recent philological approaches have shed light on his highly distinctive interpretation, one that captures how subaltern social groups are integrally included in the relations of the modern state, limiting their autonomy and ability to enact political change. When the integral state is situated within its metabolic conditions of possibility, subalternity can be viewed as a form of domination specific to the age of fossil capital. Most importantly, Gramsci's methodological and strategic approach enables us to identify moments through which an age of planetary crisis might be transformed in popular democratic ways.

**摘要：** 本文认为，葛兰西关于从属性（subalternity）的概念化为以人民民主为目标来理论化和介入全球气候危机提供了关键资源。尽管“从属者”（the subaltern）概念在后殖民著述以及更广泛的地理学思想中极具生产力，但葛兰西本人的概念化却相对受到忽视。然而，近期的文献学研究揭示了其极具特色的诠释：这一诠释揭示了从属社会群体如何被整合性地纳入现代国家的关系之中，从而限制了其自主性和推动政治变革的能力。当整体国家（integral state）被置于其可能性的代谢条件之中时，从属性可被视为化石资本时代特有的一种支配形式。最重要的是，葛兰西的方法论与战略路径使我们能够识别出那些可以以人民民主方式改变行星危机时代的契机。

### [121] Governing the Future: Artificial Intelligence as an Anticipatory Infrastructure of Control

**治理未来：作为预期性控制基础设施的人工智能**

- 作者：Giuseppe T. Cirella
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70237](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70237)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：人工智能、预期性治理、监控、算法控制

**Abstract:** Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in systems of governance, yet its political implications remain insufficiently theorized. This paper argues that AI is best understood as an anticipatory infrastructure of control: a form of power that operates through the preemptive governance of possible futures. Drawing on cases of biometric border systems, predictive policing, and AI‐enabled surveillance in authoritarian contexts, it shows how algorithmic systems classify and intervene on the basis of predicted risk. In doing so, they shift governance from regulating past and present conduct to managing future possibilities. Extending theories of discipline, biopolitics, and control, the paper demonstrates how anticipatory control is unevenly distributed, disproportionately targeting migrants, racialized communities, and political dissidents within longer histories of colonial domination and social inequality. Addressing these dynamics requires moving beyond the regulation of technologies to contesting the infrastructures through which futures are governed.

**摘要：** 人工智能（AI）日益嵌入治理体系之中，但其政治意涵尚未得到充分的理论化。本文认为，人工智能最好被理解为一种预期性控制基础设施（anticipatory infrastructure of control）：一种通过对可能未来进行先发制人式治理而运作的权力形式。本文借助生物识别边境系统、预测性警务以及威权语境下人工智能驱动的监控等案例，揭示算法系统如何基于预测风险进行分类和干预。由此，治理从规制过去和当下的行为转向管理未来的可能性。本文拓展了关于规训、生命政治和控制的理论，表明预期性控制的分布是不均衡的，在殖民统治和社会不平等的更长历史中，它不成比例地针对移民、种族化社群和政治异见者。应对这些动态，需要超越对技术本身的规制，转而对治理未来所依托的基础设施提出挑战。

### [122] Theorizing Racisms Across Italy and the United States: Lessons From Gramsci and Du Bois

**跨越意大利与美国的种族主义理论化：来自葛兰西与杜波依斯的启示**

- 作者：Camilla Hawthorne
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70227](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70227)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；批判理论、知识生产与学科反思｜关键词：种族资本主义、葛兰西、杜波依斯、关系方法

**Abstract:** The 2020 Italian Black Lives Matter mobilizations were a powerful incitement to consider what is at stake in understanding Italy as part of the global history of racial capitalism—and resistance against it. To do this, it is urgent for scholars to move from comparative to relational approaches in the study of global racisms. I argue that Antonio Gramsci and W.E.B. Du Bois provide a powerful set of analytical tools for breaking out of methodological nationalism in the study of racism and race in Italy—and beyond. When read together, Gramsci's writings on Italian national unification and Du Bois' writings on post‐Civil War Reconstruction in the United States offer both a historical grounding for understanding the connections between contestations over race and nation in Italy the United States, and a framework for how to think relationally about struggles against racism and fascism unfolding in both countries today.

**摘要：** 2020年意大利“黑人的命也是命”（Black Lives Matter）运动有力地促使人们思考：将意大利理解为种族资本主义全球历史及其反抗历史的一部分，究竟意味着什么。为此，学者们亟需在全球种族主义研究中从比较方法转向关系方法。本文认为，安东尼奥·葛兰西与W.E.B.杜波依斯为打破意大利乃至其他地区种族与种族主义研究中的方法论民族主义提供了一套有力的分析工具。将二者对照阅读——葛兰西关于意大利民族统一的论述与杜波依斯关于美国内战后重建的论述——既为理解意大利与美国围绕种族与民族的争夺之间的关联提供了历史基础，也为以关系性方式思考当今两国正在展开的反种族主义与反法西斯主义斗争提供了框架。

### [123] Moving Goods, Marginalizing Workers: Racialized Labor and Logistics‐Led Growth in Chicago's Freight Economy

**货物流动，工人边缘化：芝加哥货运经济中的种族化劳动与物流主导型增长**

- 作者：José Miguel Acosta‐Córdova
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70230](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70230)
- 主题：劳动、生计与日常生活；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：物流业、种族资本主义、劳动力市场、工资差距

**Abstract:** Transportation, distribution, and logistics have become central to the contemporary organization of capitalism in the United States amid the long‐term decline of manufacturing employment. In freight‐intensive metropolitan regions, logistics has emerged as a major site of job growth, raising questions about how this expansion reshapes racial inequality in urban labor markets. This paper examines employment and wage patterns in the Chicago metropolitan area, North America's largest inland freight hub. Drawing on IPUMS microdata from the 1990 Census and 2008–2012 and 2018–2022 American Community Survey samples, the analysis traces changes in employment and median wages across railroads, trucking, and warehousing. The findings show that logistics growth has been accompanied by increasing Black and Latino employment, whereas persistent racial wage gaps remain across sub‐industries. Interpreted through racial capitalism, these patterns demonstrate how logistics restructuring has unfolded through an already segmented metropolitan labor market, with increased racialized incorporation occurring without corresponding wage convergence.

**摘要：** 在制造业就业长期衰退的背景下，运输、配送与物流已成为美国当代资本主义组织的核心。在货运密集型大都市区，物流已成为就业增长的主要领域，这引发了一个问题：这种扩张如何重塑城市劳动力市场中的种族不平等。本文考察了北美最大的内陆货运枢纽——芝加哥大都市区的就业与工资格局。基于1990年人口普查以及2008–2012年和2018–2022年美国社区调查（American Community Survey）样本的IPUMS微观数据，本文追踪了铁路、卡车运输和仓储业的就业及工资中位数变化。研究发现，物流业增长伴随着黑人和拉丁裔就业的增加，但各细分行业中的种族工资差距依然存在。从种族资本主义视角解读，这些模式表明物流业重构是在一个本已分割的大都市劳动力市场中展开的：种族化群体的纳入程度提高，却未伴随相应的工资趋同。

### [124] Street Furniture as an Assetizing Apparatus: Advertising Concessions, Public Maintenance and the Production of Neoliberal Urban Space in Glasgow

**作为资产化装置的街道设施：格拉斯哥的广告特许经营、公共维护与新自由主义城市空间的生产**

- 作者：Sinan Wang
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70231](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70231)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；金融化、住房与房地产｜关键词：资产化、街道设施、紧缩、新自由主义城市治理

**Abstract:** Across British cities, everyday street infrastructure is increasingly sustained through revenue‐generating concessions that detach maintenance from direct municipal budgets. This article examines how street furniture in Glasgow has been incrementally reorganized as an assetizing apparatus under prolonged austerity. Drawing on micro political‐economic tracing of committee records, procurement documents and transport authority contracts, it shows how maintenance obligations are displaced from public budgets into advertising‐funded income streams, standardized through procurement and consolidated through concessionary portfolios. Rather than a sudden privatization of public space, assetization emerges as an accretive institutional process operating through commensuration, bundling and contractual duration. The article argues that low‐visibility infrastructures such as bus shelters are key sites where neoliberal urban governance is rendered durable, binding everyday public space to market performance while narrowing democratic contestation over public responsibility.

**摘要：** 在英国各城市，日常街道基础设施越来越多地依靠创收性特许经营来维持，从而使维护工作脱离市政直接预算。本文考察了在长期紧缩背景下，格拉斯哥的街道设施如何逐步被重组为一种资产化装置（assetizing apparatus）。通过对委员会记录、采购文件和交通管理机构合同进行微观政治经济追踪，本文揭示了维护责任如何从公共预算转移至广告资助的收入流，经由采购被标准化，并通过特许经营组合得以整合。资产化并非公共空间的骤然私有化，而是一种通过通约化（commensuration）、捆绑和合同期限运作的累积性制度过程。本文认为，公交候车亭等低可见度基础设施是新自由主义城市治理得以持久化的关键场所，它们将日常公共空间与市场绩效绑定，同时压缩了围绕公共责任的民主争论空间。

### [125] Tethered Urbanism: Governing Presence Without Protection in Monrovia, Liberia

**系留城市主义：利比里亚蒙罗维亚的无保护在场治理**

- 作者：Phillip Garjay Innis
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70247](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70247)
- 主题：城市空间、社会分异与不平等；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：系留城市主义、非正规住区、气候适应、民族志

**Abstract:** In Monrovia, Liberia, a seawall promised to West Point and delayed for years leaves residents neither protected nor removed. This paper develops tethered urbanism, a governing logic that holds a population in the city while the conditions of its presence remain unresolved, producing suspended settlement. Drawing on 14 months of ethnography in West Point and Monrovia's zogo zones, with a Sinkor contrast, it identifies three processes: recursive saturation, through which deferral reproduces itself as promises accumulate; relational carcerality, through which conditional care constrains exit; and reproductive improvisation, through which survival labour can be captured as an alibi for non‐provision. The circuit emerges without central design, yet distributes its costs through structured power asymmetry, as actors differ in their capacity to exit. It absorbs collective refusal, which marks its limit, not an exit. Because replacing actors would leave the circuit intact, accountability lies chiefly in the conditions that make suspension renewable.

**摘要：** 在利比里亚蒙罗维亚，一道承诺为西点（West Point）修建却拖延多年的海堤，使居民既未得到保护，也未被迁离。本文提出“系留城市主义”（tethered urbanism）概念，即一种将人口留滞于城市、而其在场条件却始终悬而未决的治理逻辑，由此产生悬置性定居。基于在西点和蒙罗维亚“zogo”区为期14个月的民族志研究，并以Sinkor作为对照，本文识别出三个过程：递归饱和，即延宕随着承诺的累积而自我再生产；关系性监禁，即有条件的照护限制了退出；以及再生产性即兴，即生存劳动可能被挪用为不提供服务的借口。这一回路并非源于中央设计，却通过结构化的权力不对称分配其成本，因为各行动者退出的能力各不相同。它吸纳了集体拒绝——集体拒绝标示着其边界，而非出口。由于替换行动者并不会使这一回路瓦解，问责主要应指向那些使悬置状态得以不断延续的条件。

### [126] Karachi in/and Sindh: Articulations, Disarticulations and the Eternal Reciprocity of Tears

**卡拉奇在/与信德：接合、脱节与眼泪的永恒互惠**

- 作者：Ayyaz Mallick
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70235](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70235)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：葛兰西、卡拉奇、空间想象、族群政治

**Abstract:** This paper develops a Gramscian sensitivity towards spatial claims and popular imaginaries to explore intertwined urban and national questions in Pakistan. The relationship between Karachi, Pakistan's largest city, and wider Sindh province animates intense contestation. Post‐1970s, this generated exclusivist ethnic politics, mediated through reified spatial divisions and claims over city, urbanity and nation. By analysing cultural and political productions, this paper considers shifting registers of urbanity, land and nation in the present conjuncture of crisis and dispossession. It delineates different articulations of urban and national questions, their slippages and contradictions, with an eye towards more inclusive, people‐centred resolutions of the Karachi–Sindh relationship. Drawing on Gramsci, the paper foregrounds the constitutive role of spatial imaginaries and practices in forging hegemonic projects. It thus calls attention to the situated spatial claims (including over city, urbanity and nation) produced by processes of urbanization and dispossession and the responsibility of intellectual‐cultural productions therein.

**摘要：** 本文发展出一种葛兰西式的敏感性，以关注空间诉求与大众想象，进而探讨巴基斯坦相互交织的城市问题与民族问题。巴基斯坦最大城市卡拉奇与更广阔的信德省之间的关系引发了激烈争夺。1970年代以后，这种争夺催生了排他性的族群政治，并以物化的空间区隔以及对城市、城市性与民族的诉求为中介。通过分析文化与政治生产，本文考察了在当前危机与剥夺的局势中城市性、土地与民族的不断变化的表述层面。本文勾勒出城市问题与民族问题的不同接合方式及其滑移与矛盾，着眼于以更具包容性、以人为本的方式解决卡拉奇—信德关系。借鉴葛兰西，本文突出了空间想象与空间实践在锻造霸权计划中的构成性作用，从而呼吁关注由城市化与剥夺过程所生产的情境化空间诉求（包括对城市、城市性与民族的诉求），以及知识—文化生产在其中所承担的责任。

### [127] The Afterlives of Homeland: Black Veteran Homelessness and Racial Geographies of Unbelonging

**家园的余生：黑人退伍军人无家可归与非归属的种族地理**

- 作者：Rupak Shrestha
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70236](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70236)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：黑人地理学、无家可归、退伍军人、家园安全

**Abstract:** This article examines how Black veterans experiencing homelessness navigate the racialized geographies of “homeland” in the United States. Drawing on ethnographic fieldwork with Walter (pseudonym), a 65‐year‐old Black Vietnam veteran living unhoused in Boulder, Colorado, I argue that the US homeland emerges in his experience as a racialized field of continual displacement. Walter's everyday life reveals a dissonance between the homeland's promise of security and his growing loss of faith in the institutions charged with providing care and protection. Bringing Black Geographies into conversation with scholarship on homeland security and homelessness, the article traces how Walter's memories of anti‐Black violence in Mississippi and Vietnam, his refusal to engage with the Department of Veterans Affairs, and his reliance on a fragile power wheelchair illuminate what I conceptualize as racialized homeland insecurity. His humor and reflections on survival also reveal forms of Black spatial knowledge through which he insists on living otherwise.

**摘要：** 本文考察了经历无家可归的黑人退伍军人如何应对美国“家园”（homeland）的种族化地理。基于对Walter（化名）——一位65岁、在科罗拉多州博尔德露宿的黑人越战老兵——的民族志田野工作，本文认为，在他的经验中，美国家园呈现为一个持续流离失所的种族化场域。Walter的日常生活揭示了家园的安全承诺与其对负责提供照护和保护的机构日益丧失信任之间的不协调。本文将黑人地理学（Black Geographies）与国土安全和无家可归研究相对话，追溯Walter对密西西比和越南反黑人暴力的记忆、他拒绝与退伍军人事务部打交道的态度，以及他对一辆脆弱电动轮椅的依赖，以阐明本文所概念化的“种族化家园不安全”。他的幽默以及对生存的反思也揭示了黑人空间知识的形式，借此他坚持以另一种方式生活。

### [128] Ecological Statecraft for Hegemonic Visions: The Governance of Chengdu Eco‐Zone Over Two Decades

**服务于霸权愿景的生态治国术：成都生态区二十年治理**

- 作者：Handuo Deng, Fulong Wu, Fangzhu Zhang
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70229](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70229)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：城市政治生态学、生态文明、生态治国术、成都

**Abstract:** This study advances urban political ecology (UPE) by examining how ecological space production has become a key terrain for pursuing a hegemonic project of ecological civilisation in China. ‘Ecological statecraft’ provides a framework for examining how the ecological visions are materialised in the Chengdu Eco‐Zone. The area transformed from a landscape of mixed villages and construction in the 2000s to a hybrid of greenways and farmlands in the 2020s. These dramatic ‘shifts’ reveal a persistent logic of the ruling party, even as specific visions are continually reshaped in response to changing domestic and external conditions. Our analysis situates state politics within broader UPE debates, particularly historical materialist and Southern everyday‐state perspectives, and brings them into dialogue with Chinese UPE scholarship on state power, land politics and ecological territorialisation. In particular, combining statecraft and everyday‐state perspectives reveals how state‐dominated ecological governance is increasingly co‐shaped by grassroots interests and practices.

**摘要：** 本研究通过考察生态空间生产如何成为中国推进生态文明这一霸权计划的关键场域，推进了城市政治生态学（UPE）研究。“生态治国术”（ecological statecraft）为考察生态愿景如何在成都生态区中得以物质化提供了一个框架。该地区从2000年代村庄与建设用地混杂的景观，转变为2020年代绿道与农田交织的混合景观。这些剧烈“转变”揭示了执政党一以贯之的逻辑，即便具体愿景随着国内外条件的变化而不断重塑。本文将国家政治置于更广泛的城市政治生态学争论之中，尤其是历史唯物主义与南方日常国家视角，并使之与中国城市政治生态学关于国家权力、土地政治和生态领域化的研究展开对话。特别地，将治国术与日常国家视角相结合，揭示出国家主导的生态治理如何日益受到基层利益与实践的共同塑造。

### [129] When Donor Worldviews Rule: External Subjectivity and the Failure of REDD + in Indonesia

**当捐助方世界观主导时：外部主体性与REDD+在印度尼西亚的失败**

- 作者：Karno B. Batiran, Fatwa Faturachmat, Micah Fisher et al.
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70253](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70253)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：REDD+、外部主体性、去政治化、印度尼西亚

**Abstract:** Reducing Emissions from Deforestation and Forest Degradation (REDD+) has become a central instrument of global climate governance, yet its ability to address the main drivers of deforestation remains contested. Political ecology has shown how REDD+ reproduces neoliberal conservation and unequal power relations but has paid less attention to why donor‐funded interventions consistently prioritize politically manageable activities over corporate actors responsible for most forest loss. Drawing on 7 years of longitudinal research in Berau Regency, Indonesia, this paper advances the concept of external subjectivity to explain how donor worldviews systematically redirect environmental governance away from the political‐economic drivers of deforestation. Based on participant observation, interviews, document analysis, and critical discourse analysis, we show that REDD+ focused on community‐based interventions while largely avoiding corporate concessions driving landscape‐scale forest conversion. This apparent implementation failure reflects a broader process of depoliticization through which donor priorities become institutionalized across multiple governance levels.

**摘要：** 减少毁林和森林退化所致排放（REDD+）已成为全球气候治理的核心工具，但其应对毁林主要驱动因素的能力仍存争议。政治生态学已揭示REDD+如何再生产新自由主义保护和不平等的权力关系，却较少关注为何捐助方资助的干预措施总是优先选择政治上易于管理的活动，而非造成大部分森林损失的企业行动者。基于在印度尼西亚贝劳县（Berau Regency）为期7年的纵向研究，本文提出“外部主体性”（external subjectivity）概念，用以解释捐助方世界观如何系统性地将环境治理引离毁林的政治经济驱动因素。基于参与式观察、访谈、文献分析和批判话语分析，我们表明，REDD+聚焦于社区层面的干预，而在很大程度上回避了推动景观尺度森林转化的企业特许经营地。这一表面上的执行失败反映了一个更广泛的去政治化过程，捐助方的优先事项借此在多个治理层级中被制度化。

### [130] When Resistance Reinforces Power: Authoritarian Populism, Extractivism, and Ecological Struggle in Mount Ida

**当抵抗强化权力：伊达山的威权民粹主义、开采主义与生态斗争**

- 作者：Hayriye Özen
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70251](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70251)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：威权民粹主义、开采主义、生态斗争、土耳其

**Abstract:** Drawing on mass protests against a gold mining project in Mount Ida, Türkiye, this article interrogates contradictory dynamics of ecological struggles in authoritarian‐populist regimes. Based on qualitative analysis of movement documents, media coverage, and published activist accounts, it examines how protests—capable of forcing a temporary state retreat—failed to crystallize into a broader struggle against extractivism. Situated conjuncturally, the mobilizations marked a moment in which disruption and hegemonic reproduction unfolded in tandem. By articulating ecological concerns through nationalist themes, the movement enabled broad mobilization and briefly disrupted the AKP's neoliberal extractivism by exposing its contradictions. Yet this same articulation ultimately reinscribed the struggle within authoritarian populism, foreclosing the formation of a counter‐hegemonic project capable of uniting heterogeneous groups. The Mount Ida case thus shows that appropriating populist signifiers such as nationalism may be necessary to challenge populist power, yet simultaneously risks reinforcing the order it seeks to contest.

**摘要：** 本文以土耳其伊达山（Mount Ida）反对金矿项目的大规模抗议为例，探讨威权民粹主义政体下生态斗争的矛盾动态。基于对运动文件、媒体报道和已发表的活动人士叙述的定性分析，本文考察了这些能够迫使国家暂时退让的抗议，为何未能凝聚为一场更广泛的反开采主义（extractivism）斗争。从局势（conjuncture）视角来看，这些动员标志着一个破坏与霸权再生产同时展开的时刻。通过以民族主义主题表达生态关切，该运动实现了广泛动员，并通过揭示正义与发展党（AKP）新自由主义开采主义的矛盾而短暂地扰乱了它。然而，正是这种接合最终将斗争重新纳入威权民粹主义框架之内，阻断了能够联合异质群体的反霸权计划的形成。因此，伊达山案例表明，挪用民族主义等民粹主义能指或许是挑战民粹主义权力所必需的，但同时也有强化其试图挑战的秩序的风险。

### [131] Epistemic Civic Death: Knowledge Infrastructures, Disciplinary Hegemony, and the Destruction of Critical Geographies

**认识论意义上的公民死亡：知识基础设施、学科霸权与批判地理学的毁灭**

- 作者：İlhan Kaya
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70234](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70234)
- 主题：批判理论、知识生产与学科反思；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：认识论公民死亡、知识基础设施、学术自由、土耳其地理学

**Abstract:** Accounts of authoritarian assault on universities center the scholar: dismissed, detained, exiled, reduced to civic death. This paper moves from the knower to the conditions of knowing. I call their destruction epistemic civic death : the dismantling of infrastructures that make critical knowledge collectively possible, such as platforms, pedagogies, and networks of trust and citation. It addresses a blind spot across academic freedom, geographies of knowledge, and comparative authoritarianism. Reading Foucault and Gramsci through Mbembe, I trace a disciplinary necropolitics where the state supplies the instrument of exception and a hegemonic discipline the target. My case is Turkish geography, built to serve a national imaginary and silent on class, ethnicity, and gender. The critical knowledge infrastructure built around the Turkish Association of Geographers was dissolved by emergency decree in 2016. I write from inside that closure. The association reopened in 2018; the community did not. An administrative decision can restore an institution, not the infrastructure of knowing it sustained.

**摘要：** 关于威权势力攻击大学的论述以学者为中心：他们被解雇、被拘押、被流放，沦为“公民死亡”。本文将关注点从认知者转向认知的条件。我将对这些条件的摧毁称为“认识论公民死亡”（epistemic civic death）：即对使批判性知识得以集体生成的基础设施的拆解，如平台、教学法以及信任与引用网络。本文回应了学术自由、知识地理学和比较威权主义研究中共同存在的一个盲点。通过姆贝姆贝（Mbembe）来阅读福柯与葛兰西，我追溯了一种学科性的死亡政治（necropolitics）：国家提供例外的工具，霸权学科提供目标。我的案例是土耳其地理学——一门为服务民族想象而建立、对阶级、族群和性别保持沉默的学科。围绕土耳其地理学家协会建立起来的批判性知识基础设施于2016年被紧急状态法令解散。我从这一关闭事件的内部写作。该协会于2018年重新开放，但学术共同体却未能恢复。行政决定可以恢复一个机构，却无法恢复它曾维系的认知基础设施。

### [132] Hidden Fractures: Communal Capitalist Urban Transformation in Post‐Protest Delhi

**隐蔽的裂痕：抗议后德里的社群主义资本主义城市转型**

- 作者：Farah Farooqi
- 发表：2026-11｜Vol. 58(6)｜DOI: [10.1111/anti.70238](https://doi.org/10.1111/anti.70238)
- 主题：城市空间、社会分异与不平等；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：社群主义资本主义、威权城市主义、空间隔离、德里

**Abstract:** This paper examines how the 2019–2020 Shaheen Bagh protests in Delhi, a landmark movement against India's Citizenship Amendment Act, accelerated urban transformations that extend well beyond the protest site. Drawing on ten months of comparative ethnographic fieldwork (2022–2023) in Shaheen Bagh, Jaitpur and Jasola Vihar, the study shows how communal capitalism, a mode of rule in which Hindu nationalist domination directs capitalist development, reshaped urban space and social relations in the protest's aftermath. The paper reads the post‐protest period through a Foucauldian account of visibility and discipline joined to the Symposium's concept of communal capitalism: the knowledge produced about a ‘dangerous’ neighbourhood becomes the basis both for its containment and for the value extracted from it. First, it shows how an initial show of sovereign force was joined by pervasive forms of control, including heightened state surveillance and community self‐regulation. Second, it traces how the exclusion of Muslims was made productive, with paradoxical results: while offering new economic opportunities to some Muslims, these dynamics also deepen spatial segregation and internal stratification. Third, it follows these processes across three localities, whose different patterns are shaped by class composition, property relations and histories of communal interaction. The findings contribute to debates on authoritarian urbanism, on Muslim urban experiences in contemporary India and on communal capitalism. The paper argues that moments of political resistance may accelerate patterns of spatial segregation while spurring new forms of economic and social adaptation in marginalised communities.

**摘要：** 本文考察了2019–2020年德里沙欣巴格（Shaheen Bagh）抗议——一场反对印度《公民身份修正法》的标志性运动——如何加速了远超抗议地点本身的城市转型。基于2022–2023年在沙欣巴格、杰特布尔（Jaitpur）和贾索拉维哈尔（Jasola Vihar）开展的为期十个月的比较民族志田野工作，本研究揭示了社群主义资本主义（communal capitalism）——一种由印度教民族主义支配引导资本主义发展的统治模式——如何在抗议之后重塑了城市空间和社会关系。本文通过福柯关于可见性与规训的论述，并结合本专题研讨所提出的社群主义资本主义概念，来解读抗议后时期：关于一个“危险”街区所生产的知识，既成为对其加以遏制的基础，也成为从中榨取价值的基础。首先，本文表明，最初的主权武力展示与弥漫性的控制形式相结合，包括强化的国家监控和社区自我规制。其次，本文追溯了对穆斯林的排斥如何被转化为具有生产性的力量，并产生了悖论性结果：这些动态在为部分穆斯林提供新经济机会的同时，也加深了空间隔离和内部分层。第三，本文在三个地区追踪这些过程，其不同模式受到阶级构成、产权关系以及社群互动历史的影响。研究结果为关于威权城市主义、当代印度穆斯林城市经验以及社群主义资本主义的讨论作出了贡献。本文认为，政治抵抗的时刻可能会加速空间隔离的模式，同时在边缘化社群中催生新的经济与社会适应形式。

## Eurasian Geography and Economics

*本期收录：8 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [133] Social justice in spatial change: mechanisms of land rent dissipation and collective spatial governance in common urban communities in China’s high-density metropolises

**空间变迁中的社会正义：中国高密度大都市普通城市社区的土地租金耗散机制与集体空间治理**

- 作者：Jieming Zhu
- 发表：2026-11-19｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/15387216.2026.2764063](https://doi.org/10.1080/15387216.2026.2764063)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策；城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：土地租金耗散、集体空间治理、社会正义、高密度城市
