### [134] Robotics applications and population migration: evidence from China

**机器人应用与人口迁移：来自中国的证据**

- 作者：Lan Zhou, Yi Zhang
- 发表：2026-11-13｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/15387216.2026.2752425](https://doi.org/10.1080/15387216.2026.2752425)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：工业机器人、人口迁移、就业结构、中国城市

**Abstract:** The application of industrial robots will create jobs while replacing the labor force, and the resulting change in employment opportunities may create frequent population movement, affecting the pattern of population distribution in various places. This study, based on 2006–19 panel data of Chinese prefecture-level cities and industry robot stock data from the International Federation of Robotics, provides evidence on the relationship between the introduction of industrial robots and China-specific population migration, revealing that industrial robots have a significant negative effect on net outflow migration and a significant positive effect on de facto population size. Industrial robots primarily affect labor by altering a city’s employment structure, and this impact diminishes as the intensity of industrial robots increases, manifesting as a “robot dividend.” An in-depth analysis of household registration among the migrant population reveals that industrial robots primarily reduce the outflow of local registered residents and the inflow of non-local registered residents, thereby decreasing net outflow migration. Finally, higher levels of urbanization help to curb population outflow.

**摘要：** 工业机器人的应用在替代劳动力的同时也会创造就业，由此引发的就业机会变化可能导致频繁的人口流动，进而影响各地的人口分布格局。本研究基于2006–2019年中国地级市面板数据和国际机器人联合会（IFR）的行业机器人存量数据，为工业机器人引入与中国特有的人口迁移之间的关系提供了证据，揭示出工业机器人对人口净流出具有显著的负向影响，对常住人口规模具有显著的正向影响。工业机器人主要通过改变城市就业结构来影响劳动力，且这种影响随着工业机器人密度的提高而递减，表现为一种“机器人红利”。对流动人口户籍情况的深入分析表明，工业机器人主要减少了本地户籍居民的流出和非本地户籍居民的流入，从而降低了人口净流出。最后，较高的城市化水平有助于抑制人口流出。

### [135] Homemaking amid uncertainty: Russian newcomers and host communities in post-2022 Tbilisi

**不确定性中的家园营造：2022年后第比利斯的俄罗斯新移民与东道社区**

- 作者：Mariam Darchiashvili
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/15387216.2026.2752284](https://doi.org/10.1080/15387216.2026.2752284)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：家园营造、俄罗斯移民、第比利斯、归属

**Abstract:** This article examines homemaking practices among both Russian newcomers and host communities in Tbilisi, Georgia, exploring how change, uncertainty, and migrant-host relations shape the making and unmaking of home. Drawing on ethnographic fieldwork conducted in 2023–24 with socially aware, economically non-privileged Russian migrants who oppose the war in Ukraine, and ongoing participant observation in Tbilisi, the article traces two interconnected processes: how host community responses to Russian migration constitute their own forms of homemaking and claiming of belonging, and how some Russian migrants enact quiet, often non-material homemaking practices – walking familiar routes, building online communities, participating in Tbilisi protests – amid constrained and contested circumstances. Situating homemaking at the intersection of migration studies and critical geographies of home, the article argues that home is not a possession but a relational and contested practice, unfolding across mobilities, memory, politics, and everyday life. Central to this argument is the role of non-human actors – streets, urban green spaces, animals, and digital platforms – as active participants in homemaking. The analysis demonstrates that contingent homes, shaped by uncertainty and precariousness, characterize not only migrant experiences but also the unsettled belonging of host communities confronting rapid social transformation in the context of reversed migration. These two forms of homemaking are not symmetrical, they are shaped by historical legacies of occupation and unequal relations of power, yet both constitute active, relational responses to displacement and change.

**摘要：** 本文考察了格鲁吉亚第比利斯的俄罗斯新移民与东道社区的家园营造（homemaking）实践，探讨变化、不确定性以及移民—东道主关系如何塑造家园的建构与解构。基于2023–2024年对具有社会意识、经济上不具特权、反对乌克兰战争的俄罗斯移民开展的民族志田野工作，以及在第比利斯持续进行的参与式观察，本文追溯了两个相互关联的过程：东道社区对俄罗斯移民的回应如何构成其自身的家园营造与归属主张形式；以及部分俄罗斯移民如何在受限且充满争议的处境中实践安静的、往往是非物质性的家园营造——走熟悉的路线、建立线上社群、参与第比利斯的抗议活动。本文将家园营造置于移民研究与家园批判地理学的交汇处，认为家园并非一种占有物，而是一种关系性的、充满争议的实践，展开于流动性、记忆、政治与日常生活之中。这一论点的核心是非人类行动者——街道、城市绿地、动物和数字平台——作为家园营造积极参与者的作用。分析表明，由不确定性和不稳定性所塑造的偶然性家园，不仅是移民经验的特征，也体现在东道社区在逆向迁移背景下面对快速社会转型时那种未定的归属之中。这两种家园营造形式并不对称，它们受到占领的历史遗产和不平等权力关系的塑造，但二者都构成了对流离失所与变迁的积极的、关系性的回应。

### [136] Social disciplining and civilising processes in China: the politics of morality and the morality of politics

**中国的社会规训与文明化进程：道德的政治与政治的道德**

- 作者：W. John Morgan
- 发表：2026-09-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/15387216.2026.2741736](https://doi.org/10.1080/15387216.2026.2741736)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：社会规训、文明化进程、道德政治、中国

### [137] Marriage-linked housing demand after the boom: changing housing pathways in Chongqing, China

**繁荣之后的婚姻相关住房需求：中国重庆住房路径的变化**

- 作者：Weijie Hu, Jo Kuys
- 发表：2026-09-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/15387216.2026.2741738](https://doi.org/10.1080/15387216.2026.2741738)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：婚房、住房需求、房地产下行、重庆

**Abstract:** China’s property downturn has weakened speculative demand, yet marriage-linked housing demand remains active along specific tenure and locational pathways. Using Chongqing as a case study of inland China, this Research Note examines how hunfang (婚房), or “marriage-linked housing,” redirects some households under weaker market conditions. The Research Note draws on qualitative fieldwork conducted between April 2023 and July 2024, including 45 semi-structured interviews and participant observation. In Chongqing, hunfang ties marriageability to property ownership, gendered expectations of male provision, parental finance, and bargaining over property title and future care after confidence in rising prices has weakened. The analysis treats hunfang as one mechanism identified within the Chongqing case study. Selective demand refers here to housing demand that remains active for particular tenures and locations without restoring market-wide expansion. Four pathways are identified: small owner-occupied flats in lower-cost housing developments on Chongqing’s urban periphery, public rental housing, long-term private renting near employment, and home purchase in county seats and smaller cities. The Research Note shifts attention from aggregate market decline to the demands that remain active within specific regional housing systems. For international housing research, the Chongqing case adds a post-boom account of how a socially embedded ownership test continues to influence tenure and location choices after expectations of capital growth weaken.

**摘要：** 中国房地产市场下行削弱了投机性需求，但与婚姻相关的住房需求仍沿着特定的产权形式和区位路径保持活跃。本研究简报以重庆作为中国内陆的案例，考察“婚房”（hunfang，即与婚姻相关的住房）如何在较弱的市场条件下引导部分家庭的住房选择。研究基于2023年4月至2024年7月开展的定性田野工作，包括45次半结构化访谈和参与式观察。在重庆，在对房价上涨的信心减弱之后，婚房仍将婚配资格与房产所有权、男性供养的性别化期待、父母出资以及围绕房产产权和未来养老照护的协商联系在一起。本分析将婚房视为在重庆案例中识别出的一种机制。此处的“选择性需求”指在特定产权形式和区位上仍保持活跃、但未能恢复全市场扩张的住房需求。研究识别出四条路径：重庆城市边缘低成本住房开发项目中的小户型自住房、公共租赁住房、在就业地附近的长期私人租赁，以及在县城和较小城市购房。本研究简报将关注点从总体市场下滑转向特定区域住房体系中仍保持活跃的需求。对于国际住房研究而言，重庆案例补充了一个繁荣后时期的叙述，说明在资本增值预期减弱之后，一种嵌入社会的所有权检验如何继续影响产权形式和区位选择。

### [138] Liminal ecotones in Eurasian and Afro-Asian geopolitics: spatial inserts between order-maintenance and power-projection

**欧亚与亚非地缘政治中的阈限交错带：介于秩序维护与权力投射之间的空间嵌入**

- 作者：Nik Hynek, Levon Ter-Ghazaryan
- 发表：2026-09-11｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/15387216.2026.2724947](https://doi.org/10.1080/15387216.2026.2724947)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；全球化、地缘经济与产业网络｜关键词：阈限交错带、主权、地缘政治、走廊

**Abstract:** We propose “liminal ecotones” to study endogenous change in the Westphalian sovereign model by coupling liminality’s reversible thresholds with the ecotone’s edge-effects and filtering. We operationalize this through A–V–E–R: Anchor (system-serving conflict de-escalation), Vector (alternative-order projection articulated within host law), Edge-effects (intensified exchanges with selective filtering), and Reversibility (incomplete contracting that splits use- from residual-control rights). The heuristic renders transgression admissible, showing sovereignty as allocative, uneven, and elastic rather than binary. We apply it to three heterogeneous inserts: China’s base in Djibouti tests military-logistical projection; the proposed Zangezur corridor tests infrastructural-connectivity and pan-Turkic de-escalation-through-trade; and Palestinian diplomatic representation tests recognition-seeking under incomplete sovereignty. Each enters through a public-goods anchor while projecting an alternative political order. Together they show that structural change need not be exogenous or macro-level: bounded, selectively-permeable sites accelerate power, trade, and recognition from within while remaining reversible.

**摘要：** 我们提出“阈限交错带”（liminal ecotones）概念，通过将阈限性的可逆门槛与生态交错带的边缘效应和过滤作用相结合，来研究威斯特伐利亚主权模式的内生变化。我们通过A–V–E–R框架将其操作化：锚定（Anchor，服务于体系的冲突降级）、向量（Vector，在东道国法律框架内表达的替代性秩序投射）、边缘效应（Edge-effects，伴随选择性过滤的强化交流）以及可逆性（Reversibility，将使用权与剩余控制权分离的不完全契约）。这一启发式框架使越界变得可被接纳，表明主权是分配性的、不均衡的、具有弹性的，而非二元的。我们将其应用于三个异质性的空间嵌入：中国在吉布提的基地检验军事—后勤投射；拟议中的赞格祖尔走廊（Zangezur corridor）检验基础设施互联互通以及通过贸易实现泛突厥降级；巴勒斯坦外交代表机构则检验不完全主权下的承认诉求。每一个案例都通过公共产品锚定进入，同时投射一种替代性政治秩序。它们共同表明，结构性变化不必是外生的或宏观层面的：有边界的、选择性渗透的场所能够从内部加速权力、贸易与承认的进程，同时保持可逆性。

### [139] China in global biodiversity governance: normative engagement at COP15

**全球生物多样性治理中的中国：COP15上的规范参与**

- 作者：Jesse Rodenbiker, Juliet Lu, Tyler Harlan
- 发表：2026-09-11｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/15387216.2026.2730997](https://doi.org/10.1080/15387216.2026.2730997)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：生物多样性治理、生态文明、COP15、生态保护红线

**Abstract:** China served as President of the Fifteenth Conference of the Parties to the Convention on Biological Diversity (COP15). The event, which was held in two parts in 2021–22, was a major milestone in global environmental governance and a watershed moment for China in its efforts to green its global image. Through a collaborative event ethnography of COP15, we analyze China’s efforts to influence global environmental norms and how such efforts were perceived by participants. We examine China’s pursuit of discursive influence through the concept of “ecological civilization,” and pursuit of policy influence through the promotion of China’s systems of ecological redlines and national parks. We find that China’s efforts met with mixed reception. Ecological civilization remained a contested discourse among COP15 participants, whereas China’s governance models, particularly ecological redlines and national parks, were broadly viewed as valuable policy innovations. These findings suggest that China’s normative engagement is advancing less through the diffusion of environmental ideas than through growing international appeal of its environmental policies and biodiversity governance practices. Our discussion further examines discursive resonance, institutionalization, and the spatialization of policies and practices. We conclude by reflecting on what COP15 reveals about China’s role in global biodiversity governance and the relationship between environmental governance and normative engagement.

**摘要：** 中国担任《生物多样性公约》第十五次缔约方大会（COP15）主席国。该会议于2021–2022年分两阶段举行，是全球环境治理的重要里程碑，也是中国努力塑造绿色全球形象的分水岭时刻。通过对COP15的协作式事件民族志（collaborative event ethnography），我们分析了中国影响全球环境规范的努力以及参会者对这些努力的看法。我们考察了中国通过“生态文明”概念追求话语影响力，以及通过推广中国的生态保护红线和国家公园体系追求政策影响力的做法。研究发现，中国的努力获得了褒贬不一的反响。生态文明在COP15参会者中仍是一种存在争议的话语，而中国的治理模式，尤其是生态保护红线和国家公园，则被普遍视为有价值的政策创新。这些发现表明，中国的规范参与与其说是通过环境理念的扩散来推进，不如说是通过其环境政策和生物多样性治理实践日益增长的国际吸引力来推进。我们的讨论进一步考察了话语共鸣、制度化以及政策与实践的空间化。最后，我们反思了COP15所揭示的中国在全球生物多样性治理中的角色，以及环境治理与规范参与之间的关系。

### [140] “It just isn’t happening”: emotional obstacles in Russo-Georgian queer interactions in post-February 2022 Georgia

**“就是没有发生”：2022年2月后格鲁吉亚俄格酷儿互动中的情感障碍**

- 作者：Talia Kollek, David Rypel
- 发表：2026-08-27｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1080/15387216.2026.2721700](https://doi.org/10.1080/15387216.2026.2721700)
- 主题：移民、种族与身份｜关键词：酷儿迁移、情感理论、归属、格鲁吉亚

**Abstract:** Since Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in February 2022, over 100,000 Russian citizens have migrated to Georgia. Among them were a large number of LGBTQ+ people concerned about a rise in legislative homophobia and transphobia. With shared experiences of anti-queer sentiment, one might expect the emergence of a sense of transnational belonging between Georgian and Russian LGBTQ+ people, now encountering one another in Georgia’s cities. Our ethnographic fieldwork with Georgian and Russian queers has shown that this was not the case. While not necessarily hostile to one another, the communities have remained largely siloed, with few instances of overlap. In this article we interrogate why a shared sense of belonging has not emerged. Drawing upon affect theory, we propose that “affectual blockages” resulting from dissonant emotional landscapes have created a barrier between Georgian and Russian queer collectivities, preventing the emergence of a sense of togetherness across the national boundary. Our work contributes insights on emotions as an obstacle to belonging, shifts the focus of queer migration literature to center-to-periphery mobility within the Global East, and adds queer perspectives and experiences to a body of literature on Russian war emigration following the full-scale invasion.

**摘要：** 自2022年2月俄罗斯全面入侵乌克兰以来，已有超过10万名俄罗斯公民移居格鲁吉亚。其中有大量LGBTQ+人士，他们担忧立法层面的恐同和恐跨现象日益加剧。由于共同经历过反酷儿情绪，人们或许会预期，如今在格鲁吉亚城市中相遇的格鲁吉亚与俄罗斯LGBTQ+群体之间会形成一种跨国归属感。然而，我们对格鲁吉亚和俄罗斯酷儿开展的民族志田野工作表明，情况并非如此。尽管彼此并不一定敌对，这两个社群在很大程度上仍相互隔绝，交集甚少。本文探究为何共同的归属感未能出现。借鉴情感理论（affect theory），我们提出，由不协调的情感景观所导致的“情感阻塞”（affectual blockages）在格鲁吉亚与俄罗斯酷儿集体之间筑起了屏障，阻碍了跨越国界的共同感的形成。本研究就情感作为归属障碍提供了见解，将酷儿迁移文献的焦点转向“全球东方”（Global East）内部从中心到边缘的流动，并为全面入侵后俄罗斯战争移民的相关文献补充了酷儿视角与经验。

## International Journal of Urban and Regional Research

*本期收录：8 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [141] GENTRIFYING CAPITAL FROM WITHIN : Rethinking Transnational Gentrification, Spatial Fix and Transnational Migration through Foreign, Urban and Rural Circuits in Kumasi, Ghana

**从内部绅士化的资本：基于加纳库马西外国、城市与乡村循环对跨国绅士化、空间修复与跨国移民的再思考**

- 作者：Lewis Abedi Asante, Christian Narh Opata, Richmond Juvenile Ehwi et al.
- 发表：2026-11-08｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1111/1468-2427.70158](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70158)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：跨国绅士化、空间修复、中国移民、加纳库马西

**Abstract:** In this article, we examine the rapid neighbourhood transformation of Ahodwo in Kumasi, Ghana, where Chinese entrepreneurial migrants have settled over two decades. We draw on qualitative fieldwork to advance arguments that extend the concepts of transnational gentrification, spatial fix, and transnational migration, and demonstrate that the capital driving neighbourhood change in Ahodwo operates through two intersecting circuits: a transnational one, involving the direct investment of Chinese surplus capital in urban businesses, and a domestic one, where profits from informal gold mining (galamsey) in rural and periurban areas are reinvested into urban businesses. This insight deviates from the assumption that transnational gentrification is primarily financed from overseas and brings into view rural–urban capital circuits within the global South. Gentrifying capital is therefore both exogenous and endogenous, interacting in complex ways. The process is also locally curated: local landlords and brokers facilitate the clustering of a foreign tenant class by tailoring commercial and residential spaces, financing neighbourhood security, and producing conditions for a transnational rent gap. These dynamics inflate rents, reprofile tenants, and generate varied displacement forms. Chinese migration in Ahodwo uniquely coproduces capital and urban transformation across foreign, urban, and rural settings, challenging conventional narratives of capital origins, circulation, and gentrification.

**摘要：** 本文考察加纳库马西（Kumasi）阿霍德沃（Ahodwo）街区的快速变迁，二十年来中国创业移民在此定居。我们基于定性田野调查，提出拓展跨国绅士化、空间修复（spatial fix）和跨国移民概念的论点，并表明推动阿霍德沃街区变迁的资本通过两条相互交织的循环运作：一条是跨国循环，涉及中国过剩资本对城市商业的直接投资；另一条是国内循环，即来自农村和城市周边地区非正规金矿开采（galamsey）的利润被再投资于城市商业。这一洞见偏离了跨国绅士化主要由海外资金推动的假设，并揭示了全球南方内部的城乡资本循环。因此，绅士化资本既是外生的也是内生的，二者以复杂方式相互作用。这一过程也经过了地方性的策划：当地房东和中介通过定制商业和居住空间、出资维护街区安全、营造跨国租金差（rent gap）条件，促成外国租户阶层的集聚。这些动态推高了租金，重塑了租户构成，并产生了多样的迁离形式。阿霍德沃的中国移民在外国、城市和乡村情境中独特地共同生产了资本与城市转型，挑战了关于资本来源、流通和绅士化的传统叙事。

### [142] NAVIGATING UNCERTAINTY IN REGIONAL SOFT PLANNING : The Interpretive Dynamics of Emerging Planning Practices

**在区域软规划中应对不确定性：新兴规划实践的诠释动态**

- 作者：SABRINA FREDIN, THERESE ALDINGE
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1111/1468-2427.70160](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70160)
- 主题：城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：软规划、不确定性、区域规划、瑞典

**Abstract:** This study investigates how uncertainty shapes the emergence and implementation of a new regional soft planning practice. While soft, non‐statutory planning has been widely discussed, less is known about how actors interpret these new regional planning practices and how uncertainty persists beyond formal adoption. The article addresses this gap by conceptualizing uncertainty as foundational, relational and temporal, and examining how these dimensions structure collaboration between a regional authority and its municipalities. Drawing on semi‐structured interviews with municipal and regional planners in Region Östergötland in Sweden, the analysis shows how unclear starting conditions and institutional ambiguity made it difficult for actors to understand the purpose and implications of the new regional planning practice. We demonstrate how uneven capacities between large and small municipalities translated interpretive openness into asymmetrical influence at first, how uncertainty was consciously used as a deliberate tactic to allow for more open discussions, and how uncertainty endured after adoption as actors struggled to relate the non‐binding strategy to statutory planning instruments and implementation responsibilities. We argue that uncertainty is not only a hindrance but a constitutive feature of regional soft planning, simultaneously enabling experimentation and risking reinforcing power asymmetries.

**摘要：** 本研究探讨不确定性如何塑造一种新的区域软规划实践的出现与实施。尽管软性的非法定规划已被广泛讨论，但对于行动者如何诠释这些新的区域规划实践，以及不确定性如何在正式采纳之后持续存在，人们了解较少。本文将不确定性概念化为基础性、关系性和时间性的，并考察这些维度如何构建区域当局与其所辖市镇之间的协作，以弥补这一空白。基于对瑞典东约特兰（Östergötland）地区市镇和区域规划者的半结构式访谈，分析显示，不明确的起始条件和制度模糊性使行动者难以理解新区域规划实践的目的和影响。我们展示了大小市镇之间不均衡的能力如何在初期将诠释的开放性转化为不对称的影响力，不确定性如何被有意识地用作一种策略以促成更开放的讨论，以及在采纳之后，随着行动者难以将这一非约束性战略与法定规划工具和实施责任相联系，不确定性如何持续存在。我们认为，不确定性不仅是一种阻碍，更是区域软规划的构成性特征，它既促成了实验，也可能强化权力不对称。

### [143] TERRITORIAL VALORIZATION : How Positive Stereotypes Advance Suburban Water Insecurity in Detroit

**领域增值：正面刻板印象如何加剧底特律郊区的用水不安全**

- 作者：Dana Kornberg, Mackenzie Taradalsky
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1111/1468-2427.70161](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70161)
- 主题：城市空间、社会分异与不平等｜关键词：领域增值、用水不安全、郊区贫困、底特律

**Abstract:** Suburbs historically enabled and protected white middle‐class wealth in the United States; however, their more recent diversification has included a rise in suburban poverty. This article examines these dynamics through the issue of household water access—a feature of rising suburban impoverishment that deserves greater attention. For two years, the first author participated in a Detroit‐area advocacy group, working together to conduct interviews with a hard‐to‐reach group of suburban residents struggling to afford their water bills. These 15 interviews reveal that water insecurity affects households even in the most affluent suburbs and that access to water assistance and affordability programs has been inadequate and variable. We explain these findings by developing the concept of territorial valorization —the relational counterpart of territorial stigma—to capture the phenomenon whereby certain areas may fail to acknowledge and address social problems in an effort to maintain their historically positive reputations. While territorial valorization has the power to attract investment and resources, we theorize that its positive stereotypes can have negative consequences for residents who fail to meet their demands.

**摘要：** 在美国，郊区在历史上促成并保护了白人中产阶级的财富；然而，郊区近来的多样化也伴随着郊区贫困的上升。本文通过家庭用水获取问题考察这些动态，这是郊区贫困化加剧中值得更多关注的一个特征。第一作者在两年间参与了底特律地区的一个倡导团体，共同对一群难以接触、难以负担水费的郊区居民开展访谈。这15次访谈揭示，即使在最富裕的郊区，用水不安全也影响着家庭，且水费援助和可负担性项目的可及性不足且参差不齐。为解释这些发现，我们提出领域增值（territorial valorization）概念——即领域污名（territorial stigma）的关系性对应物——用以描述某些地区为维护其历史上的正面声誉而未能承认和解决社会问题的现象。尽管领域增值具有吸引投资和资源的力量，我们从理论上认为，其正面刻板印象可能对无法满足这些期望的居民造成负面后果。

### [144] HOUSING RETROFITS IN JUST TRANSITIONS ? Emerging Contradictions in Catalonia, Spain

**公正转型中的住房改造？西班牙加泰罗尼亚的新兴矛盾**

- 作者：Melissa García‐Lamarca
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1111/1468-2427.70159](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70159)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；能源转型与基础设施｜关键词：住房改造、公正转型、脱碳、加泰罗尼亚

**Abstract:** Efforts to decarbonize residential housing are growing globally. Following ambitious targets set by the European Commission's Renovation Wave and subsidized through Covid‐19 recovery funds, Spain's housing retrofit programme deployed EUR 3.4 billion in European grants to retrofit half a million homes between 2022 and 2026. This article uncovers emerging political, economic, environmental and social contradictions around expanding housing retrofits in Spain, with a focus on Catalonia, to explore how funds were disbursed and who stands to benefit. In considering the political economy of housing in just transitions, I draw from two dozen interviews in 2023 with a range of actors, meeting observations and grey literature to highlight contradictions emerging across five domains. These include the territorial distribution of funds, the universal nature of retrofit grants, the treatment of vulnerable populations, the scale of intervention and the nature of the housing retrofit sector. Together the contradictions largely illustrate how public retrofit funding has favoured increased capital circulation alongside reinforced social relations and ideologies of private property ownership and wealth accumulation. While the overall push is towards what I term a Spanish growth economy of housing repair, some contradictions in the five domains point to possibilities for a more transformative just housing transition.

**摘要：** 住宅脱碳的努力正在全球范围内增长。在欧盟委员会“翻新浪潮”（Renovation Wave）设定的雄心勃勃目标下，并借助新冠疫情复苏基金的资助，西班牙住房改造计划在2022年至2026年间投入了34亿欧元欧洲补助资金，用于改造50万套住房。本文以加泰罗尼亚为重点，揭示西班牙扩大住房改造过程中出现的政治、经济、环境和社会矛盾，以探讨资金如何发放以及谁将从中受益。在考察公正转型中的住房政治经济时，我利用2023年对各类行动者开展的二十余次访谈、会议观察和灰色文献，突出了在五个领域中出现的矛盾，包括：资金的地域分配、改造补助的普惠性质、对弱势群体的处理、干预规模以及住房改造行业的性质。总体而言，这些矛盾在很大程度上表明，公共改造资金倾向于促进资本流通的增加，同时强化了私有产权和财富积累的社会关系与意识形态。尽管总体趋势指向我所称的西班牙住房维修增长经济，但五个领域中的一些矛盾也揭示了实现更具变革性的公正住房转型的可能性。

### [145] EMIGRE CITY ‐ MAKING : The Micropolitics of Diasporic Real Estate Investment and Urban Growth in Pakistan

**移民城市营造：巴基斯坦离散群体房地产投资与城市增长的微观政治**

- 作者：Fatima Tassadiq
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1111/1468-2427.70156](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70156)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；移民、种族与身份｜关键词：离散投资、移民城市营造、房地产投机、巴基斯坦

**Abstract:** Contemporary cities in the global South are being reshaped through the intersecting logics of burgeoning real estate sectors and diasporic investment. This article examines the case of Pakistan to explore how emigration trajectories and remittance practices contribute to the transformation of urban landscapes in countries of origin through processes of emigre city‐making. Over the past few decades, the neoliberalizing real estate sector has aligned with shifts in the investment practices of elite emigrants, redirecting remittances from home‐building—once intended for eventual return—to speculative investments in urban real estate. These investments are part of broader, multi‐sited transnational strategies aimed at supporting permanent settlement in North Atlantic countries. Often made during presales, these speculative purchases and accompanying risk‐mitigation strategies help to create a niche market for vacant land, particularly in developments led by the military and its allies. These investments contribute to urbanization in major, second‐tier and emerging cities through low density suburban gated communities. By tracing the logics of such investments, this article sheds light on alternative trajectories of transnational urbanism and neoliberalization, illustrating how land and housing financialization unfolds in postcolonial cities through global flows of non‐corporate private capital, rather than institutional transnational capital and well‐developed financial markets.

**摘要：** 全球南方的当代城市正在新兴房地产部门与离散群体投资相互交织的逻辑中被重塑。本文以巴基斯坦为例，探讨移民轨迹和侨汇实践如何通过移民城市营造（emigre city-making）过程，推动原籍国城市景观的转变。过去几十年间，日益新自由主义化的房地产部门与精英移民投资实践的转变相契合，使侨汇从曾以最终回归为目的的自建住房，转向对城市房地产的投机性投资。这些投资是更广泛的、多地点跨国策略的一部分，旨在支持在北大西洋国家的永久定居。这些投机性购买往往发生在预售阶段，连同相应的风险规避策略，帮助形成了一个闲置土地的利基市场，尤其是在由军方及其盟友主导的开发项目中。这些投资通过低密度的郊区封闭社区，推动了主要城市、二线城市和新兴城市的城市化。通过追溯此类投资的逻辑，本文揭示了跨国城市主义和新自由主义化的另类轨迹，说明在后殖民城市中，土地和住房金融化如何通过非企业私人资本的全球流动展开，而非依托机构化的跨国资本和成熟的金融市场。

### [146] NEGATIVE ASSETS : Settler Colonialism, Speculative Urbanism, and the Moral Economy of Smallholding on Nairobi's Real Estate Frontier

**负资产：内罗毕房地产前沿上的定居者殖民主义、投机城市主义与小农的道德经济**

- 作者：Peter Lockwood, Smith Ouma, Tom Gillespie
- 发表：2026-11-06｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1111/1468-2427.70154](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70154)
- 主题：金融化、住房与房地产；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：负资产、定居者殖民主义、投机城市主义、内罗毕

**Abstract:** The expansion of Nairobi's real estate frontier into its peri‐urban hinterlands creates new opportunities and new dilemmas for smallholder families whose land is skyrocketing in value. Instead of taking the opportunity to become urban rentiers, many smallholders across Nairobi's peripheries are selling their inherited ‘ancestral’ plots in spite of strident taboos about the dangers of alienating patrimonial property. To explain why this is the case, we demonstrate how settler colonial histories of land reform created a demographic of smallholders that possessed land as a ‘negative asset’, never viable for long‐term commercial farming, and to which they became precariously rooted. While land's status as a commodity has been resisted despite titling, its rising value suggests that the commodification process is approaching thorough completion, with meagre plots sold rather than retained. Drawing on ethnographic cases of land sale, the article argues that the combination of settler colonial histories of land reform and the arrival of the real estate frontier works together to erode the moral economy of smallholding, forcing ‘ancestral’ land onto the market.

**摘要：** 内罗毕房地产前沿向城市周边腹地的扩张，为土地价值飞涨的小农家庭带来了新的机遇和新的困境。许多内罗毕周边地区的小农并未借此机会成为城市食利者，而是不顾关于转让祖产之危险的严厉禁忌，出售其继承的“祖传”地块。为解释这一现象，我们展示了定居者殖民时期的土地改革历史如何造就了一个小农群体，他们所拥有的土地是一种“负资产”，从未具备长期商业化耕作的可行性，而他们却岌岌可危地扎根于此。尽管在确权之后土地作为商品的地位仍受到抵制，但其不断上涨的价值表明商品化进程正趋于彻底完成，微薄的地块被出售而非保留。基于土地出售的民族志案例，本文认为，定居者殖民主义的土地改革历史与房地产前沿的到来共同作用，侵蚀了小农的道德经济，迫使“祖传”土地进入市场。

### [147] SPACE AND POLITICAL PARTICIPATION IN POPULAR NEIGHBORHOODS : Insights from the Chilean Urban Uprising

**平民街区的空间与政治参与：来自智利城市起义的洞见**

- 作者：Nicolás Angelcos, Valentina Abufhele
- 发表：2026-11-02｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1111/1468-2427.70152](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70152)
- 主题：城市空间、社会分异与不平等；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：政治参与、城市起义、惯习、智利圣地亚哥

**Abstract:** In this article we examine how space shapes the political dispositions of residents in marginalized neighborhoods and gave form to institutional and contentious participation during the 2019 urban uprising in Chile. Based on qualitative research in two neighborhoods on Santiago's outskirts, we reconstruct residents’ engagement with politics prior to and during the urban uprising. We use Lefebvre's relational concept of space in dialogue with Bourdieu's theory of habitus to understand how space (perceived, conceived, lived) interacts with individual resources, mainly cultural capital. This interaction shows that participation strategies during the revolt stemmed from political dispositions developed over time, having been shaped by the diverse and conflicting ways residents perceive, conceive, and live space. These strategies are also influenced by what Bourdieu terms residents’ ‘volume of capital’. Based on their political dispositions, residents of popular neighborhoods display a varied repertoire of political strategies. These strategies include institutional, collaborative, and oppositional participation, with the aim of resolving their daily material needs, affecting national politics, and challenging the neighborhood's reputation within the city's imagination.

**摘要：** 本文考察空间如何塑造边缘化街区居民的政治倾向，并在2019年智利城市起义期间赋予制度性参与和抗争性参与以具体形式。基于在圣地亚哥郊区两个街区开展的定性研究，我们重构了居民在城市起义之前和期间的政治参与。我们将列斐伏尔（Lefebvre）的关系性空间概念与布迪厄（Bourdieu）的惯习理论对话，以理解空间（感知的、构想的、生活的）如何与个体资源（主要是文化资本）相互作用。这一互动表明，起义期间的参与策略源于长期形成的政治倾向，而这些倾向由居民感知、构想和生活空间的多样且相互冲突的方式所塑造。这些策略还受到布迪厄所称居民“资本总量”的影响。基于其政治倾向，平民街区居民展现出多样的政治策略组合，包括制度性、协作性和对抗性参与，旨在解决日常物质需求、影响国家政治，并挑战街区在城市想象中的声誉。

### [148] ‘ PARTICIPATION IS WHAT WE DO EVERY DAY ’: Unpacking the Meanings and Practices of Participation Addressing Mobility Justice in Rio de Janeiro and Porto Alegre, Brazil

**“参与就是我们每天所做的事”：解析巴西里约热内卢和阿雷格里港应对移动正义的参与意义与实践**

- 作者：Aline Fernandes Barata, Sue Brownill
- 发表：2026-10-29｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1111/1468-2427.70155](https://doi.org/10.1111/1468-2427.70155)
- 主题：健康、移动性与可达性；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：移动正义、参与、反叛规划、巴西

**Abstract:** Participation is a topic that has been widely debated in urban studies. Despite this, we argue in this paper that policies and academic work on mobility planning have paid little attention to the meanings attached to participation and its role in mobility justice. Building on the literature on spaces for participation, Southern theories and insurgent planning, this paper shows that participation has multiple and contested meanings, particularly when addressing mobility injustices in the global South. Based on case studies in Favela Santa Marta, Rio de Janeiro, and Vila Tronco, Porto Alegre, both in Brazil, we provide evidence of alternative meanings of participation and a range of participatory practices exercised by residents with, despite, and against the state. The everyday practices that flow from and interact with these meanings uphold the freedoms to move around, thrive, and dwell, challenging mobility planning and what we call ‘authorized participatory discourse’. Our findings show that while participation with the state is only one part of the story, particularly in contexts of informality and exclusion, the state's and residents’ responses are far from monolithic. We conclude by arguing that a focus on everyday practices can add new insights to the understanding of mobility justice and insurgent planning.

**摘要：** 参与是城市研究中被广泛讨论的话题。尽管如此，本文认为，关于移动规划的政策和学术研究很少关注参与所承载的意义及其在移动正义（mobility justice）中的作用。本文借鉴关于参与空间、南方理论和反叛规划（insurgent planning）的文献，表明参与具有多重且有争议的意义，尤其是在应对全球南方的移动不公时。基于巴西里约热内卢圣玛尔塔贫民窟（Favela Santa Marta）和阿雷格里港维拉通科（Vila Tronco）的案例研究，我们提供了参与的另类意义以及居民与国家合作、不顾国家和对抗国家所践行的一系列参与实践的证据。由这些意义衍生并与之互动的日常实践维护了移动、繁荣和栖居的自由，挑战了移动规划以及我们所称的“授权式参与话语”。研究发现，与国家的合作式参与只是故事的一部分，尤其是在非正规性和排斥的情境中，而国家和居民的回应也远非铁板一块。最后，我们认为，关注日常实践可以为理解移动正义和反叛规划提供新的洞见。

## Geoforum

*本期收录：15 篇｜卷期：Online first / 在线优先*

### [149] Necropolitical ecologies of divestment: corporate withdrawal, structural exclusion, and biotic justice in Isokoland

**撤资的死亡政治生态：伊索科兰的企业退出、结构性排斥与生物正义**

- 作者：Sampson Adese
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104918](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104918)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；能源转型与基础设施｜关键词：死亡政治生态学、石油撤资、生物正义、尼日尔三角洲

**Abstract:** This article examines international oil company (IOC) divestment in Isokoland, Niger Delta, as a process that reorganises rather than resolves extractive violence. Using necropolitical ecology and necroecology as analytical frameworks, it argues that corporate withdrawal from onshore oil assets transfers operational control while dispersing responsibility for historical pollution, livelihood loss, and unresolved remediation. Based on qualitative fieldwork across eight Isoko communities, alongside legal, environmental, corporate, and civil society materials, the article situates divestment within a longer genealogy of land alienation structured by colonial mineral law, the Land Use Act, petroleum licensing, and coercive state enforcement. It shows how Shell, Eni, and related operators’ asset transfers in OML 26, OML 30, and OML 61 reproduced enclave logics by excluding host communities from ownership decisions, accountability mechanisms, and meaningful environmental governance. The article further demonstrates how gas flaring, oil spills, abandoned infrastructure, weakened compensation regimes, and compressed agricultural cycles produce durable necroecological conditions in soil, water, bodies, and livelihoods. Against divestment narratives framed as local content or energy transition, the article advances biotic justice as a framework for enforceable remediation, ecological restoration, political recognition, and historical liability across Niger Delta sacrificed zones.

**摘要：** 本文考察国际石油公司（international oil company, IOC）在尼日尔三角洲伊索科兰（Isokoland）的撤资，将其视为一个重组而非化解采掘暴力的过程。文章以死亡政治生态学（necropolitical ecology）和死亡生态学（necroecology）为分析框架，认为企业从陆上石油资产中撤出在转移运营控制权的同时，也分散了对历史污染、生计丧失和未完成修复的责任。基于在八个伊索科社区开展的质性田野调查，并结合法律、环境、企业和公民社会材料，本文将撤资置于更长的土地剥夺谱系之中，这一谱系由殖民时期矿产法、《土地使用法》（Land Use Act）、石油许可制度和强制性国家执法所建构。文章表明，壳牌（Shell）、埃尼（Eni）及相关运营商在OML 26、OML 30和OML 61油田区块的资产转让，通过将东道社区排除在所有权决策、问责机制和实质性环境治理之外，再生产了飞地逻辑。文章进一步揭示，天然气燃除、石油泄漏、废弃基础设施、被削弱的补偿制度以及被压缩的农业周期，如何在土壤、水体、身体和生计中制造持久的死亡生态状况。针对以本地成分（local content）或能源转型为名的撤资叙事，本文提出生物正义（biotic justice）作为一个框架，以推动尼日尔三角洲各“牺牲区”实现可强制执行的修复、生态恢复、政治承认和历史责任追究。

### [154] From growth machine to integration machine: A historical-institutional perspective of Chinese statecraft under rural revitalization

**从增长机器到整合机器：乡村振兴下中国国家治理术的历史制度视角**

- 作者：Handuo Deng, Shengxi Xin
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104891](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104891)
- 主题：政治地理、国家与地缘政治；城市治理、规划与政策｜关键词：整合机器、增长机器、乡村振兴、国家治理术

**Abstract:** This study analyses the transition trends of China’s spatial governance from a growth machine to an “integration machine,” from an institutional reconfiguration perspective. We propose that while the urban growth machine has dominated spatial politics in China, its institutional foundations also provide the conditions for the emergence of an integration machine. This integration machine reflects a path-dependent logic rooted in the historical Chinese statecraft of integrating fragmented grassroots communities into cohesive territorial governance — an alternative logic to capital-driven growth. Drawing on China’s Rural Revitalization, including its strategic position, policy initiatives, and local implementation, this article reveals how the integration machine is underpinned by the reconfiguration of party, bureaucratic, and fiscal institutions. Meanwhile, new challenges have emerged from the intensifying interplay between institutions and actions during implementation. By conceptualizing the integration machine, the study introduces a new perspective on territorial governance by emphasizing the institutional foundations of statecraft.

**摘要：** 本文从制度重构的视角，分析中国空间治理从增长机器向“整合机器”（integration machine）转变的趋势。我们提出，尽管城市增长机器长期主导着中国的空间政治，但其制度基础也为整合机器的出现提供了条件。整合机器体现了一种路径依赖的逻辑，植根于中国历史上将分散的基层社区整合为有凝聚力的领土治理的国家治理术（statecraft）——这是一种不同于资本驱动增长的替代性逻辑。本文以中国乡村振兴为例，结合其战略定位、政策举措和地方实施，揭示整合机器如何以党、科层和财政制度的重构为支撑。与此同时，在实施过程中制度与行动之间日益加剧的互动也带来了新的挑战。通过对整合机器进行概念化，本研究强调国家治理术的制度基础，为领土治理提供了新的视角。

### [155] The territorial power of appearing and disappearing groundwater conflicts

**地下水冲突显现与消隐的领土权力**

- 作者：David Kuhn
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104835](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104835)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态｜关键词：地下水冲突、水社会领土、尺度重构、权力关系

**Abstract:** • Hydrogeological and power relations keep groundwater conflicts latent. • Conflicts turning manifest require powerful shifts within hydrosocial territories. • This paper reveals how humans and other-than-humans influence conflicts’ trajectories. • Groundwater and long-distance water transfers can trigger, demobilise or shift conflicts. • Solved conflicts can appear again in the future and somewhere else. This article aims for an enhanced understanding of groundwater conflicts. Worldwide, people compete over groundwater. However, this rarely leads to manifest conflicts. Whether a groundwater conflict remains latent or turns manifest, i.e. into an open, direct and hence visible confrontation, relates to hydrogeological processes as well as societal power relations. This article investigates how manifest groundwater conflicts appear and disappear and with which effects for whom. The conceptual lens of hydrosocial territories applied to a groundwater conflict in the Südharz region, Germany, and the Júcar river basin, Spain, enables the deep investigation into the visible and invisible contestations around groundwater quality and quantity. In Südharz, a toxic event co-produced by uranium and a citizen initiative turned long-looming tensions around water supply into a manifest conflict. In Spain, a drought event co-produced by groundwater-river interaction and downstream users led to a manifest conflict between two regions. In both cases, the power of existing large water infrastructures and institutions contributed eventually to the disappearance of the manifest conflict by re-scaling the respective systems of water supply. Thereby, conflicts about the fundamental relations between societies and groundwater have been kept under the surface – for now. In the future, they are very likely to appear somewhere in the newly established hydrosocial territories.

**摘要：** • 水文地质关系与权力关系使地下水冲突保持潜伏状态。• 冲突转为显性需要水社会领土内部发生强有力的变化。• 本文揭示了人类与非人类如何影响冲突的轨迹。• 地下水和长距离调水可以引发、消解或转移冲突。• 已解决的冲突可能在未来、在别处再次出现。本文旨在加深对地下水冲突的理解。在世界范围内，人们围绕地下水展开竞争，但这很少导致显性冲突。地下水冲突究竟保持潜伏还是转为显性——即公开、直接因而可见的对抗——既与水文地质过程有关，也与社会权力关系有关。本文探究显性地下水冲突如何出现和消失，以及对谁产生何种影响。本文将水社会领土（hydrosocial territories）这一概念视角应用于德国南哈尔茨（Südharz）地区和西班牙胡卡尔河（Júcar）流域的地下水冲突，从而深入考察围绕地下水水质和水量的可见与不可见的争夺。在南哈尔茨，由铀与一个公民倡议共同造成的毒性事件，使长期酝酿的供水紧张关系转变为显性冲突。在西班牙，由地下水—河流相互作用与下游用户共同造成的干旱事件，引发了两个地区之间的显性冲突。在这两个案例中，现有大型水利基础设施和制度的权力通过对各自供水系统进行尺度重构，最终促成了显性冲突的消失。由此，关于社会与地下水之间根本关系的冲突暂时被压在了表面之下。未来，它们很可能会在新建立的水社会领土中的某处再次出现。

### [161] Crafting post-growth? A support-structure diagnosis of Jaipur’s craft economy

**打造后增长？斋浦尔手工艺经济的支撑结构诊断**

- 作者：Rishika Mukhopadhyay, Kanika Prajapat, Angelos Varvarousis
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104885](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104885)
- 主题：区域发展、创新与演化经济地理；劳动、生计与日常生活｜关键词：后增长、手工艺经济、支撑结构、斋浦尔

**Abstract:** Post-growth scholarship has often celebrated craft as a privileged site for imagining postcapitalist and post-growth transitions. Yet this discussion remains largely Global North-centred and empirically underdeveloped, drawing mostly on small-scale, elective and niche craft economies rather than large craft sectors where artisanal labour constitutes a major livelihood system. This paper addresses this gap through the case of Jaipur’s craft economy, one of the world’s largest concentrated craft economies. The study draws on semi-structured interviews with craft-sector experts, field visits to craft fairs and markets, participant observation at a socio-ecological enterprise, and documentary analysis conducted between 2023 and 2024. We examine how Jaipur’s craft sector enables, constrains and complicates post-growth-compatible forms of territorial development. To do so, we develop a support-structure diagnosis, understood as an analysis of the institutional, material, financial, cultural, geographical and historical conditions sustaining, transforming or absorbing craft economies into dominant growth-oriented circuits. We map three interrelated support structures operating across multiple scales: top-down institutional structures, bottom-up and intermediary structures, and local conditions. The article shows that Jaipur’s large-scale, decentralised and historically embedded craft economy unsettles simple distinctions between local and global, formal and informal, traditional and entrepreneurial. It requires a more situated understanding of relocalisation, alterity and market integration in post-growth debates. We identify four dynamics through which support structures interact: encouragement, collaboration, suppression and friction. Theoretically, equity, central to post-growth economies, remains a concern for Jaipur. Scale, heterogeneity of informalisation and decentralisation, and multiple axes of power in Jaipur’s craft ecosystem open up a productive challenge for post-growth scholarship.

**摘要：** 后增长研究常将手工艺推崇为想象后资本主义和后增长转型的特殊场域。然而，这一讨论在很大程度上以全球北方为中心，且经验研究不足，主要依托小规模、自主选择型和小众的手工艺经济，而非手工劳动构成主要生计体系的大型手工艺部门。本文以世界上规模最大的集中型手工艺经济之一——斋浦尔（Jaipur）手工艺经济为案例，弥补这一空白。研究基于2023年至2024年间开展的手工艺行业专家半结构式访谈、手工艺集市与市场实地考察、在一家社会生态企业中的参与式观察以及文献分析。我们考察斋浦尔手工艺部门如何促成、制约并复杂化与后增长相兼容的领土发展形式。为此，我们提出一种支撑结构诊断（support-structure diagnosis），即对维系、转化手工艺经济或将其吸纳进主导性增长导向循环的制度、物质、金融、文化、地理和历史条件进行分析。我们描绘了在多个尺度上运作的三类相互关联的支撑结构：自上而下的制度结构、自下而上与中介结构，以及地方条件。文章表明，斋浦尔大规模、去中心化且具有历史嵌入性的手工艺经济，动摇了地方与全球、正式与非正式、传统与创业之间的简单区分，这要求在后增长讨论中对再地方化、另类性和市场整合形成更具情境性的理解。我们识别出支撑结构相互作用的四种动态：鼓励、协作、压制和摩擦。在理论上，作为后增长经济核心的公平问题在斋浦尔仍值得关注。斋浦尔手工艺生态系统中的规模、非正规化和去中心化的异质性以及多重权力轴线，对后增长研究提出了富有成效的挑战。

### [162] Care, connection and preserving the common world in Lebanon

**黎巴嫩的关怀、联结与共同世界的守护**

- 作者：Tiina Järvi
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104919](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104919)
- 主题：批判理论、知识生产与学科反思；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：关怀、共同世界、阿伦特、黎巴嫩

**Abstract:** Lebanon has experienced several concurrent calamities in the first half of 2020 s: economic collapse, Beirut port explosion, and rounds of all-out war as Israel has bombarded the country and caused massive destruction and displacement. All these upheavals have made evident the absence of adequate state responses, leaving a gap that has been filled by other actors who have stepped in to care for Lebanon and its denizens. These practices of care form the basis of this article, which explores how care becomes a way to connect with and protect Lebanon when the political leaders fail to do so. The article builds on fieldwork conducted in Lebanese civil society organisations between 2023 and 2025 and turns to Hannah Arendt to theorise care as a way to preserve and connect with a common world. For Arendt, care embodies amor mundi – love of the world – which commits us to tend it for the sake of the coming generations. I suggest that the activities of care practiced by the people introduced in this article can be read through this prism: they engage in caring to protect the world they view as their home – Lebanon. I further suggest that care becomes a way to connect with that world, to demonstrate its importance but also to create and maintain a connection in a context defined by the absence of the state.

**摘要：** 2020年代上半叶，黎巴嫩经历了多重并发的灾难：经济崩溃、贝鲁特港口爆炸，以及以色列轰炸该国所引发的多轮全面战争，造成了大规模破坏和流离失所。这些动荡都表明国家应对的严重缺位，由此留下的空白由其他介入关怀黎巴嫩及其居民的行动者所填补。这些关怀实践构成了本文的基础，文章探讨在政治领导人未能尽责之时，关怀如何成为联结与保护黎巴嫩的一种方式。本文基于2023年至2025年间在黎巴嫩公民社会组织中开展的田野调查，借助汉娜·阿伦特（Hannah Arendt）的思想，将关怀理论化为一种守护并联结共同世界的方式。在阿伦特看来，关怀体现了“爱世界”（amor mundi），它要求我们为了后代而照料这个世界。我认为，本文所介绍的人们所从事的关怀活动可以透过这一视角来解读：他们通过关怀来守护他们视为家园的世界——黎巴嫩。我进一步提出，在国家缺位的背景下，关怀成为联结这一世界的方式，既彰显其重要性，也建立并维系一种联系。

### [163] Green surveillance: the social implications of harnessing local communities for conservation law enforcement in Namibia

**绿色监控：纳米比亚动员地方社区参与保护执法的社会影响**

- 作者：Ruben Schneider
- 发表：2027-01｜Online first / 在线优先｜DOI: [10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104880](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.geoforum.2026.104880)
- 主题：环境、气候与政治生态；政治地理、国家与地缘政治｜关键词：绿色监控、军事化保护、本体权力、纳米比亚

**Abstract:** In response to the social injustice and counterproductivity attributed to militarised conservation, anti-poaching efforts increasingly focus on ‘inclusivity’ and surveillance. This shift can be explained with the emergence of ontopower, a new form of environmental power that focuses on anticipatory and pre-emptive actions to avoid the virtual, future destruction of nature. This article argues that conservation’s novel combination of inclusivity, surveillance, and ontopower produces a particular mode of conservation governance which I call ‘green surveillance’. Green surveillance captures the open-ended use of military and criminal intelligence approaches to the collection of universal information on environmental crime which target local communities in the pursuit of conservation security. Drawing on ethnographic fieldwork, the article examines what green surveillance looks like in north-west Namibia, where local people are being ‘harnessed’ for conservation law enforcement. By showing how local people experience green surveillance, the article responds to calls to investigate the social implications of community-focused surveillance efforts employed as part of militarised conservation. The analysis reveals that green surveillance can have severe negative social implications for rural communities, such as local instrumentalisation, exploitation of precarious livelihoods, physical and slow violence, social exclusion, breakdown in trust and social cohesion, and false reporting; all contributing to social conflict.

**摘要：** 针对军事化保护（militarised conservation）所带来的社会不公及适得其反的效果，反盗猎工作日益聚焦于“包容性”与监控。这一转变可以用本体权力（ontopower）的出现来解释，后者是一种新的环境权力形式，侧重通过预见性和先发制人的行动来避免潜在的、未来的自然破坏。本文认为，保护领域中包容性、监控与本体权力的新组合，产生了一种特定的保护治理模式，我称之为“绿色监控”（green surveillance）。绿色监控指为追求保护安全，以开放式方式运用军事和刑事情报手段，收集关于环境犯罪的全面信息，并以地方社区为对象。基于民族志田野调查，本文考察了纳米比亚西北部绿色监控的具体样貌，当地居民正被“动员”参与保护执法。通过展示当地居民如何经历绿色监控，本文回应了学界关于调查军事化保护中以社区为重点的监控行动之社会影响的呼吁。分析表明，绿色监控可能对农村社区产生严重的负面社会影响，如对地方的工具化、对不稳定生计的剥削、身体暴力与慢暴力、社会排斥、信任与社会凝聚力的瓦解以及虚假举报，这些都加剧了社会冲突。
